@ENABLED
class ChatVoiceTests(ChatTestCase):
    """Voice messages are transcribed, then answered like typed ones."""

    def post_voice(self, audio):
        data = {"page": "/"}
        if audio is not None:
            data["audio"] = audio
        return self.client.post(reverse("chatbot:voice"), data)

    def test_transcribes_and_answers(self):
        with mock.patch(TRANSCRIBE, return_value=("Â¿QuÃ© hay hoy?", "es-ES")):
            with mock.patch(GENERATE, return_value=text_response("Hoy hay yoga.")):
                response = self.post_voice(wav())
        self.assertEqual(
            response.json(),
            {
                "reply": "Hoy hay yoga.",
                "declined": False,
                "unanswered": False,
                "transcript": "Â¿QuÃ© hay hoy?",
                "language": "es-ES",
            },
        )

    def test_rejects_bad_uploads(self):
        too_big = SimpleUploadedFile(
            "a.wav", b"0" * (4 * 1024 * 1024 + 1), content_type="audio/wav"
        )
        text = SimpleUploadedFile("a.txt", b"hi", content_type="text/plain")
        statuses = [self.post_voice(a).status_code for a in (None, too_big, text)]
        self.assertEqual(statuses, [400, 400, 400])

    def test_unheard_audio(self):
        with mock.patch(TRANSCRIBE, return_value=("", "")):
            self.assertEqual(self.post_voice(wav()).status_code, 422)

    def test_transcription_failure(self):
        with mock.patch(TRANSCRIBE, side_effect=gemini.GeminiError("x")):
            self.assertEqual(self.post_voice(wav()).status_code, 503)

    def test_answer_failure(self):
        with mock.patch(TRANSCRIBE, return_value=("Hello", "en-US")):
            with mock.patch(GENERATE, side_effect=gemini.GeminiError("x")):
                self.assertEqual(self.post_voice(wav()).status_code, 503)


@ENABLED
class HistoryAndResetTests(ChatTestCase):
    """History restores the chat and delivers managers' answers once."""

    def test_answers_are_delivered_into_the_chat_once(self):
        anonymous = create_inquiry(None, "Dogs allowed?")
        session = self.client.session
        session[assistant.INQUIRIES_KEY] = [anonymous.pk]
        session.save()

        data = self.client.get(reverse("chatbot:history")).json()
        self.assertEqual(data, {"messages": [], "updates": 0, "pending": True})

        answer_inquiry(anonymous, "Yes, on a leash.", make_user("staff"))
        data = self.client.get(reverse("chatbot:history")).json()
        self.assertEqual(data["updates"], 1)
        self.assertFalse(data["pending"])
        self.assertIn("Yes, on a leash.", data["messages"][0]["text"])

        data = self.client.get(reverse("chatbot:history")).json()
        self.assertEqual(data["updates"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(len(data["messages"]), 1)

    def test_signed_in_users_get_answers_from_any_session(self):
        user = make_user("asker")
        inquiry = create_inquiry(user, "Parking?")
        answer_inquiry(inquiry, "Street parking only.", make_user("staff"))
        self.client.force_login(user)
        data = self.client.get(reverse("chatbot:history")).json()
        self.assertEqual(data["updates"], 1)

    def test_reset_clears_the_conversation(self):
        session = self.client.session
        session[assistant.HISTORY_KEY] = [{"role": "user", "text": "Hi"}]
        session.save()
        response = self.client.post(reverse("chatbot:reset"))
        self.assertEqual(response.json(), {"messages": []})
        self.assertEqual(self.client.session[assistant.HISTORY_KEY], [])


class InquiryPageTests(TestCase):
    """Hosts answer questions about their events; staff answer all."""

    @classmethod
    def setUpTestData(cls):
        cls.host = make_user("host")
        cls.staff = make_user("staff", is_staff=True)
        cls.asker = make_user("asker")
        cls.event = make_event("host-1", "Kite Day", host=cls.host)
        cls.hosted = create_inquiry(
            cls.asker,
            "Â¿Hay baÃ±os?",
            question_english="Are there restrooms?",
            language="Spanish",
            event=cls.event,
        )
        cls.general = create_inquiry(None, "Is the site free?")

    def test_login_required(self):
        response = self.client.get(reverse("chatbot:inquiries"))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 302)

    def test_hosts_see_questions_about_their_events(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        response = self.client.get(reverse("chatbot:inquiries"))
        self.assertContains(response, "Are there restrooms?")
        self.assertContains(response, "Asked in Spanish")
        self.assertNotContains(response, "Is the site free?")

    def test_staff_see_everything(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.staff)
        response = self.client.get(reverse("chatbot:inquiries"))
        self.assertContains(response, "Are there restrooms?")
        self.assertContains(response, "Is the site free?")

    def test_answering_translates_and_notifies_the_asker(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        url = reverse("chatbot:answer_inquiry", args=[self.hosted.pk])
        mail.outbox.clear()
        with mock.patch(
            "chatbot.gemini.translate", return_value="SÃ­, junto al lago."
        ) as translate:
            response = self.client.post(url, {"answer": "Yes, by the lake."})
        self.assertRedirects(response, reverse("chatbot:inquiries"))
        translate.assert_called_once_with("Yes, by the lake.", "Spanish")
        self.hosted.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(self.hosted.status, Inquiry.Status.ANSWERED)
        self.assertEqual(self.hosted.answer_for_asker, "SÃ­, junto al lago.")
        notification = Notification.objects.get(recipient=self.asker)
        self.assertIn("SÃ­, junto al lago.", notification.body)
        self.assertEqual(mail.outbox[0].to, ["asker@example.com"])

        response = self.client.post(url, {"answer": "Again"}, follow=True)
        self.assertContains(response, "already answered")
        response = self.client.get(reverse("chatbot:inquiries"))
        self.assertContains(response, "Answer from host")

    def test_empty_answers_are_rejected(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        url = reverse("chatbot:answer_inquiry", args=[self.hosted.pk])
        response = self.client.post(url, {"answer": "  "}, follow=True)
        self.assertContains(response, "Write an answer")

    def test_other_users_cannot_answer(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.asker)
        url = reverse("chatbot:answer_inquiry", args=[self.hosted.pk])
        self.assertEqual(self.client.post(url, {"answer": "Hi"}).status_code, 404)

    def test_translation_failures_still_send_the_answer(self):
        with mock.patch("chatbot.gemini.translate", side_effect=gemini.GeminiError):
            self.assertTrue(answer_inquiry(self.hosted, "Yes.", self.host))
        self.hosted.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(self.hosted.answer_for_asker, "Yes.")


class InquiryAdminTests(TestCase):
    """Answering in the admin sends the answer like the Questions page."""

    def setUp(self):
        self.admin = InquiryAdmin(Inquiry, AdminSite())
        self.staff = make_user("admin", is_staff=True)
        self.request = RequestFactory().post("/")
        self.request.user = self.staff

    def test_no_adding_and_answer_on_save(self):
        self.assertFalse(self.admin.has_add_permission(self.request))
        inquiry = create_inquiry(None, "Open late?")
        self.assertEqual(str(inquiry), "Open late?")
        inquiry.answer = " Until 9 PM. "
        self.admin.save_model(self.request, inquiry, None, True)
        inquiry.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(inquiry.status, Inquiry.Status.ANSWERED)
        self.assertEqual(inquiry.answer, "Until 9 PM.")
        self.assertEqual(inquiry.answered_by, self.staff)

        inquiry.answer = "Until 10 PM."
        self.admin.save_model(self.request, inquiry, None, True)
        inquiry.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(inquiry.answer, "Until 10 PM.")


class EventOnPageTests(TestCase):
    """Only event detail pages give the assistant page context."""

    def test_resolves_event_pages_only(self):
        event = make_event("e1", "Swim")
        self.assertEqual(assistant.event_on_page(f"/events/{event.pk}/?x=1"), event)
        self.assertIsNone(assistant.event_on_page("/search"))
        self.assertIsNone(assistant.event_on_page("/nowhere/"))
        self.assertIsNone(assistant.event_on_page(None))


@ENABLED
class ChatApiEndpointTests(ChatTestCase):
    """The user story's /api/chat contract: success, API failure, empty input."""

    URL = "/api/chat"

    def post(self, body):
        return self.client.post(self.URL, body, content_type="application/json")

    def test_url(self):
        self.assertEqual(reverse("chatbot:message"), self.URL)

    def test_success(self):
        with mock.patch(GENERATE, return_value=text_response("Hello!")):
            response = self.post({"message": "Hi"})
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(response.json()["reply"], "Hello!")

    def test_api_failure_returns_a_friendly_error(self):
        with mock.patch(GENERATE, side_effect=gemini.GeminiError("down")):
            response = self.post({"message": "Hi"})
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 503)
        self.assertEqual(response.json(), {"error": views.UNAVAILABLE})

    def test_gemini_rate_limit_returns_a_friendly_error(self):
        error = gemini.GeminiError("quota", rate_limited=True)
        with mock.patch(GENERATE, side_effect=error):
            response = self.post({"message": "Hi"})
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 503)
        self.assertEqual(response.json(), {"error": views.BUSY})
        with mock.patch(TRANSCRIBE, side_effect=error):
            response = self.client.post(
                reverse("chatbot:voice"), {"audio": wav(), "page": "/"}
            )
        self.assertEqual(response.json(), {"error": views.BUSY})

    def test_empty_input(self):
        for body in ({"message": ""}, {"message": "   "}, {}):
            response = self.post(body)
            self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 400)
            self.assertEqual(response.json(), {"error": "Type a message first."})

    def test_api_key_never_reaches_the_page(self):
        response = self.client.get(reverse("login"))
        self.assertContains(response, 'data-message-url="/api/chat"')
        self.assertNotContains(response, "test-key")


@ENABLED
class VoiceCallTests(ChatTestCase):
    """Voice calls get a locked token, run tools here, and save each turn."""

    def post_json(self, name, body):
        return self.client.post(
            reverse(name), json.dumps(body), content_type="application/json"
        )

    def test_session_returns_a_token_for_the_voice_setup(self):
        make_event("live-1", "Jazz Night")
        self.post_json("chatbot:live_turn", {"message": "Hi", "reply": "Hello!"})
        with mock.patch(
            "chatbot.gemini.create_live_token", return_value="auth_tokens/t"
        ) as create:
            response = self.post_json("chatbot:live", {"page": "/"})
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(response.json()["token"], "auth_tokens/t")
        self.assertTrue(response.json()["url"].startswith("wss://"))
        setup = create.call_args.args[0]
        voice = setup["generationConfig"]["speechConfig"]
        self.assertEqual(voice["languageCode"], "en-US")
        self.assertEqual(
            voice["voiceConfig"]["prebuiltVoiceConfig"]["voiceName"], "Achernar"
        )
        self.assertIn("inputAudioTranscription", setup)
        self.assertIn("outputAudioTranscription", setup)
        prompt = setup["systemInstruction"]["parts"][0]["text"]
        self.assertIn("LIVE VOICE CALL", prompt)
        self.assertIn("Visitor: Hi", prompt)
        self.assertNotContains(response, "test-key")

    def test_session_failure_is_friendly(self):
        with mock.patch(
            "chatbot.gemini.create_live_token", side_effect=gemini.GeminiError("x")
        ):
            response = self.post_json("chatbot:live", {})
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 503)
        self.assertEqual(response.json(), {"error": views.UNAVAILABLE})

    def test_tools_run_for_the_visitor(self):
        make_event("live-2", "Sunrise Yoga")
        response = self.post_json(
            "chatbot:live_tool", {"name": "search_events", "args": {"query": "yoga"}}
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertIn("Sunrise Yoga", json.dumps(response.json()["result"]))

    def test_calls_cannot_forward_questions(self):
        response = self.post_json(
            "chatbot:live_tool",
            {"name": "escalate_to_event_manager", "args": {"question": "Dogs?"}},
        )
        self.assertIn("error", response.json()["result"])
        self.assertFalse(Inquiry.objects.exists())

    def test_bad_tool_requests(self):
        self.assertEqual(
            self.client.post(
                reverse("chatbot:live_tool"), "nope", content_type="application/json"
            ).status_code,
            400,
        )
        response = self.post_json("chatbot:live_tool", {"name": "drop_tables"})
        self.assertIn("error", response.json()["result"])

    def test_turns_are_saved_to_history_and_log(self):
        response = self.post_json(
            "chatbot:live_turn",
            {"message": "Any jazz?", "reply": "Yes, Friday!", "page": "/"},
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        history = self.client.get(reverse("chatbot:history")).json()["messages"]
        self.assertEqual(
            history,
            [
                {"role": "user", "text": "Any jazz?"},
                {"role": "model", "text": "Yes, Friday!"},
            ],
        )
        log = ChatLog.objects.get()
        self.assertTrue(log.via_voice)
        self.assertEqual(log.outcome, ChatLog.Outcome.ANSWERED)

    def test_greeting_turns_have_no_visitor_message(self):
        self.post_json("chatbot:live_turn", {"reply": "Hi! How can I help?"})
        history = self.client.get(reverse("chatbot:history")).json()["messages"]
        self.assertEqual(history, [{"role": "model", "text": "Hi! How can I help?"}])

    def test_empty_turns_are_rejected(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.post_json("chatbot:live_turn", {}).status_code, 400)

    def test_call_traffic_has_its_own_rate_limit(self):
        for _ in range(views.RATE_LIMIT + 1):
            self.post_json("chatbot:live_turn", {"reply": "ok"})
        # Typed chat still works after a busy call.
        with mock.patch(GENERATE, return_value=text_response("Hello!")):
            self.assertEqual(self.post_message("Hi").status_code, 200)
        for _ in range(views.VOICE_CALL_RATE_LIMIT):
            self.post_json("chatbot:live_turn", {"reply": "ok"})
        response = self.post_json("chatbot:live_turn", {"reply": "ok"})
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 429)

    def test_widget_links_the_call_endpoints(self):
        response = self.client.get(reverse("login"))
        self.assertContains(response, 'data-live-url="/api/chat/live"')


@ENABLED
class SafetyAndFallbackTests(ChatTestCase):
    """Unsafe requests are declined; unknown answers say where to ask."""

    def test_safety_blocked_replies_are_declined(self):
        blocked = {"candidates": [{"finishReason": "SAFETY", "content": {}}]}
        with mock.patch(GENERATE, return_value=blocked):
            response = self.post_message("Something inappropriate")
        self.assertEqual(response.json()["reply"], assistant.BLOCKED_REPLY)
        self.assertTrue(response.json()["declined"])
        self.assertFalse(Inquiry.objects.exists())
        self.assertEqual(ChatLog.objects.get().outcome, ChatLog.Outcome.DECLINED)

    @override_settings(CHATBOT_SUPPORT_EMAIL="help@example.com")
    def test_fallback_offers_the_admin_email(self):
        with mock.patch(GENERATE, return_value=text_response("")) as generate:
            reply = self.post_message("Is there a coat check?").json()["reply"]
        self.assertEqual(
            reply,
            "Sorry, I'm not sure about that. Please contact the site admin at "
            "help@example.com.",
        )
        prompt = generate.call_args.args[0]["systemInstruction"]["parts"][0]["text"]
        self.assertIn("Admin email: help@example.com", prompt)
        page = self.client.get(reverse("login"))
        self.assertContains(page, "email the site admin at")
        self.assertContains(page, "mailto:help@example.com")

    def test_prompt_never_promises_a_person_will_follow_up(self):
        with mock.patch(GENERATE, return_value=text_response("Hi")) as generate:
            self.post_message("Hi")
        prompt = generate.call_args.args[0]["systemInstruction"]["parts"][0]["text"]
        self.assertIn("comments", prompt)
        self.assertIn("contact the site admin", prompt)
        self.assertNotIn("escalate_to_event_manager", prompt)
        tools = generate.call_args.args[0]["tools"][0]["functionDeclarations"]
        self.assertNotIn("escalate_to_event_manager", [t["name"] for t in tools])

    def test_prompt_includes_active_faqs_only(self):
        FAQ.objects.create(question="Hidden?", answer="Nope.", is_active=False)
        with mock.patch(GENERATE, return_value=text_response("Hi")) as generate:
            self.post_message("Can I get a refund?")
        prompt = generate.call_args.args[0]["systemInstruction"]["parts"][0]["text"]
        self.assertIn("Can I get a refund?", prompt)
        self.assertIn("[Cancellations & refunds]", prompt)
        self.assertNotIn("Hidden?", prompt)
        self.assertIn("Admin email: none", prompt)

    def test_safety_settings_are_sent(self):
        with mock.patch("chatbot.gemini.urlopen") as urlopen:
            urlopen.side_effect = OSError("offline")
            self.post_message("Hi")
        sent = json.loads(urlopen.call_args.args[0].data)
        self.assertEqual(
            {s["category"] for s in sent["safetySettings"]},
            {
                "HARM_CATEGORY_HARASSMENT",
                "HARM_CATEGORY_HATE_SPEECH",
                "HARM_CATEGORY_SEXUALLY_EXPLICIT",
                "HARM_CATEGORY_DANGEROUS_CONTENT",
            },
        )


@ENABLED
class ChatLogTests(ChatTestCase):
    """Conversations are logged anonymously, with personal details masked."""

    def test_logs_mask_emails_and_long_numbers(self):
        self.client.force_login(make_user("logger"))
        message = "Email me at jo@example.com or call (212) 555-0199 about 2026-11-07"
        with mock.patch(GENERATE, return_value=text_response("Sure!")):
            self.post_message(message, page="/search")
        log = ChatLog.objects.get()
        self.assertEqual(
            log.message, "Email me at [email] or call [number] about 2026-11-07"
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            (log.reply, log.outcome, log.page), ("Sure!", "answered", "/search")
        )
        self.assertTrue(log.signed_in)
        self.assertFalse(log.via_voice)
        self.assertEqual(str(log), log.message)
        self.assertNotIn("logger", str(vars(log)))

    def test_unanswered_errors_and_voice_are_logged(self):
        with mock.patch(GENERATE, return_value=text_response("")):
            self.post_message("Unknown?")
        with mock.patch(GENERATE, side_effect=gemini.GeminiError("x")):
            self.post_message("Down?")
        with mock.patch(TRANSCRIBE, return_value=("Hola", "es")):
            with mock.patch(GENERATE, return_value=text_response("Â¡Hola!")):
                self.client.post(reverse("chatbot:voice"), {"audio": wav()})
        outcomes = list(
            ChatLog.objects.order_by("pk").values_list("outcome", "via_voice")
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            outcomes, [("unanswered", False), ("error", False), ("answered", True)]
        )

    @override_settings(CHATBOT_LOG_CONVERSATIONS=False)
    def test_logging_can_be_turned_off(self):
        with mock.patch(GENERATE, return_value=text_response("Hi")):
            self.post_message("Hello")
        self.assertFalse(ChatLog.objects.exists())

    def test_logs_are_read_only_in_the_admin(self):
        admin = ChatLogAdmin(ChatLog, AdminSite())
        request = RequestFactory().get("/")
        self.assertFalse(admin.has_add_permission(request))
        self.assertFalse(admin.has_change_permission(request))
        self.assertEqual(str(FAQ(question="Q?")), "Q?")

"""URL configuration for config project.

The `urlpatterns` list routes URLs to views. For more information please see:
    https://docs.djangoproject.com/en/5.2/topics/http/urls/
Examples:
Function views
    1. Add an import:  from my_app import views
    2. Add a URL to urlpatterns:  path('', views.home, name='home')
Class-based views
    1. Add an import:  from other_app.views import Home
    2. Add a URL to urlpatterns:  path('', Home.as_view(), name='home')
Including another URLconf
    1. Import the include() function: from django.urls import include, path
    2. Add a URL to urlpatterns:  path('blog/', include('blog.urls'))
"""

from django.contrib import admin
from django.urls import include, path

"""Admin configuration for imported and hosted events."""

from django.contrib import admin

from .models import Event, EventPost, EventReply, Notification


@admin.register(Notification)
class NotificationAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    """List sent notifications so staff can check who was told what."""

    list_display = (
        "subject",
        "recipient",
        "event",
        "contact_request",
        "created_at",
        "read_at",
    )
    list_select_related = (
        "recipient",
        "event",
        "contact_request__sender",
        "contact_request__recipient",
    )


@admin.register(Event)
class EventAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    """Show events in a readable, searchable list, with hosts if any."""

    list_display = ("title", "start_time", "end_time", "status", "host")
    list_filter = ("status", "borough")
    list_select_related = ("host",)
    search_fields = ("title", "location", "park_names")
    ordering = ("start_time",)


@admin.register(EventPost)
class EventPostAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    """Manage public discussion comments flagged in the moderation queue."""

    list_display = ("__str__", "event", "author", "created_at")
    list_select_related = ("event", "author")
    search_fields = ("body", "event__title", "author__username")


@admin.register(EventReply)
class EventReplyAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    """Manage replies independently of their parent comments."""

    list_display = ("__str__", "post", "author", "created_at")
    list_select_related = ("post", "author")
    search_fields = ("body", "post__event__title", "author__username")

FILE: events\community_views.py

"""Views for event interests, discussions, and contact requests."""

from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.db import transaction
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404, redirect
from django.urls import reverse
from django.utils import timezone
from django.views.decorators.http import require_POST

from accounts.models import User

from .contact_services import create_contact_request
from .forms import ContactRequestForm, EventPostForm, EventReplyForm
from .models import (
    ContactRequest,
    Conversation,
    Event,
    EventInterest,
    EventPost,
    Notification,
)
from .notifications import notify_contact_request


def event_detail_url(event_id):
    """Return the URL for an event detail page."""
    return reverse("event_detail", args=[event_id])


def get_open_event(event_id):
    """Return an event that still accepts interest and contact interactions."""
    event = get_object_or_404(Event, pk=event_id)

    if event.status != Event.Status.UPCOMING:
        return None

    return event


@login_required
@require_POST
def toggle_event_interest(request, event_id):
    """Set, switch, or remove the current user's attendance intention."""
    event = get_open_event(event_id)

    if event is None:
        return redirect(event_detail_url(event_id))

    # Keep compatibility with requests that do not specify a status.
    status = request.POST.get(
        "status",
        EventInterest.Status.INTERESTED,
    )

    valid_statuses = {
        EventInterest.Status.INTERESTED,
        EventInterest.Status.WANT_TO_ATTEND,
    }

    if status not in valid_statuses:
        return redirect(event_detail_url(event_id))

    with transaction.atomic():
        interest = EventInterest.objects.filter(
            event=event,
            user=request.user,
        ).first()

        if interest is None:
            EventInterest.objects.create(
                event=event,
                user=request.user,
                status=status,
            )
        elif interest.status == status:
            interest.delete()
        else:
            interest.status = status
            interest.save(update_fields=["status"])

    return redirect(event_detail_url(event_id))


@login_required
@require_POST
def create_event_post(request, event_id):
    """Create a discussion post only when the event is upcoming."""
    event = get_object_or_404(Event, pk=event_id)

    if event.status != Event.Status.UPCOMING:
        return redirect(event_detail_url(event_id))

    form = EventPostForm(request.POST)

    if form.is_valid():
        post = form.save(commit=False)
        post.event = event
        post.author = request.user
        post.save()

    return redirect(event_detail_url(event_id))


@login_required
@require_POST
def create_event_reply(request, post_id):
    """Reply to a post only when its event is upcoming."""
    post = get_object_or_404(
        EventPost.objects.select_related("event", "author"),
        pk=post_id,
    )

    if post.event.status != Event.Status.UPCOMING:
        return redirect(event_detail_url(post.event_id))

    form = EventReplyForm(request.POST)

    if form.is_valid():
        with transaction.atomic():
            reply = form.save(commit=False)
            reply.post = post
            reply.author = request.user
            reply.save()

            # Notify the original post author, but never notify yourself.
            if post.author_id != request.user.pk:
                subject = f"New reply to your comment on {post.event.title}"
                Notification.objects.create(
                    recipient=post.author,
                    event=post.event,
                    subject=subject[
                        : Notification._meta.get_field("subject").max_length
                    ],
                    body=(
                        f"{request.user.get_display_name()} replied to "
                        f"your comment on '{post.event.title}'."
                    ),
                )

    return redirect(event_detail_url(post.event_id))


@login_required
@require_POST
def send_contact_request(request, event_id, user_id):
    """Send a contact request to a visible attendee of the same event."""
    event = get_open_event(event_id)

    if event is None:
        return redirect(event_detail_url(event_id))

    recipient = get_object_or_404(
        User,
        pk=user_id,
        share_profile_with_attendees=True,
        event_interests__event=event,
    )

    if recipient.pk == request.user.pk:
        return redirect(event_detail_url(event_id))

    if not EventInterest.objects.filter(
        event=event,
        user=request.user,
    ).exists():
        return redirect(event_detail_url(event_id))

    form = ContactRequestForm(request.POST)

    if not form.is_valid():
        return redirect(event_detail_url(event_id))

    create_contact_request(
        event,
        request.user,
        recipient,
        form.cleaned_data["message"],
        notification_sender=notify_contact_request,
    )

    return redirect(event_detail_url(event_id))


@login_required
@require_POST
def respond_to_contact_request(request, request_id, action):
    """Accept or decline a pending contact request addressed to the user."""
    with transaction.atomic():
        contact_request = get_object_or_404(
            ContactRequest.objects.select_for_update(),
            pk=request_id,
            recipient=request.user,
            status=ContactRequest.Status.PENDING,
        )
        destination = (
            "notifications"
            if request.POST.get("return_to") == "notifications"
            else event_detail_url(contact_request.event_id)
        )

        if action == "accept":
            contact_request.status = ContactRequest.Status.ACCEPTED
        elif action == "decline":
            contact_request.status = ContactRequest.Status.DECLINED
        else:
            return redirect(destination)

        contact_request.save(update_fields=["status", "updated_at"])
        if action == "accept":
            Conversation.get_or_create_for_users(
                contact_request.sender,
                contact_request.recipient,
            )
        Notification.objects.filter(
            contact_request=contact_request,
            recipient=request.user,
            read_at__isnull=True,
        ).update(read_at=timezone.now())

    return redirect(destination)

FILE: events\contact_services.py
============================================================
"""Shared contact-request workflows."""

from django.db import transaction

from accounts.models import ContactPermission, User

from .models import ContactRequest, Conversation, Event, EventInterest
from .notifications import notify_contact_request


def create_contact_request(
    event,
    sender,
    recipient,
    message,
    notification_sender=notify_contact_request,
):
    """Create one eligible event contact request, or return an existing one."""
    if (
        event.status != Event.Status.UPCOMING
        or sender.pk == recipient.pk
        or not recipient.share_profile_with_attendees
        or not EventInterest.objects.filter(event=event, user=sender).exists()
        or not EventInterest.objects.filter(event=event, user=recipient).exists()
    ):
        return None, False

    with transaction.atomic():
        User.objects.select_for_update(no_key=True).get(pk=sender.pk)
        existing = (
            ContactRequest.objects.filter(
                event=event,
                sender=sender,
                recipient=recipient,
            )
            .exclude(status=ContactRequest.Status.DECLINED)
            .first()
        )
        if existing:
            return existing, False

        contact_request = ContactRequest.objects.create(
            event=event,
            sender=sender,
            recipient=recipient,
            message=message,
            status=(
                ContactRequest.Status.ACCEPTED
                if recipient.contact_permission == ContactPermission.OPEN
                else ContactRequest.Status.PENDING
            ),
        )
        notification_sender(contact_request)
        if contact_request.status == ContactRequest.Status.ACCEPTED:
            Conversation.get_or_create_for_users(sender, recipient)

    return contact_request, True

FILE: events\context_processors.py
============================================================
"""Template context shared by every page."""

from django.db.models import Q

from .models import DirectMessage


def notifications(request):
    """Expose unread notification and direct-message counts.

    The counts are callables, so their queries only run on pages that show them.
    """
    if not request.user.is_authenticated:
        return {}
    unread = request.user.notifications.filter(read_at__isnull=True)
    unread_messages = DirectMessage.objects.filter(
        Q(conversation__user_one=request.user) | Q(conversation__user_two=request.user),
        read_at__isnull=True,
    ).exclude(sender=request.user)
    return {
        "unread_notification_count": unread.count,
        "unread_message_count": unread_messages.count,
    }



============================================================
FILE: events\conversation_services.py
============================================================
"""Shared access checks for private conversations and conversation reports."""

from django.db.models import Q
from django.http import Http404
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404

from .models import ContactRequest, Conversation


def has_accepted_contact(first_user, second_user):
    """Return whether either direction has an accepted contact request."""
    return (
        ContactRequest.objects.filter(status=ContactRequest.Status.ACCEPTED)
        .filter(
            Q(sender=first_user, recipient=second_user)
            | Q(sender=second_user, recipient=first_user)
        )
        .exists()
    )


def get_accessible_conversation(user, conversation_id):
    """Return an approved participant's conversation, otherwise respond 404."""
    thread = get_object_or_404(
        Conversation.objects.select_related("user_one", "user_two").filter(
            Q(user_one=user) | Q(user_two=user)
        ),
        pk=conversation_id,
    )
    other_user = thread.other_participant(user)
    if not has_accepted_contact(user, other_user):
        raise Http404("Conversation not found.")
    return thread

"""Filters that narrow event listings by date range, borough, and category."""

from datetime import datetime, time, timedelta
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

from django import forms
from django.db.models import Q
from django.utils import timezone

NYC_TIMEZONE = ZoneInfo("America/New_York")

DATE_PRESETS = (
    ("today", "Today"),
    ("weekend", "This weekend"),
    ("month", "This month"),
)

# NYC Parks property IDs start with a borough letter, e.g. "B073" is in Brooklyn.
BOROUGHS = (
    ("manhattan", "Manhattan", "M"),
    ("brooklyn", "Brooklyn", "B"),
    ("queens", "Queens", "Q"),
    ("bronx", "The Bronx", "X"),
    ("staten-island", "Staten Island", "R"),
)
BOROUGH_PREFIXES = {slug: prefix for slug, _, prefix in BOROUGHS}

# Friendly groups of the feed's own category names, which must match exactly.
CATEGORY_GROUPS = (
    (
        "fitness",
        "Fitness",
        (
            "Fitness",
            "Exercise Classes",
            "Outdoor Fitness",
            "Shape Up NYC",
            "Running/Jogging",
            "Strength Training/Weightlifting",
            "Walking",
        ),
    ),
    ("yoga", "Yoga & Pilates", ("Yoga & Pilates Classes",)),
    (
        "sports",
        "Sports",
        (
            "Sports",
            "Basketball/Netball",
            "Baseball/Softball",
            "Football",
            "Social Sports",
            "Sports Camps",
            "Swimming/Aquatics",
            "Track & Field",
            "Adventure Courses/Rock Climbing",
        ),
    ),
    (
        "art",
        "Arts & Crafts",
        ("Art", "Arts & Crafts", "Arts, Culture & Fun Series", "Theater"),
    ),
    ("dance", "Dance", ("Dance", "Dance Classes")),
    (
        "music-film",
        "Music & Film",
        (
            "Concerts",
            "Free Summer Concerts",
            "Film",
            "Movies Under the Stars",
            "Free Summer Movies",
        ),
    ),
    (
        "nature",
        "Nature & Outdoors",
        (
            "Nature",
            "Gardening",
            "Wildlife",
            "Birding",
            "Hiking",
            "Waterfront",
            "Urban Park Rangers",
            "Astronomy",
        ),
    ),
    (
        "kids",
        "Kids & Family",
        ("Best for Kids", "Family Camping with the Urban Park Rangers"),
    ),
    ("history", "History & Tours", ("History", "Tours", "Open House New York")),
    (
        "education",
        "Education & Talks",
        ("Education", "STEM Classes", "Talks", "Workshops"),
    ),
    ("games", "Games", ("Games",)),
    (
        "festivals",
        "Festivals & Food",
        ("Festivals", "Fall Festivals", "Halloween", "Food", "Markets"),
    ),
    ("volunteer", "Volunteering", ("Volunteer", "It's My Park")),
    ("seniors", "Seniors & Accessible", ("Seniors", "Accessible Activities")),
)
# Hosted events store the group labels themselves, so labels match as well.
CATEGORY_NAMES = {
    slug: tuple(dict.fromkeys((label, *names)))
    for slug, label, names in CATEGORY_GROUPS
}

# Categories are stored as one string, e.g. "Art | Games".
CATEGORY_SEPARATOR = " | "


def nyc_today():
    """Return today's date in New York, where every event takes place."""
    return timezone.localtime(timezone.now(), NYC_TIMEZONE).date()


def preset_range(preset, today):
    """Return the inclusive ``(start, end)`` dates a quick date button stands for.

    "This weekend" is the coming Saturday and Sunday, or only the remaining
    days once the weekend has started; "This month" runs from today to the
    last day of the month.
    """
    if preset == "today":
        return today, today
    if preset == "weekend":
        if today.weekday() == 6:
            return today, today
        saturday = today + timedelta(days=5 - today.weekday())
        return saturday, saturday + timedelta(days=1)
    next_month = (today.replace(day=28) + timedelta(days=4)).replace(day=1)
    return today, next_month - timedelta(days=1)


def start_of_day(day):
    """Return midnight in New York at the start of ``day``."""
    return datetime.combine(day, time.min, tzinfo=NYC_TIMEZONE)


def category_match(name):
    """Match events whose category list contains exactly ``name``."""
    separator = CATEGORY_SEPARATOR
    return (
        Q(categories=name)
        | Q(categories__startswith=name + separator)
        | Q(categories__endswith=separator + name)
        | Q(categories__contains=separator + name + separator)
    )


class EventFilterForm(forms.Form):
    """Validate the filter bar shown under the event search box.

    A quick date button submits ``when``, which replaces any typed dates so
    the dates on screen always describe the results.
    """

    when = forms.ChoiceField(choices=DATE_PRESETS, required=False)
    date_from = forms.DateField(
        label="From",
        required=False,
        widget=forms.DateInput(attrs={"type": "date"}, format="%Y-%m-%d"),
    )
    date_to = forms.DateField(
        label="To",
        required=False,
        widget=forms.DateInput(attrs={"type": "date"}, format="%Y-%m-%d"),
    )
    borough = forms.MultipleChoiceField(
        choices=[(slug, label) for slug, label, _ in BOROUGHS],
        required=False,
        widget=forms.CheckboxSelectMultiple,
    )
    category = forms.MultipleChoiceField(
        choices=[(slug, label) for slug, label, _ in CATEGORY_GROUPS],
        required=False,
        widget=forms.CheckboxSelectMultiple,
    )

    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
        """Prefix element IDs so they never clash with other forms on a page."""
        kwargs.setdefault("auto_id", "filter-%s")
        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)

    def clean(self):
        """Expand quick date buttons and drop date ranges that end too early."""
        cleaned = super().clean()
        if cleaned.get("when"):
            cleaned["date_from"], cleaned["date_to"] = preset_range(
                cleaned["when"], nyc_today()
            )
        start, end = cleaned.get("date_from"), cleaned.get("date_to")
        if start and end and start > end:
            self.add_error("date_to", "The end date must be on or after the start.")
            cleaned.pop("date_from")
        return cleaned


def filter_params(filters):
    """Return the active filters as ordered query-string pairs.

    Quick date buttons are expanded to their dates, so the pairs can rebuild
    the same results on another page or populate the form again.
    """
    params = []
    for key in ("date_from", "date_to"):
        if filters.get(key):
            params.append((key, filters[key].isoformat()))
    params.extend(("borough", slug) for slug in filters.get("borough", []))
    params.extend(("category", slug) for slug in filters.get("category", []))
    return params


def filter_events(events, filters):
    """Narrow an event queryset to events matching every active filter.

    Events count as being on a date range when they overlap it. Selecting
    several boroughs or categories matches any of them.
    """
    start, end = filters.get("date_from"), filters.get("date_to")
    if start:
        events = events.filter(
            Q(end_time__gte=start_of_day(start))
            | Q(end_time__isnull=True, start_time__gte=start_of_day(start))
        )
    if end:
        events = events.filter(start_time__lt=start_of_day(end + timedelta(days=1)))
    boroughs = Q()
    for slug in filters.get("borough", []):
        boroughs |= Q(borough=slug) | Q(park_ids__istartswith=BOROUGH_PREFIXES[slug])
    categories = Q()
    for slug in filters.get("category", []):
        for name in CATEGORY_NAMES[slug]:
            categories |= category_match(name)
    return events.filter(boroughs, categories)

============================================================
FILE: events\management\commands\sync_events.py
============================================================
"""Management command that runs the hourly events sync from cron."""

from django.core.management.base import BaseCommand, CommandError

from events.services import SyncError, sync_events


class Command(BaseCommand):
    """Fetch the NYC Parks events feed and sync it into the database."""

    help = (
        "Fetch NYC Parks events from the open data feed, upsert changes, "
        "and mark events done once their end time has passed."
    )

    def handle(self, *args, **options):
        """Run one sync and print a summary, or fail loudly for cron."""
        try:
            counts = sync_events()
        except SyncError as error:
            raise CommandError(str(error)) from error
        self.stdout.write(
            self.style.SUCCESS(
                "Event sync complete: {fetched} fetched, {created} created, "
                "{updated} updated, {unchanged} unchanged, "
                "{marked_done} marked done.".format(**counts)
            )
        )



============================================================
FILE: events\messaging_views.py
============================================================
"""Views for private attendee conversations."""

from django.contrib import messages
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.contrib.auth.mixins import LoginRequiredMixin
from django.db import transaction
from django.db.models import Count, Prefetch, Q
from django.http import Http404, JsonResponse
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404, redirect, render
from django.urls import reverse
from django.utils import timezone
from django.views.decorators.http import require_GET
from django.views.generic import ListView

from accounts.models import User

from .contact_services import create_contact_request
from .conversation_services import get_accessible_conversation, has_accepted_contact
from .forms import ContactStartForm, DirectMessageForm
from .models import (
    ContactRequest,
    Conversation,
    DirectMessage,
    Event,
)


def _shared_events(first_user, second_user):
    """Return events both users marked as interested in."""
    return (
        Event.objects.filter(interests__user=first_user)
        .filter(interests__user=second_user)
        .distinct()
        .order_by("start_time", "title", "pk")
    )


def _shared_upcoming_events(first_user, second_user):
    """Return shared events that still accept new contact requests."""
    return _shared_events(first_user, second_user).filter(status=Event.Status.UPCOMING)


def _message_data(message):
    """Return the public fields needed to render one chat bubble."""
    return {
        "id": message.pk,
        "sender_id": message.sender_id,
        "sender_name": message.sender.get_display_name(),
        "body": message.body,
        "created_at": message.created_at.isoformat(),
    }


class InboxView(LoginRequiredMixin, ListView):
    """Show the signed-in user's conversations, newest activity first."""

    template_name = "events/inbox.html"
    context_object_name = "conversations"
    paginate_by = 20

    def get_queryset(self):
        user = self.request.user
        latest_messages = DirectMessage.objects.select_related("sender").order_by(
            "-created_at", "-pk"
        )
        return (
            Conversation.objects.filter(Q(user_one=user) | Q(user_two=user))
            .select_related("user_one", "user_two")
            .annotate(
                unread_count=Count(
                    "messages",
                    filter=Q(messages__read_at__isnull=True)
                    & ~Q(messages__sender=user),
                )
            )
            .prefetch_related(
                Prefetch(
                    "messages",
                    queryset=latest_messages,
                    to_attr="ordered_messages",
                )
            )
            .order_by("-updated_at", "-pk")
        )

    def get_context_data(self, **kwargs):
        context = super().get_context_data(**kwargs)
        for thread in context["conversations"]:
            thread.other_user = thread.other_participant(self.request.user)
        return context


@login_required
def message_user(request, user_id):
    """Open an approved conversation or start the contact-request flow."""
    recipient = get_object_or_404(User, pk=user_id)
    if recipient.pk == request.user.pk:
        raise Http404("Conversation not found.")

    if has_accepted_contact(request.user, recipient):
        conversation, _ = Conversation.get_or_create_for_users(request.user, recipient)
        return redirect("conversation", conversation_id=conversation.pk)
    if not recipient.share_profile_with_attendees:
        raise Http404("Profile not found.")

    shared_events = _shared_upcoming_events(request.user, recipient)
    pending_request = (
        ContactRequest.objects.filter(status=ContactRequest.Status.PENDING)
        .filter(
            Q(sender=request.user, recipient=recipient)
            | Q(sender=recipient, recipient=request.user)
        )
        .select_related("event")
        .first()
    )

    if request.method == "POST":
        form = ContactStartForm(
            request.POST,
            shared_events=shared_events,
        )
        if form.is_valid():
            event = form.cleaned_data["event"]
            contact_request, created = create_contact_request(
                event,
                request.user,
                recipient,
                form.cleaned_data["message"],
            )
            if contact_request is None:
                messages.error(
                    request,
                    "Choose an upcoming event that you both are interested in.",
                )
                return redirect("message_user", user_id=recipient.pk)
            if contact_request.status == ContactRequest.Status.ACCEPTED:
                conversation, _ = Conversation.get_or_create_for_users(
                    request.user, recipient
                )
                return redirect("conversation", conversation_id=conversation.pk)
            if not created:
                messages.info(
                    request,
                    "Your contact request is already awaiting a response.",
                )
                return redirect("message_user", user_id=recipient.pk)

            messages.success(
                request,
                "Your contact request was sent. You can message once it is accepted.",
            )
            return redirect("message_user", user_id=recipient.pk)
    else:
        form = ContactStartForm(shared_events=shared_events)

    return render(
        request,
        "events/message_user.html",
        {
            "recipient": recipient,
            "form": form,
            "pending_request": pending_request,
            "has_shared_events": shared_events.exists(),
        },
    )


@login_required
def conversation(request, conversation_id):
    """Display a conversation and accept replies from either participant."""
    thread = get_accessible_conversation(request.user, conversation_id)
    other_user = thread.other_participant(request.user)

    if request.method == "POST":
        form = DirectMessageForm(request.POST)
        if form.is_valid():
            with transaction.atomic():
                message = DirectMessage.objects.create(
                    conversation=thread,
                    sender=request.user,
                    body=form.cleaned_data["body"],
                )
                thread.save(update_fields=["updated_at"])
            if request.headers.get("x-requested-with") == "XMLHttpRequest":
                message.sender = request.user
                return JsonResponse({"message": _message_data(message)})
            return redirect("conversation", conversation_id=thread.pk)
        if request.headers.get("x-requested-with") == "XMLHttpRequest":
            return JsonResponse(
                {"errors": form.errors.get_json_data()},
                status=400,
            )
    else:
        form = DirectMessageForm()

    DirectMessage.objects.filter(
        conversation=thread,
        read_at__isnull=True,
    ).exclude(
        sender=request.user,
    ).update(read_at=timezone.now())

    thread_messages = thread.messages.select_related("sender", "event").order_by(
        "created_at", "pk"
    )
    latest_message = thread.messages.order_by("-pk").first()
    return render(
        request,
        "events/conversation.html",
        {
            "conversation": thread,
            "other_user": other_user,
            "thread_messages": thread_messages,
            "latest_message_id": latest_message.pk if latest_message else 0,
            "updates_url": reverse("conversation_updates", args=[thread.pk]),
            "form": form,
        },
    )


@login_required
@require_GET
def conversation_updates(request, conversation_id):
    """Return new messages for one of the signed-in user's conversations."""
    thread = get_accessible_conversation(request.user, conversation_id)

    try:
        after_id = int(request.GET.get("after", "0"))
        if after_id < 0:
            raise ValueError
    except ValueError:
        return JsonResponse(
            {"error": "The after cursor must be a non-negative integer."},
            status=400,
        )

    new_messages = list(
        thread.messages.filter(pk__gt=after_id)
        .select_related("sender")
        .order_by("pk")[:100]
    )
    incoming_ids = [
        message.pk for message in new_messages if message.sender_id != request.user.pk
    ]
    if incoming_ids:
        DirectMessage.objects.filter(
            pk__in=incoming_ids,
            read_at__isnull=True,
        ).update(read_at=timezone.now())

    response = JsonResponse(
        {
            "messages": [_message_data(message) for message in new_messages],
        }
    )
    response["Cache-Control"] = "no-store"
    return response


@login_required
@require_GET
def unread_message_count(request):
    """Return the signed-in user's current unread direct-message count."""
    count = (
        DirectMessage.objects.filter(
            Q(conversation__user_one=request.user)
            | Q(conversation__user_two=request.user),
            read_at__isnull=True,
        )
        .exclude(sender=request.user)
        .count()
    )
    response = JsonResponse({"count": count})
    response["Cache-Control"] = "no-store"
    return response

FILE: events\migrations\0008_backfill_contact_notifications.py

"""Bring pre-existing pending requests into the notification list."""

from django.db import migrations


def backfill_contact_notifications(apps, schema_editor):
    request_model = apps.get_model("events", "ContactRequest")
    notification_model = apps.get_model("events", "Notification")
    alias = schema_editor.connection.alias
    requests = (
        request_model.objects.using(alias)
        .filter(status="pending")
        .select_related("sender")
    )
    for contact_request in requests.iterator():
        sender = contact_request.sender
        name = sender.display_name or sender.username
        notification, created = notification_model.objects.using(alias).get_or_create(
            contact_request_id=contact_request.pk,
            defaults={
                "recipient_id": contact_request.recipient_id,
                "event_id": contact_request.event_id,
                "subject": f"Contact request from {name}",
                "body": contact_request.message,
            },
        )
        if created:
            # auto_now_add overrides values supplied during creation.
            notification_model.objects.using(alias).filter(pk=notification.pk).update(
                created_at=contact_request.created_at
            )


def remove_contact_notifications(apps, schema_editor):
    notification_model = apps.get_model("events", "Notification")
    notification_model.objects.using(schema_editor.connection.alias).filter(
        contact_request__isnull=False
    ).delete()


class Migration(migrations.Migration):
    dependencies = [("events", "0007_notification_contact_request")]

    operations = [
        migrations.RunPython(
            backfill_contact_notifications, remove_contact_notifications
        ),
    ]

============================================================
FILE: events\models.py

"""Data models for NYC Parks events imported from the city's open data feed."""

from django.contrib.postgres.indexes import GinIndex
from django.db import models
from django.utils import timezone

from .filters import NYC_TIMEZONE, nyc_today
from .search import event_search_vector


class Event(models.Model):
    """One event, either copied from the NYC Parks feed or posted by a user.

    The feed only publishes a rolling two-week window, so this table is the
    durable copy of the data: rows stay in the database after they disappear
    from the feed and are marked done once their end time has passed.

    ``content_hash`` fingerprints the imported content so an hourly sync can
    skip rows that have not really changed, and ``guid`` is the unique
    identifier the feed uses for each event. Events posted on the platform
    have a ``host`` and a ``guid`` starting with ``HOSTED_GUID_PREFIX``, which
    the feed never uses, so the sync leaves them alone.
    """

    HOSTED_GUID_PREFIX = "host-"

    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        """Lifecycle of an event.

        The hourly sync moves upcoming events to done; hosts can cancel their
        own upcoming events. Only upcoming events are listed or accept
        interest, posts, and contact requests.
        """

        UPCOMING = "upcoming", "Upcoming"
        DONE = "done", "Done"
        CANCELLED = "cancelled", "Cancelled"

    class Borough(models.TextChoices):
        """The five NYC boroughs a hosted event can take place in."""

        MANHATTAN = "manhattan", "Manhattan"
        BROOKLYN = "brooklyn", "Brooklyn"
        QUEENS = "queens", "Queens"
        BRONX = "bronx", "The Bronx"
        STATEN_ISLAND = "staten-island", "Staten Island"

    guid = models.CharField("source id", max_length=64, unique=True, db_index=True)
    host = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.User",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="hosted_events",
        verbose_name="host",
    )
    borough = models.CharField(
        "borough", max_length=20, choices=Borough.choices, blank=True
    )
    title = models.CharField("title", max_length=300)
    description = models.TextField("description", blank=True)
    start_time = models.DateTimeField("start time", null=True, blank=True)
    end_time = models.DateTimeField("end time", null=True, blank=True)
    location = models.CharField("location", max_length=300, blank=True)
    park_names = models.CharField("park names", max_length=300, blank=True)
    park_ids = models.CharField("park ids", max_length=100, blank=True)
    categories = models.CharField("categories", max_length=500, blank=True)
    latitude = models.FloatField("latitude", null=True, blank=True)
    longitude = models.FloatField("longitude", null=True, blank=True)
    event_url = models.URLField("event URL", max_length=500, blank=True)
    registration_url = models.URLField("registration URL", max_length=500, blank=True)
    image_url = models.URLField("image URL", max_length=500, blank=True)
    instructor = models.CharField("instructor", max_length=100, blank=True)
    contact_phone = models.CharField("contact phone", max_length=50, blank=True)
    status = models.CharField(
        "status",
        max_length=10,
        choices=Status.choices,
        default=Status.UPCOMING,
        db_index=True,
    )
    content_hash = models.CharField("content hash", max_length=64)
    source_version = models.CharField("source version", max_length=64, blank=True)
    first_seen_at = models.DateTimeField("first seen at", auto_now_add=True)
    last_synced_at = models.DateTimeField("last synced at", null=True, blank=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField("updated at", auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        """List events chronologically by default."""

        ordering = ["start_time", "end_time", "title"]
        indexes = [GinIndex(event_search_vector(), name="event_search_gin")]

    def __str__(self):
        """Return the event title for admin listings and debug output."""
        return self.title

    @property
    def primary_category(self):
        """Return the first category the feed listed, e.g. "Fitness".

        The sync stores every category in one pipe-separated string, while a
        card tag only has room for the first name.
        """
        return self.categories.split("|")[0].strip()

    @property
    def has_ended(self):
        """Return whether the event is over, even before the sync notices.

        Uses the sync's rule: an event ends at its end time, or, without one,
        once the day it starts has passed in New York.
        """
        if self.status == self.Status.DONE:
            return True
        if self.end_time:
            return self.end_time < timezone.now()
        if self.start_time:
            return timezone.localtime(self.start_time, NYC_TIMEZONE).date() < (
                nyc_today()
            )
        return False

    @property
    def is_open(self):
        """Return whether the event is upcoming or under way, not cancelled."""
        return self.status == self.Status.UPCOMING and not self.has_ended

    @property
    def is_cancelled(self):
        """Return whether the host or the feed cancelled this event.

        Hosts cancel through the cancelled status. The feed has no such
        status and instead prefixes the title, spelled both "CANCELLED" and
        "CANCELED".
        """
        if self.status == self.Status.CANCELLED:
            return True
        return self.title.lstrip().upper().startswith(("CANCELLED", "CANCELED"))


class EventInterest(models.Model):
    """Record a user's attendance intention for an event."""

    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        INTERESTED = "interested", "Interested"
        WANT_TO_ATTEND = "want_to_attend", "Want to Attend"

    event = models.ForeignKey(
        Event,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="interests",
    )
    user = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.User",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="event_interests",
    )
    status = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=Status.choices,
        default=Status.INTERESTED,
    )
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["-created_at"]
        constraints = [
            models.UniqueConstraint(
                fields=["event", "user"],
                name="unique_event_interest",
            ),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.user} â€” {self.get_status_display()} â€” {self.event}"


class Notification(models.Model):
    """An event update or incoming contact request.

    Event updates become read when displayed. Contact requests stay unread
    until accepted or declined.
    """

    recipient = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.User",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="notifications",
    )
    event = models.ForeignKey(
        Event,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="notifications",
    )
    contact_request = models.OneToOneField(
        "ContactRequest",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="notification",
    )
    subject = models.CharField("subject", max_length=200)
    body = models.TextField("message")
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    read_at = models.DateTimeField("read at", null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["-created_at", "-pk"]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.subject} â†’ {self.recipient}"


class ContactRequest(models.Model):
    """A request to contact another user through a shared event."""

    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        PENDING = "pending", "Pending"
        ACCEPTED = "accepted", "Accepted"
        DECLINED = "declined", "Declined"

    event = models.ForeignKey(
        Event,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="contact_requests",
    )
    sender = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.User",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="sent_contact_requests",
    )
    recipient = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.User",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="received_contact_requests",
    )
    message = models.CharField(max_length=500, blank=True)
    status = models.CharField(
        max_length=10,
        choices=Status.choices,
        default=Status.PENDING,
    )
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["-created_at"]
        constraints = [
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=~models.Q(sender=models.F("recipient")),
                name="contact_request_not_self",
            ),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.sender} â†’ {self.recipient} ({self.status})"


class Conversation(models.Model):
    """A private message thread shared by a pair of users."""

    user_one = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.User",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="conversations_as_one",
    )
    user_two = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.User",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="conversations_as_two",
    )
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["-updated_at", "-pk"]
        constraints = [
            models.UniqueConstraint(
                fields=["user_one", "user_two"],
                name="unique_user_pair_conversation",
            ),
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=models.Q(user_one__lt=models.F("user_two")),
                name="conversation_users_in_order",
            ),
        ]

    @classmethod
    def get_or_create_for_users(cls, first_user, second_user):
        """Return the single pair thread with participant IDs in order."""
        if first_user.pk == second_user.pk:
            raise ValueError("A conversation requires two different users.")
        user_one, user_two = sorted((first_user, second_user), key=lambda user: user.pk)
        return cls.objects.get_or_create(user_one=user_one, user_two=user_two)

    def other_participant(self, user):
        """Return the other participant, or None when user is not in thread."""
        if user.pk == self.user_one_id:
            return self.user_two
        if user.pk == self.user_two_id:
            return self.user_one
        return None

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Conversation: {self.user_one} â†” {self.user_two}"


class DirectMessage(models.Model):
    """A message within a pair-level conversation, optionally about an event."""

    conversation = models.ForeignKey(
        Conversation,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="messages",
    )
    sender = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.User",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="direct_messages",
    )
    event = models.ForeignKey(
        Event,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="direct_messages",
    )
    body = models.TextField(max_length=5000)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    read_at = models.DateTimeField("read at", null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["created_at", "pk"]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Message from {self.sender} in conversation {self.conversation_id}"


class EventPost(models.Model):
    """A discussion post associated with an event."""

    event = models.ForeignKey(
        Event,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="discussion_posts",
    )
    author = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.User",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="event_posts",
    )
    body = models.TextField(max_length=5000)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["-created_at"]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Post by {self.author} on {self.event}"


class EventReply(models.Model):
    """A reply to a discussion post."""

    post = models.ForeignKey(
        EventPost,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="replies",
    )
    author = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.User",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="event_replies",
    )
    body = models.TextField(max_length=3000)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["created_at"]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Reply by {self.author} on post {self.post_id}"

FILE: events\notifications.py
============================================================
"""Event announcements and in-app contact request notifications."""

from django.conf import settings
from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.core.mail import send_mass_mail
from django.utils import dateformat, timezone
from django.utils.text import Truncator

from .filters import NYC_TIMEZONE
from .models import ContactRequest, Notification

# Edits to these fields send interested users an automatic notice.
NOTIFIED_CHANGES = ("start_time", "end_time", "location")
# Event titles can be longer than a notification subject allows.
SUBJECT_MAX_LENGTH = Notification._meta.get_field("subject").max_length


def interested_recipients(event):
    """Return the users interested in an event, other than its host."""
    return (
        get_user_model()
        .objects.filter(event_interests__event=event)
        .exclude(pk=event.host_id)
        .distinct()
    )


def notify_interested(event, subject, body, event_url):
    """Send a message to everyone interested in an event.

    Each recipient gets an in-app notification and their own email, so no
    one sees anyone else's address.

    Returns:
        int: how many people were notified.
    """
    recipients = list(interested_recipients(event))
    subject = Truncator(subject).chars(SUBJECT_MAX_LENGTH)
    Notification.objects.bulk_create(
        Notification(recipient=user, event=event, subject=subject, body=body)
        for user in recipients
    )
    email_body = f"{body}\n\nEvent details: {event_url}"
    send_mass_mail(
        [
            (subject, email_body, settings.DEFAULT_FROM_EMAIL, [user.email])
            for user in recipients
        ]
    )
    return len(recipients)


def notify_contact_request(contact_request):
    """Create at most one recipient notification for a saved request."""
    pending = contact_request.status == ContactRequest.Status.PENDING
    subject = "Contact request" if pending else "Introduction"
    notification, _ = Notification.objects.get_or_create(
        contact_request=contact_request,
        defaults={
            "recipient_id": contact_request.recipient_id,
            "event_id": contact_request.event_id,
            "subject": f"{subject} from {contact_request.sender.get_display_name()}",
            "body": contact_request.message,
            "read_at": None if pending else timezone.now(),
        },
    )
    return notification


def describe_when(start, end):
    """Describe an event's date and time in New York time, e.g. for emails."""
    if not start:
        return "To be announced"
    when = dateformat.format(
        timezone.localtime(start, NYC_TIMEZONE), r"l, F j, Y \a\t g:i A"
    )
    if end:
        when += " â€“ " + dateformat.format(
            timezone.localtime(end, NYC_TIMEZONE), "g:i A"
        )
    return when


def change_notice(event, previous, changed_fields):
    """Return the subject and body announcing an edit, or None if not needed.

    Args:
        event: the event with its new details.
        previous: the field values before the edit.
        changed_fields: names of the fields the host changed.
    """
    if not set(NOTIFIED_CHANGES) & set(changed_fields):
        return None
    lines = []
    if {"start_time", "end_time"} & set(changed_fields):
        before = describe_when(previous["start_time"], previous["end_time"])
        after = describe_when(event.start_time, event.end_time)
        lines.append(f"When: {after} (was {before})")
    if "location" in changed_fields:
        lines.append(f"Where: {event.location} (was {previous['location']})")
    host = event.host.get_display_name()
    body = f"{host} changed the details of {event.title}:\n\n" + "\n".join(lines)
    return f"Update: {event.title} has changed", body


def cancel_notice(event, message=""):
    """Return the subject and body announcing that a host cancelled an event.

    Args:
        event: the cancelled event.
        message: the host's optional note to attendees.
    """
    host = event.host.get_display_name()
    lines = [
        f"{host} cancelled {event.title}.",
        "",
        f"It was planned for: {describe_when(event.start_time, event.end_time)}",
    ]
    if event.location:
        lines.append(f"Where: {event.location}")
    if message:
        lines += ["", f"Message from {host}:", message]
    return f"Cancelled: {event.title}", "\n".join(lines)

FILE: events\search.py
