"""PostgreSQL full-text expressions and queries for event discovery."""

from django.contrib.postgres.search import SearchQuery, SearchRank, SearchVector
from django.db.models import F


def event_search_vector():
    """Build the same weighted document for the GIN index and search queries."""
    return (
        SearchVector("title", config="english", weight="A")
        + SearchVector(
            "categories", "park_names", "location", config="english", weight="B"
        )
        + SearchVector("description", config="english", weight="C")
    )


def search_events(events, query):
    """Match an event queryset against user text, ordered by relevance."""
    search_query = SearchQuery(query, config="english", search_type="websearch")
    vector = event_search_vector()
    return (
        events.annotate(search_document=vector)
        .filter(search_document=search_query)
        .annotate(rank=SearchRank(vector, search_query))
        .order_by("-rank", F("start_time").asc(nulls_last=True), "pk")
    )



============================================================
FILE: events\services.py
============================================================
"""Fetch NYC Parks events from the open data feed and sync them into the DB.

The feed (https://data.cityofnewyork.us/api/v3/views/w3wp-dpdi/query.json)
publishes a rolling two-week window of events in a single JSON array. Each
sync upserts every record by its unique ``guid`` and only writes rows whose
content actually changed, detected with a hash of the imported fields; the
dataset is rewritten wholesale on every refresh, so the feed's own version
stamps would flag every row as changed even when nothing moved.
"""

import hashlib
import html
import json
import logging
from datetime import datetime
from urllib.error import URLError
from urllib.request import Request, urlopen
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

from django.db import transaction
from django.db.models import Q
from django.utils import timezone
from django.utils.html import strip_tags

from .models import Event

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

FEED_URL = "https://data.cityofnewyork.us/api/v3/views/w3wp-dpdi/query.json"
REQUEST_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 30
NYC_TIMEZONE = ZoneInfo("America/New_York")

# Imported fields compared between syncs to detect real changes.
HASHED_FIELDS = (
    "title",
    "description",
    "start_time",
    "end_time",
    "location",
    "park_names",
    "park_ids",
    "categories",
    "latitude",
    "longitude",
    "event_url",
    "registration_url",
    "image_url",
    "instructor",
    "contact_phone",
)


class SyncError(Exception):
    """Raised when the feed cannot be fetched or parsed; the DB is untouched."""


def fetch_feed():
    """Download the events feed and return its list of records.

    Returns:
        list: raw records exactly as published by the feed.

    Raises:
        SyncError: if the request fails or the payload is not a list of
            records, leaving the database untouched.
    """
    request = Request(
        FEED_URL,
        headers={
            "Accept": "application/json",
            "User-Agent": "team-5-events-sync/1.0",
        },
    )
    try:
        with urlopen(request, timeout=REQUEST_TIMEOUT_SECONDS) as response:
            payload = json.load(response)
    except (URLError, OSError, ValueError) as error:
        raise SyncError(f"could not fetch the events feed: {error}") from error
    if not isinstance(payload, list) or not all(
        isinstance(record, dict) for record in payload
    ):
        raise SyncError("the events feed did not contain a list of records")
    return payload


def clean_text(value):
    """Return plain text with HTML entities decoded and tags removed."""
    if not value:
        return ""
    text = strip_tags(html.unescape(str(value)))
    return " ".join(text.split())


def secure_url(value):
    """Return the URL served over https, because the feed only links via http."""
    url = str(value or "").strip()
    if url.startswith("http://"):
        return "https://" + url[len("http://") :]
    return url


def nested_url(record, key):
    """Return the https URL stored under ``record[key].url``, if present."""
    container = record.get(key) or {}
    return secure_url(container.get("url"))


def parse_datetime(value):
    """Parse a naive NYC-local ISO timestamp into an aware datetime, or None."""
    if not value:
        return None
    try:
        parsed = datetime.fromisoformat(value)
    except ValueError:
        return None
    return parsed.replace(tzinfo=NYC_TIMEZONE)


def parse_coordinates(value):
    """Parse the feed's "latitude, longitude" string into a (lat, lon) pair."""
    try:
        latitude, longitude = (float(part) for part in str(value).split(","))
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None, None
    return latitude, longitude


def content_hash(data):
    """Fingerprint the imported fields so unchanged rows can be skipped."""
    material = json.dumps(
        {field: data[field] for field in HASHED_FIELDS},
        sort_keys=True,
        ensure_ascii=False,
        default=str,
    )
    return hashlib.sha256(material.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def parse_record(record):
    """Convert one feed record into validated field values for :class:`Event`.

    Returns:
        dict: model field values including ``content_hash`` and
        ``source_version``.

    Raises:
        SyncError: if the record has no usable ``guid`` or ``title``.
    """
    guid = str(record.get("guid") or "").strip()
    title = clean_text(record.get("title"))
    if not guid or not title:
        raise SyncError(f"feed record is missing a guid or title: {guid!r}")
    latitude, longitude = parse_coordinates(record.get("coordinates"))
    data = {
        "guid": guid,
        "title": title[:300],
        "description": clean_text(record.get("description")),
        "start_time": parse_datetime(record.get("starttime")),
        "end_time": parse_datetime(record.get("endtime")),
        "location": clean_text(record.get("location"))[:300],
        "park_names": clean_text(record.get("parknames"))[:300],
        "park_ids": clean_text(record.get("parkids"))[:100],
        "categories": clean_text(record.get("categories"))[:500],
        "latitude": latitude,
        "longitude": longitude,
        "event_url": nested_url(record, "link")[:500],
        "registration_url": nested_url(record, "registration_url")[:500],
        "image_url": nested_url(record, "image")[:500],
        "instructor": clean_text(record.get("instructor"))[:100],
        "contact_phone": clean_text(record.get("contact_phone"))[:50],
        "source_version": str(record.get(":version") or "")[:64],
    }
    data["content_hash"] = content_hash(data)
    return data


def sync_events():
    """Fetch the feed and upsert events, then mark finished ones done.

    New records are created, records whose content changed are updated, and
    unchanged records are left alone. Records that vanished from the feed are
    kept because the feed only publishes a rolling window. Events posted by
    hosts never appear in the feed, so only this last step affects them:
    upcoming events whose end time has passed are marked done, and hosted
    events without an end time are done once the day they start has ended.

    Returns:
        dict: counts for ``fetched``, ``created``, ``updated``, ``unchanged``
        and ``marked_done``.

    Raises:
        SyncError: if the feed cannot be fetched or parsed; no rows change.
    """
    records = fetch_feed()
    parsed = [parse_record(record) for record in records]
    counts = {
        "fetched": len(parsed),
        "created": 0,
        "updated": 0,
        "unchanged": 0,
        "marked_done": 0,
    }
    now = timezone.now()
    with transaction.atomic():
        existing = {event.guid: event for event in Event.objects.all()}
        created = []
        for data in parsed:
            current = existing.get(data["guid"])
            if current is None:
                created.append(Event(**data, last_synced_at=now))
                continue
            if current.content_hash == data["content_hash"]:
                counts["unchanged"] += 1
                continue
            for field, value in data.items():
                setattr(current, field, value)
            current.last_synced_at = now
            current.save()
            counts["updated"] += 1
        if created:
            Event.objects.bulk_create(created, batch_size=100)
            counts["created"] = len(created)
        start_of_today = timezone.localtime(now, NYC_TIMEZONE).replace(
            hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0
        )
        counts["marked_done"] = (
            Event.objects.filter(status=Event.Status.UPCOMING)
            .filter(
                Q(end_time__lt=now)
                | Q(
                    host__isnull=False,
                    end_time__isnull=True,
                    start_time__lt=start_of_today,
                )
            )
            .update(status=Event.Status.DONE)
        )
    logger.info("event sync finished: %s", counts)
    return counts

============================================================
FILE: events\static\events\message-count.js
============================================================
"use strict";

(() => {
    const link = document.querySelector("[data-message-count-url]");
    if (!link) return;

    let badge = link.querySelector(".message-count-badge");
    let requestInProgress = false;

    async function refreshMessageCount() {
        if (document.hidden || requestInProgress) return;
        requestInProgress = true;

        try {
            const response = await fetch(link.dataset.messageCountUrl, {
                cache: "no-store",
                headers: { Accept: "application/json" }
            });
            if (!response.ok) return;

            const data = await response.json();
            const count = Number(data.count);
            if (!Number.isInteger(count) || count < 0) return;

            if (count > 0) {
                if (!badge) {
                    badge = document.createElement("span");
                    badge.className = "count-pill message-count-badge";
                    link.append(" ");
                    link.append(badge);
                }
                badge.textContent = `${count} new`;
            } else if (badge) {
                badge.remove();
                badge = null;
            }
            link.setAttribute(
                "aria-label",
                count ? `Messages, ${count} new` : "Messages"
            );
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("Unable to refresh unread message count.", error);
        } finally {
            requestInProgress = false;
        }
    }

    refreshMessageCount();
    window.setInterval(refreshMessageCount, 3000);
    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", refreshMessageCount);
})();

{% if user.is_authenticated %}
    {% if user.pk == attendee.user.pk %}
        <p class="attendee-contact-note">This is your profile.</p>
    {% elif attendee.contact_request %}
        <p class="status-message">
            {% if attendee.contact_request.status == "pending" %}
                Contact request sent &mdash; awaiting response.
            {% else %}
                Contact request accepted.
            {% endif %}
        </p>
    {% elif can_interact and user_has_interest %}
        <form method="post" action="{% url 'send_contact_request' event.pk attendee.user.pk %}">
            {% csrf_token %}
            <textarea
                class="attendee-contact-message"
                name="message"
                maxlength="500"
                rows="2"
                placeholder="Add an optional message..."
                aria-label="Optional contact request message"
            ></textarea>
            <button type="submit">Send Contact Request</button>
        </form>
    {% elif can_interact %}
        <p class="attendee-contact-note">
            Select Interested or Want to Attend before requesting to connect.

FILE: events\templates\events\conversation.html
============================================================
{% load static tz %}<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>Conversation with {{ other_user.get_display_name }} | NYC Parks Events</title>
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="{% static 'events/messages.css' %}">
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="{% static 'reports/menus.css' %}">
    <script src="{% static 'reports/menus.js' %}" defer></script>
</head>
<body class="chat-page">
    <main class="chat-shell">
        <header class="chat-header">
            <a class="chat-back" href="{% url 'inbox' %}" aria-label="Back to messages">&larr;</a>
            <a class="chat-person" href="{% url 'public_profile' username=other_user.username %}">
                <span class="chat-avatar" aria-hidden="true">{{ other_user.get_display_name|slice:":1"|upper }}</span>
                <span class="chat-person-name">{{ other_user.get_display_name }}</span>
            </a>
            <a class="chat-profile-link" href="{% url 'public_profile' username=other_user.username %}">Profile</a>
            {% url 'conversation_report' conversation.pk as report_url %}
            {% include 'reports/_report_menu.html' with report_url=report_url menu_label="Conversation actions" report_label="Report user" only %}
        </header>
        <p class="chat-moderation-notice"><small>Conversations may be reviewed by administrators for moderation purposes.</small></p>

        {% if messages %}
        <ul class="message-alerts" role="status">
            {% for alert in messages %}<li>{{ alert }}</li>{% endfor %}
        </ul>
        {% endif %}

        <section
            class="chat-history"
            aria-label="Messages"
            aria-live="polite"
            aria-relevant="additions"
            data-latest-message-id="{{ latest_message_id }}"
            data-updates-url="{{ updates_url }}"
        >
            {% if thread_messages %}
            <ol class="message-list">
                {% for message in thread_messages %}
                <li
                    class="message-row{% if message.sender_id == request.user.pk %} is-own-message{% endif %}"
                    data-message-id="{{ message.pk }}"
                >
                    <div class="message-bubble">
                        {% if message.sender_id != request.user.pk %}
                        <span class="message-sender">{{ message.sender.get_display_name }}</span>
                        {% endif %}
                        <p class="message-body">{{ message.body|linebreaksbr }}</p>
                        <time class="message-time" datetime="{{ message.created_at|date:'c' }}">
                            {% timezone "America/New_York" %}{{ message.created_at|date:"g:i A" }}{% endtimezone %}
                        </time>
                    </div>
                </li>
                {% endfor %}
            </ol>
            {% else %}
            <p class="chat-empty">No messages yet. Say hello to start the conversation.</p>
            {% endif %}
        </section>

        <form class="chat-composer" method="post">
            {% csrf_token %}
            {{ form.non_field_errors }}
            {{ form.body.errors }}
            {{ form.body }}
            <button type="submit" aria-label="Send message">Send</button>
            <span class="chat-status" role="status" aria-live="polite"></span>
        </form>
    </main>
    <script>
        const history = document.querySelector(".chat-history");
        const list = history.querySelector(".message-list");
        const composer = document.querySelector(".chat-composer");
        const messageField = composer.querySelector("textarea");
        const sendButton = composer.querySelector("button");
        const status = composer.querySelector(".chat-status");
        let latestMessageId = Number(history.dataset.latestMessageId);
        let pollInProgress = false;

        function appendMessage(message) {
            if (history.querySelector(`[data-message-id="${message.id}"]`)) {
                latestMessageId = Math.max(latestMessageId, message.id);
                return;
            }

            const rows = list || history.querySelector(".message-list") || createMessageList();
            const item = document.createElement("li");
            item.className = "message-row";
            item.dataset.messageId = message.id;
            if (message.sender_id === {{ request.user.pk }}) {
                item.classList.add("is-own-message");
            }

            const bubble = document.createElement("div");
            bubble.className = "message-bubble";
            if (message.sender_id !== {{ request.user.pk }}) {
                const sender = document.createElement("span");
                sender.className = "message-sender";
                sender.textContent = message.sender_name;
                bubble.append(sender);
            }

            const body = document.createElement("p");
            body.className = "message-body";
            body.textContent = message.body;
            bubble.append(body);

            const time = document.createElement("time");
            time.className = "message-time";
            time.dateTime = message.created_at;
            time.textContent = new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, {
                hour: "numeric",
                minute: "2-digit"
            }).format(new Date(message.created_at));
            bubble.append(time);
            item.append(bubble);
            rows.append(item);
            latestMessageId = Math.max(latestMessageId, message.id);
            history.scrollTop = history.scrollHeight;
        }

        function createMessageList() {
            const newList = document.createElement("ol");
            newList.className = "message-list";
            const empty = history.querySelector(".chat-empty");
            if (empty) empty.remove();
            history.append(newList);
            return newList;
        }

        async function pollMessages() {
            if (pollInProgress || document.hidden) return;
            pollInProgress = true;
            try {
                const response = await fetch(
                    `${history.dataset.updatesUrl}?after=${latestMessageId}`,
                    {
                        cache: "no-store",
                        headers: { "Accept": "application/json" }
                    }
                );
                if (!response.ok) throw new Error("Message updates are unavailable.");
                const data = await response.json();
                data.messages.forEach(appendMessage);
                status.textContent = "";
            } catch (error) {
                status.textContent = "Trying to reconnect...";
            } finally {
                pollInProgress = false;
            }
        }

        composer.addEventListener("submit", async function (event) {
            event.preventDefault();
            if (!messageField.value.trim()) return;
            sendButton.disabled = true;
            status.textContent = "";
            try {
                const response = await fetch(window.location.href, {
                    method: "POST",
                    body: new URLSearchParams(new FormData(composer)),
                    headers: {
                        "Accept": "application/json",
                        "X-Requested-With": "XMLHttpRequest"
                    }
                });
                const data = await response.json();
                if (!response.ok) throw new Error("Message could not be sent.");
                appendMessage(data.message);
                messageField.value = "";
                messageField.focus();
            } catch (error) {
                status.textContent = error.message || "Message could not be sent.";
            } finally {
                sendButton.disabled = false;
            }
        });

        history.scrollTop = history.scrollHeight;
        pollMessages();
        window.setInterval(pollMessages, 3000);
        document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", function () {
            if (!document.hidden) pollMessages();
        });
    </script>
    {% include "chatbot/widget.html" %}
</body>
</html>

        document.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
            if (!event.target.closest(".profile-preview")) {
                document.querySelectorAll(".profile-preview.open").forEach(
                    function (item) {
                        item.classList.remove("open");
                        const button = item.querySelector(
                            ".profile-preview-trigger"
                        );
                        if (button) {
                            button.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
                        }
                    }
                );
            }
        });
    </script>

    <main class="event-form-page">
        <a class="search-back" href="{% url 'event_detail' event.pk %}">Back to event</a>
        <h1>Cancel this event?</h1>
        <p class="event-form-intro">
            <strong>{{ event.title }}</strong>,
            {% timezone "America/New_York" %}
                {% if event.start_time %}{{ event.start_time|date:"l, F j, Y" }} at {{ event.start_time|date:"g:i A" }}{% else %}date to be announced{% endif %}{% endtimezone %}.
            It will be removed from the home page and search, and moved to the
            archive in My events. This can't be undone.
        </p>

        <form class="event-form" method="post">
            {% csrf_token %}
            <p class="cancel-recipients">
                {% if recipient_count %}
                    <strong>{{ recipient_count }}</strong>
                    {% if recipient_count == 1 %}person is{% else %}people are{% endif %}
                    signed up as Interested or Want to Attend. They'll get an
                    email and a notification that the event is cancelled.
                {% else %}
                    Nobody is signed up for this event yet, so no one will be
                    notified.
                {% endif %}
            </p>
            {% with field=form.message %}
            <div class="form-field{% if field.errors %} has-error{% endif %}">
                <label for="{{ field.id_for_label }}">{{ field.label }} <span class="optional">(optional)</span></label>
                {{ field }}
                <p class="help-text">{{ field.help_text }}</p>
                {{ field.errors }}
            </div>
            {% endwith %}
            <div class="form-actions">
                <button type="submit" class="btn-primary btn-danger">Cancel event</button>
                <a href="{% url 'event_detail' event.pk %}">Keep event</a>

        </form>

                        {% endfor %}

                {{ field }}
                {% endif %}

============================================================
FILE: events\tests\test_cancelling.py
============================================================
"""Tests for hosts cancelling their events and the My events archive."""

from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from unittest.mock import patch
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.core import mail
from django.test import TestCase
from django.urls import reverse
from django.utils import timezone

from events.models import Event, EventInterest, Notification
from events.notifications import cancel_notice, notify_interested

NYC = ZoneInfo("America/New_York")


class CancelTestCase(TestCase):
    """A host, a fan of each interest kind, a bystander, and a hosted event."""

    @classmethod
    def setUpTestData(cls):
        User = get_user_model()

        def user(name, **fields):
            return User.objects.create_user(
                username=name,
                email=f"{name}@example.com",
                password="pass12345",
                **fields,
            )

        cls.host = user("host", display_name="Park Pals")
        cls.fan = user("fan")
        cls.goer = user("goer")
        cls.bystander = user("bystander")

    def setUp(self):
        self.event = self.hosted("yoga")
        EventInterest.objects.create(event=self.event, user=self.fan)
        EventInterest.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            user=self.goer,
            status=EventInterest.Status.WANT_TO_ATTEND,
        )
        EventInterest.objects.create(event=self.event, user=self.host)
        self.cancel_url = reverse("event_cancel", args=[self.event.pk])
        self.detail_url = reverse("event_detail", args=[self.event.pk])

    def hosted(self, guid, host=None, **fields):
        """Create an upcoming event posted by the host."""
        defaults = {
            "title": "Sunset Yoga",
            "description": "Bring a mat.",
            "start_time": datetime(2099, 1, 15, 18, 30, tzinfo=NYC),
            "end_time": datetime(2099, 1, 15, 19, 45, tzinfo=NYC),
            "location": "Pier 1",
            "borough": Event.Borough.BROOKLYN,
            "categories": "Fitness",
            "registration_url": "https://example.org/tickets",
        }
        defaults.update(fields)
        return Event.objects.create(
            guid=Event.HOSTED_GUID_PREFIX + guid,
            host=host or self.host,
            content_hash="",
            **defaults,
        )

    def cancel(self, message="", user=None):
        self.client.force_login(user or self.host)
        return self.client.post(self.cancel_url, {"message": message}, follow=True)


class CancelEventTests(CancelTestCase):
    """The host confirms, the event is cancelled, and attendees are told."""

    def test_host_sees_cancel_button(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        self.assertContains(self.client.get(self.detail_url), self.cancel_url)

    def test_others_do_not_see_cancel_button(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.fan)
        self.assertNotContains(self.client.get(self.detail_url), self.cancel_url)

    def test_confirmation_page_counts_both_interest_kinds(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        response = self.client.get(self.cancel_url)
        self.assertContains(response, "Cancel this event?")
        self.assertContains(response, "This can't be undone.")
        self.assertEqual(response.context["recipient_count"], 2)
        self.event.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(self.event.status, Event.Status.UPCOMING)

    def test_cancel_without_message(self):
        response = self.cancel()

        self.assertRedirects(response, self.detail_url)
        self.assertContains(
            response, "Event cancelled. We let 2 interested people know."
        )
        self.event.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(self.event.status, Event.Status.CANCELLED)
        self.assertEqual(
            sorted(email.to[0] for email in mail.outbox),
            ["fan@example.com", "goer@example.com"],
        )
        email = mail.outbox[0]
        self.assertEqual(email.subject, "Cancelled: Sunset Yoga")
        self.assertIn("Park Pals cancelled Sunset Yoga.", email.body)
        self.assertIn(
            "It was planned for: Thursday, January 15, 2099 at 6:30 PM â€“ 7:45 PM",
            email.body,
        )
        self.assertIn("Where: Pier 1", email.body)
        self.assertNotIn("Message from", email.body)
        self.assertIn("http://testserver" + self.detail_url, email.body)
        self.assertEqual(
            set(Notification.objects.values_list("recipient__username", flat=True)),
            {"fan", "goer"},
        )

    def test_cancel_with_message(self):
        self.cancel(message="Rain is forecast all day.")
        body = Notification.objects.first().body
        self.assertTrue(
            body.endswith("Message from Park Pals:\nRain is forecast all day.")
        )
        self.assertIn("Rain is forecast all day.", mail.outbox[0].body)

    def test_cancel_with_nobody_interested(self):
        EventInterest.objects.all().delete()
        response = self.cancel()
        self.assertEqual(
            [str(message) for message in response.context["messages"]],
            ["Event cancelled."],
        )
        self.assertEqual(mail.outbox, [])

    def test_message_too_long_is_rejected(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        response = self.client.post(self.cancel_url, {"message": "x" * 2001})
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertTrue(response.context["form"].errors)
        self.event.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(self.event.status, Event.Status.UPCOMING)
        self.assertEqual(mail.outbox, [])

    def test_only_host_can_cancel(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.bystander)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get(self.cancel_url).status_code, 404)
        self.assertEqual(self.cancel(user=self.bystander).status_code, 404)
        self.event.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(self.event.status, Event.Status.UPCOMING)

    def test_feed_events_cannot_be_cancelled(self):
        feed = Event.objects.create(guid="feed-1", title="Parks Tour", content_hash="")
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        response = self.client.get(reverse("event_cancel", args=[feed.pk]))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 404)

    def test_ended_events_cannot_be_cancelled(self):
        past = timezone.now() - timedelta(days=2)
        self.event.start_time = past
        self.event.end_time = past + timedelta(hours=1)
        self.event.save()
        self.assertEqual(self.cancel().status_code, 404)

    def test_cancelling_twice_notifies_once(self):
        self.cancel()
        self.assertEqual(self.cancel().status_code, 404)
        self.assertEqual(len(mail.outbox), 2)

    def test_simultaneous_cancel_notifies_once(self):
        """A request that loaded the event before another cancelled it stops."""
        stale = Event.objects.get(pk=self.event.pk)
        Event.objects.filter(pk=self.event.pk).update(status=Event.Status.CANCELLED)
        with patch("events.views.get_hosted_upcoming_event", return_value=stale):
            self.assertEqual(self.cancel().status_code, 404)
        self.assertEqual(mail.outbox, [])
        self.assertFalse(Notification.objects.exists())

    def test_anonymous_redirected_to_login(self):
        response = self.client.post(self.cancel_url)
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 302)
        self.assertIn(reverse("login"), response["Location"])

    def test_other_methods_not_allowed(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.put(self.cancel_url).status_code, 405)


class CancelledEventTests(CancelTestCase):
    """A cancelled event is archived: hidden from listings and read-only."""

    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        self.event.status = Event.Status.CANCELLED
        self.event.save()

    def test_hidden_from_home_search_and_filters(self):
        self.hosted("still-on", title="Sunrise Yoga")
        self.client.force_login(self.fan)
        search = reverse("search")
        for response in (
            self.client.get(reverse("landing")),
            self.client.get(search, {"q": "yoga"}),
            self.client.get(search, {"borough": "brooklyn"}),
        ):
            self.assertContains(response, "Sunrise Yoga")
            self.assertNotContains(response, "Sunset Yoga")

    def test_detail_page_shows_banner_and_hides_actions(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        response = self.client.get(self.detail_url)
        self.assertContains(response, "This event was cancelled")
        self.assertContains(response, "new comments are closed")
        for hidden in (
            reverse("event_edit", args=[self.event.pk]),
            self.cancel_url,
            reverse("event_notify", args=[self.event.pk]),
            reverse("toggle_event_interest", args=[self.event.pk]),
            reverse("create_event_post", args=[self.event.pk]),
            "https://example.org/tickets",
        ):
            self.assertNotContains(response, hidden)

    def test_host_cannot_edit_or_message(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        edit = self.client.get(reverse("event_edit", args=[self.event.pk]))
        self.assertEqual(edit.status_code, 404)
        notify = self.client.post(
            reverse("event_notify", args=[self.event.pk]),
            {"subject": "Hi", "message": "Hello"},
        )
        self.assertEqual(notify.status_code, 404)

    def test_interest_cannot_be_added(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.bystander)
        self.client.post(reverse("toggle_event_interest", args=[self.event.pk]))
        self.assertFalse(
            EventInterest.objects.filter(user=self.bystander, event=self.event).exists()
        )

    def test_model_flags(self):
        self.assertTrue(self.event.is_cancelled)
        self.assertFalse(self.event.is_open)
        self.assertFalse(self.event.has_ended)


class MyEventsTests(CancelTestCase):
    """Hosts see their upcoming events and an archive of the rest."""

    def test_requires_login(self):
        response = self.client.get(reverse("my_events"))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 302)
        self.assertIn(reverse("login"), response["Location"])

    def test_splits_upcoming_and_archived(self):
        past = timezone.now() - timedelta(days=3)
        ended = self.hosted(
            "ended",
            title="Old Picnic",
            start_time=past,
            end_time=past + timedelta(hours=1),
        )
        cancelled = self.hosted(
            "cancelled",
            title="Rained Out",
            start_time=datetime(2099, 3, 1, 10, 0, tzinfo=NYC),
            end_time=None,
            status=Event.Status.CANCELLED,
        )
        self.hosted("other", host=self.fan, title="Someone Else's Event")
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        response = self.client.get(reverse("my_events"))

        self.assertEqual(list(response.context["upcoming_events"]), [self.event])
        self.assertEqual(list(response.context["archived_events"]), [cancelled, ended])
        self.assertContains(response, reverse("event_edit", args=[self.event.pk]))
        self.assertContains(response, self.cancel_url)
        self.assertNotContains(response, reverse("event_edit", args=[ended.pk]))
        self.assertContains(response, "Cancelled")
        self.assertContains(response, "Ended")
        self.assertNotContains(response, "Someone Else")

    def test_empty_states(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.bystander)
        response = self.client.get(reverse("my_events"))
        self.assertContains(response, "You have no upcoming events.")
        self.assertContains(response, "Cancelled and past events will show up here.")

    def test_header_links_to_my_events(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        self.assertContains(self.client.get(reverse("landing")), reverse("my_events"))
        profile = reverse("public_profile", kwargs={"username": "host"})
        self.assertContains(self.client.get(profile), reverse("my_events"))


class CancelNoticeTests(CancelTestCase):
    """The wording of cancellation notices."""

    def test_without_date_or_location(self):
        event = self.hosted("tba", start_time=None, end_time=None, location="")
        subject, body = cancel_notice(event)
        self.assertEqual(subject, "Cancelled: Sunset Yoga")

        self.assertNotIn("Where:", body)

    def test_long_titles_fit_the_subject(self):
        notify_interested(self.event, "x" * 300, "Body", event_url="http://x")
        subject = Notification.objects.first().subject
        self.assertEqual(len(subject), 200)
        self.assertEqual(mail.outbox[0].subject, subject)



============================================================
FILE: events\tests\test_command.py

"""Tests for the sync_events management command."""

from io import StringIO
from unittest.mock import patch

from django.core.management import CommandError, call_command
from django.test import TestCase

from events.services import SyncError

COUNTS = {
    "fetched": 852,
    "created": 3,
    "updated": 2,
    "unchanged": 847,
    "marked_done": 5,
}


class SyncEventsCommandTests(TestCase):
    """The command prints a summary or fails loudly for cron."""

    def test_successful_sync_prints_counts(self):
        """A good run reports every count from the sync service."""
        out = StringIO()
        with patch(
            "events.management.commands.sync_events.sync_events",
            return_value=COUNTS,
        ) as mocked:
            call_command("sync_events", stdout=out)

        mocked.assert_called_once()
        self.assertIn("Event sync complete: 852 fetched", out.getvalue())
        self.assertIn("3 created", out.getvalue())
        self.assertIn("2 updated", out.getvalue())
        self.assertIn("847 unchanged", out.getvalue())
        self.assertIn("5 marked done", out.getvalue())

    def test_sync_failure_becomes_a_command_error(self):
        """SyncError is surfaced as CommandError so cron sees a non-zero exit."""
        with patch(
            "events.management.commands.sync_events.sync_events",
            side_effect=SyncError("feed unreachable"),
        ):
            with self.assertRaisesMessage(CommandError, "feed unreachable"):
                call_command("sync_events", stdout=StringIO())

"""Tests for event interests, discussions, and contact requests."""

from datetime import datetime
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.test import TestCase
from django.urls import reverse

from accounts.models import ContactPermission
from events.models import ContactRequest, Event, EventInterest, EventPost, EventReply

User = get_user_model()
NYC = ZoneInfo("America/New_York")


def make_event(guid="community-1", **kwargs):
    """Create an event with sensible defaults."""
    defaults = {
        "title": "Community Yoga",
        "content_hash": "v" * 64,
        "start_time": datetime(2099, 3, 15, 9, 0, tzinfo=NYC),
        "status": Event.Status.UPCOMING,
    }
    defaults.update(kwargs)
    return Event.objects.create(guid=guid, **defaults)


class CommunityViewTestBase(TestCase):
    """Shared fixtures for community view tests."""

    def setUp(self):
        self.event = make_event()
        self.alice = User.objects.create_user(
            username="alice",
            email="alice@example.com",
            password="pass12345",
        )
        self.bob = User.objects.create_user(
            username="bob",
            email="bob@example.com",
            password="pass12345",
            share_profile_with_attendees=True,
        )
        self.client.force_login(self.alice)

    def detail_url(self):
        return reverse("event_detail", args=[self.event.pk])


class EventInterestViewTests(CommunityViewTestBase):
    """Tests for toggling interest in an event."""

    def test_add_interest(self):
        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("toggle_event_interest", args=[self.event.pk])
        )

        self.assertRedirects(response, self.detail_url())
        self.assertTrue(
            EventInterest.objects.filter(event=self.event, user=self.alice).exists()
        )

    def test_remove_existing_interest(self):
        EventInterest.objects.create(event=self.event, user=self.alice)

        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("toggle_event_interest", args=[self.event.pk])
        )

        self.assertRedirects(response, self.detail_url())
        self.assertFalse(
            EventInterest.objects.filter(event=self.event, user=self.alice).exists()
        )

    def test_closed_event_does_not_change_interest(self):
        self.event.status = Event.Status.DONE
        self.event.save()
        EventInterest.objects.create(event=self.event, user=self.alice)

        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("toggle_event_interest", args=[self.event.pk])
        )

        self.assertRedirects(response, self.detail_url())
        self.assertTrue(
            EventInterest.objects.filter(event=self.event, user=self.alice).exists()
        )

    def test_get_is_not_allowed(self):
        response = self.client.get(
            reverse("toggle_event_interest", args=[self.event.pk])
        )

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 405)

    def test_anonymous_user_is_redirected_to_login(self):
        self.client.logout()

        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("toggle_event_interest", args=[self.event.pk])
        )

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 302)
        self.assertIn("/login", response.url)


class EventDiscussionViewTests(CommunityViewTestBase):
    """Tests for event posts and replies."""

    def test_create_post(self):
        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("create_event_post", args=[self.event.pk]),
            {"body": "  Looking forward to this event!  "},
        )

        self.assertRedirects(response, self.detail_url())
        post = EventPost.objects.get()
        self.assertEqual(post.body, "Looking forward to this event!")
        self.assertEqual(post.author, self.alice)
        self.assertEqual(post.event, self.event)

    def test_blank_post_is_not_saved(self):
        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("create_event_post", args=[self.event.pk]),
            {"body": "   "},
        )

        self.assertRedirects(response, self.detail_url())
        self.assertEqual(EventPost.objects.count(), 0)

    def test_post_on_closed_event_is_not_saved(self):
        self.event.status = Event.Status.DONE
        self.event.save()

        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("create_event_post", args=[self.event.pk]),
            {"body": "Hello"},
        )

        self.assertRedirects(response, self.detail_url())
        self.assertEqual(EventPost.objects.count(), 0)

    def test_create_reply(self):
        post = EventPost.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            author=self.bob,
            body="What should I bring?",
        )

        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("create_event_reply", args=[post.pk]),
            {"body": "Bring a yoga mat."},
        )

        self.assertRedirects(response, self.detail_url())
        reply = EventReply.objects.get()
        self.assertEqual(reply.body, "Bring a yoga mat.")
        self.assertEqual(reply.author, self.alice)
        self.assertEqual(reply.post, post)

    def test_blank_reply_is_not_saved(self):
        post = EventPost.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            author=self.bob,
            body="Question",
        )

        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("create_event_reply", args=[post.pk]),
            {"body": "   "},
        )

        self.assertRedirects(response, self.detail_url())
        self.assertEqual(EventReply.objects.count(), 0)

    def test_reply_to_closed_event_is_not_saved(self):
        post = EventPost.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            author=self.bob,
            body="Question",
        )
        self.event.status = Event.Status.DONE
        self.event.save()

        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("create_event_reply", args=[post.pk]),
            {"body": "Reply"},
        )

        self.assertRedirects(response, self.detail_url())
        self.assertEqual(EventReply.objects.count(), 0)


class ContactRequestViewTests(CommunityViewTestBase):
    """Tests for sending and responding to contact requests."""

    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        EventInterest.objects.create(event=self.event, user=self.alice)
        EventInterest.objects.create(event=self.event, user=self.bob)

    def send_url(self, recipient=None):
        recipient = recipient or self.bob
        return reverse(
            "send_contact_request",
            args=[self.event.pk, recipient.pk],
        )

    def test_send_request_requiring_approval(self):
        response = self.client.post(
            self.send_url(),
            {"message": "Hi, I would like to connect."},
        )

        self.assertRedirects(response, self.detail_url())
        contact_request = ContactRequest.objects.get()
        self.assertEqual(contact_request.sender, self.alice)
        self.assertEqual(contact_request.recipient, self.bob)
        self.assertEqual(contact_request.event, self.event)
        self.assertEqual(contact_request.message, "Hi, I would like to connect.")
        self.assertEqual(contact_request.status, ContactRequest.Status.PENDING)

    def test_open_contact_permission_accepts_request_automatically(self):
        self.bob.contact_permission = ContactPermission.OPEN
        self.bob.save()

        self.client.post(self.send_url(), {"message": "Hello"})

        contact_request = ContactRequest.objects.get()
        self.assertEqual(contact_request.status, ContactRequest.Status.ACCEPTED)

    def test_empty_optional_message_is_allowed(self):
        response = self.client.post(self.send_url(), {})

        self.assertRedirects(response, self.detail_url())
        contact_request = ContactRequest.objects.get()
        self.assertEqual(contact_request.message, "")

    def test_cannot_send_request_to_self(self):
        self.alice.share_profile_with_attendees = True
        self.alice.save()

        response = self.client.post(
            self.send_url(self.alice),
            {"message": "Hi"},
        )

        self.assertRedirects(response, self.detail_url())
        self.assertEqual(ContactRequest.objects.count(), 0)

    def test_sender_must_be_interested_in_event(self):
        EventInterest.objects.filter(event=self.event, user=self.alice).delete()

        response = self.client.post(self.send_url(), {"message": "Hello"})

        self.assertRedirects(response, self.detail_url())
        self.assertEqual(ContactRequest.objects.count(), 0)

    def test_recipient_must_share_profile(self):
        self.bob.share_profile_with_attendees = False
        self.bob.save()

        response = self.client.post(self.send_url(), {"message": "Hello"})

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 404)
        self.assertEqual(ContactRequest.objects.count(), 0)

    def test_recipient_must_be_interested_in_same_event(self):
        EventInterest.objects.filter(event=self.event, user=self.bob).delete()

        response = self.client.post(self.send_url(), {"message": "Hello"})

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 404)
        self.assertEqual(ContactRequest.objects.count(), 0)

    def test_duplicate_pending_request_is_not_created(self):
        ContactRequest.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            sender=self.alice,
            recipient=self.bob,
            message="First request",
        )

        response = self.client.post(self.send_url(), {"message": "Second request"})

        self.assertRedirects(response, self.detail_url())
        self.assertEqual(ContactRequest.objects.count(), 1)

    def test_duplicate_accepted_request_is_not_created(self):
        ContactRequest.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            sender=self.alice,
            recipient=self.bob,
            status=ContactRequest.Status.ACCEPTED,
        )

        response = self.client.post(self.send_url(), {"message": "Again"})

        self.assertRedirects(response, self.detail_url())
        self.assertEqual(ContactRequest.objects.count(), 1)

    def test_declined_request_can_be_sent_again(self):
        ContactRequest.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            sender=self.alice,
            recipient=self.bob,
            status=ContactRequest.Status.DECLINED,
        )

        response = self.client.post(self.send_url(), {"message": "Retry"})

        self.assertRedirects(response, self.detail_url())
        self.assertEqual(ContactRequest.objects.count(), 2)
        self.assertTrue(
            ContactRequest.objects.filter(
                sender=self.alice,
                recipient=self.bob,
                status=ContactRequest.Status.PENDING,
            ).exists()
        )

    def test_closed_event_rejects_contact_request(self):
        self.event.status = Event.Status.DONE
        self.event.save()

        response = self.client.post(self.send_url(), {"message": "Hello"})

        self.assertRedirects(response, self.detail_url())
        self.assertEqual(ContactRequest.objects.count(), 0)

    def test_accept_pending_request(self):
        contact_request = ContactRequest.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            sender=self.bob,
            recipient=self.alice,
        )

        response = self.client.post(
            reverse(
                "respond_to_contact_request",
                args=[contact_request.pk, "accept"],
            )
        )

        self.assertRedirects(response, self.detail_url())
        contact_request.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(contact_request.status, ContactRequest.Status.ACCEPTED)

    def test_decline_pending_request(self):
        contact_request = ContactRequest.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            sender=self.bob,
            recipient=self.alice,
        )

        response = self.client.post(
            reverse(
                "respond_to_contact_request",
                args=[contact_request.pk, "decline"],
            )
        )

        self.assertRedirects(response, self.detail_url())
        contact_request.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(contact_request.status, ContactRequest.Status.DECLINED)

    def test_invalid_action_does_not_change_request(self):
        contact_request = ContactRequest.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            sender=self.bob,
            recipient=self.alice,
        )

        response = self.client.post(
            reverse(
                "respond_to_contact_request",
                args=[contact_request.pk, "unknown"],
            )
        )

        self.assertRedirects(response, self.detail_url())
        contact_request.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(contact_request.status, ContactRequest.Status.PENDING)

    def test_another_user_cannot_respond_to_request(self):
        contact_request = ContactRequest.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            sender=self.bob,
            recipient=self.alice,
        )
        self.client.force_login(self.bob)

        response = self.client.post(
            reverse(
                "respond_to_contact_request",
                args=[contact_request.pk, "accept"],
            )
        )

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 404)
        contact_request.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(contact_request.status, ContactRequest.Status.PENDING)

    def test_already_processed_request_cannot_be_processed_again(self):
        contact_request = ContactRequest.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            sender=self.bob,
            recipient=self.alice,
            status=ContactRequest.Status.ACCEPTED,
        )

        response = self.client.post(
            reverse(
                "respond_to_contact_request",
                args=[contact_request.pk, "decline"],
            )
        )

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 404)
        contact_request.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(contact_request.status, ContactRequest.Status.ACCEPTED)



============================================================
FILE: events\tests\test_contact_notification_migration.py
============================================================
"""Exercise the historical-model backfill against the real migration graph."""

from datetime import timedelta
from importlib import import_module
from types import SimpleNamespace

from django.db import connection
from django.db.migrations.executor import MigrationExecutor
from django.test import TransactionTestCase
from django.utils import timezone


class ContactNotificationMigrationTests(TransactionTestCase):
    """The backfill preserves timestamps and existing event notifications."""

    before = [
        ("events", "0006_eventinterest_status"),
        ("accounts", "0005_user_interests"),
    ]
    after = [
        ("events", "0008_backfill_contact_notifications"),
        ("accounts", "0005_user_interests"),
    ]

    def setUp(self):
        self.addCleanup(self.restore_latest)
        executor = MigrationExecutor(connection)
        executor.migrate(self.before)
        self.old_apps = executor.loader.project_state(self.before).apps

    def restore_latest(self):
        executor = MigrationExecutor(connection)
        executor.migrate(executor.loader.graph.leaf_nodes())

    def test_backfill_is_pending_only_idempotent_and_reversible(self):
        user_model = self.old_apps.get_model("accounts", "User")
        event_model = self.old_apps.get_model("events", "Event")
        request_model = self.old_apps.get_model("events", "ContactRequest")
        notification_model = self.old_apps.get_model("events", "Notification")
        sender = user_model.objects.create(
            username="sender",
            email="sender@example.com",
            display_name="Park Pals",
            interests=["fitness"],
        )
        recipient = user_model.objects.create(
            username="recipient", email="recipient@example.com"
        )
        event = event_model.objects.create(
            guid="migration", title="Migration event", content_hash="v" * 64
        )
        attendance = self.old_apps.get_model("events", "EventInterest").objects.create(
            event=event, user=recipient, status="want_to_attend"
        )
        original = notification_model.objects.create(
            recipient=recipient, event=event, subject="Original event update", body=""
        )
        requests = [
            request_model.objects.create(
                sender=sender,
                recipient=recipient,
                event=event,
                status=status,
                message="Hello",
            )
            for status in ("pending", "accepted", "declined")
        ]
        timestamp = timezone.now() - timedelta(days=3)
        request_model.objects.filter(pk=requests[0].pk).update(created_at=timestamp)
        executor = MigrationExecutor(connection)
        executor.migrate(self.after)
        apps = executor.loader.project_state(self.after).apps
        notification_model = apps.get_model("events", "Notification")
        self.assertEqual(
            apps.get_model("events", "EventInterest")
            .objects.get(pk=attendance.pk)
            .status,
            "want_to_attend",
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            apps.get_model("accounts", "User").objects.get(pk=sender.pk).interests,
            ["fitness"],
        )
        self.assertEqual(notification_model.objects.count(), 2)
        notification = notification_model.objects.get(contact_request_id=requests[0].pk)
        self.assertEqual(notification.recipient_id, recipient.pk)
        self.assertEqual(notification.event_id, event.pk)
        self.assertEqual(notification.created_at, timestamp)
        self.assertEqual(notification.subject, "Contact request from Park Pals")
        self.assertEqual(notification.body, "Hello")
        self.assertIsNone(notification.read_at)
        migration = import_module(
            "events.migrations.0008_backfill_contact_notifications"
        )
        editor = SimpleNamespace(connection=connection)
        migration.backfill_contact_notifications(apps, editor)
        self.assertEqual(notification_model.objects.count(), 2)
        sender.display_name = ""
        sender.save()
        # Verify the username fallback on another historical pending request.
        request_model = apps.get_model("events", "ContactRequest")
        extra = request_model.objects.create(
            sender_id=sender.pk,
            recipient_id=recipient.pk,
            event_id=event.pk,
            status="pending",
        )
        migration.backfill_contact_notifications(apps, editor)
        self.assertEqual(
            notification_model.objects.get(contact_request_id=extra.pk).subject,
            "Contact request from sender",
        )
        MigrationExecutor(connection).migrate(self.before)
        self.assertEqual(
            list(
                self.old_apps.get_model("events", "Notification").objects.values_list(
                    "pk", flat=True
                )
            ),
            [original.pk],
        )



============================================================
FILE: events\tests\test_contact_notifications.py
