"""Contact request delivery, resolution, ordering, and transaction tests."""

from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from datetime import timedelta
from threading import Barrier
from unittest.mock import patch

from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.core import mail
from django.db import close_old_connections, connections
from django.http import Http404
from django.test import Client, RequestFactory, TransactionTestCase
from django.urls import reverse
from django.utils import timezone

from accounts.models import ContactPermission
from events.community_views import respond_to_contact_request, send_contact_request
from events.models import ContactRequest, Event, EventInterest, Notification
from events.notifications import notify_contact_request
from events.tests.test_notifications import NotifyTestCase


class ContactNotificationTests(NotifyTestCase):
    """Incoming requests remain new until the recipient resolves them."""

    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        self.fan.share_profile_with_attendees = True
        self.fan.save()
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        self.send_url = reverse(
            "send_contact_request", args=[self.event.pk, self.fan.pk]
        )

    def incoming(self, status=ContactRequest.Status.PENDING, **kwargs):
        contact_request = ContactRequest.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            sender=self.host,
            recipient=self.fan,
            status=status,
            **kwargs,
        )
        return contact_request, notify_contact_request(contact_request)

    def response_url(self, contact_request, action="accept"):
        return reverse("respond_to_contact_request", args=[contact_request.pk, action])

    def test_send_creates_recipient_only_notification_without_email(self):
        self.host.display_name = "Park Pals"
        self.host.save()
        response = self.client.post(self.send_url, {"message": "Hello!"})
        self.assertRedirects(response, self.detail_url)
        notification = Notification.objects.get()
        self.assertEqual(notification.recipient, self.fan)
        self.assertEqual(notification.event, self.event)
        self.assertEqual(notification.contact_request, ContactRequest.objects.get())
        self.assertEqual(notification.subject, "Contact request from Park Pals")
        self.assertEqual(notification.body, "Hello!")
        self.assertIsNone(notification.read_at)
        self.assertFalse(self.host.notifications.exists())
        self.assertEqual(mail.outbox, [])

    def test_optional_message_and_idempotent_helper(self):
        self.client.post(self.send_url)
        contact_request = ContactRequest.objects.get()
        first = Notification.objects.get()
        again = notify_contact_request(contact_request)
        self.assertEqual(first.pk, again.pk)
        self.assertEqual(first.body, "")
        self.assertEqual(Notification.objects.count(), 1)

    def test_open_contact_is_read_introduction(self):
        self.fan.contact_permission = ContactPermission.OPEN
        self.fan.save()
        self.client.post(self.send_url, {"message": "Hello!"})
        notification = Notification.objects.get()
        self.assertEqual(notification.subject, "Introduction from host")
        self.assertIsNotNone(notification.read_at)
        self.assertEqual(notification.contact_request.status, "accepted")
        self.client.force_login(self.fan)
        response = self.client.get(reverse("notifications"))
        self.assertContains(response, "Status: Accepted")
        self.assertContains(response, f'href="/messages/{self.host.pk}/"')
        self.assertNotContains(response, "New</span>")
        self.assertNotContains(response, ">Accept</button>")
        self.assertNotContains(response, ">Decline</button>")
        self.assertFalse(self.host.notifications.exists())
        self.assertEqual(mail.outbox, [])

    def test_duplicate_submissions_and_retry_after_decline(self):
        self.client.post(self.send_url)
        self.client.post(self.send_url)
        self.assertEqual(ContactRequest.objects.count(), 1)
        self.assertEqual(Notification.objects.count(), 1)
        contact_request = ContactRequest.objects.get()
        self.client.force_login(self.fan)
        self.client.post(self.response_url(contact_request, "decline"))
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        self.client.post(self.send_url)
        self.assertEqual(ContactRequest.objects.count(), 2)
        self.assertEqual(Notification.objects.count(), 2)
        self.assertEqual(Notification.objects.filter(read_at__isnull=True).count(), 1)
        latest = ContactRequest.objects.get(status="pending")
        self.client.force_login(self.fan)
        self.client.post(self.response_url(latest))
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        self.client.post(self.send_url)
        self.assertEqual(Notification.objects.count(), 2)

    def test_profile_link_for_each_state_and_message_only_when_accepted(self):
        self.host.display_name = "Park Pals"
        self.host.save()
        self.client.force_login(self.fan)
        profile_url = reverse("public_profile", args=[self.host.username])
        message_url = f"/messages/{self.host.pk}/"
        for status in ContactRequest.Status.values:
            with self.subTest(status=status):
                contact_request, notification = self.incoming(status=status)
                response = self.client.get(reverse("notifications"))
                self.assertContains(
                    response, f'<a href="{profile_url}">Park Pals</a>', html=True
                )
                if status == ContactRequest.Status.ACCEPTED:
                    self.assertContains(response, f'href="{message_url}"')
                    self.assertContains(response, "Message</a>")
                    self.assertNotContains(response, "<button")
                else:
                    self.assertNotContains(response, f'href="{message_url}"')
                if status == ContactRequest.Status.PENDING:
                    notification.refresh_from_db()
                    self.assertIsNone(notification.read_at)
                    self.assertContains(response, "New</span>")
                contact_request.delete()

    def test_profile_link_preserves_existing_visibility_rules(self):
        self.incoming()
        self.client.force_login(self.fan)
        profile_url = reverse("public_profile", args=[self.host.username])
        self.assertContains(
            self.client.get(reverse("notifications")), f'href="{profile_url}"'
        )
        response = self.client.get(profile_url)
        self.assertTemplateUsed(response, "accounts/profile_unavailable.html")
        self.host.share_profile_with_attendees = True
        self.host.save()
        response = self.client.get(profile_url)
        self.assertTemplateUsed(response, "accounts/public_profile.html")
        self.assertEqual(response.context["profile_user"], self.host)

    def test_message_link_appears_after_acceptance_from_either_page(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.fan)
        message_url = f"/messages/{self.host.pk}/"
        for return_to in ("notifications", ""):
            with self.subTest(return_to=return_to):
                contact_request, notification = self.incoming()
                self.assertNotContains(
                    self.client.get(reverse("notifications")), f'href="{message_url}"'
                )
                response = self.client.post(
                    self.response_url(contact_request), {"return_to": return_to}
                )
                self.assertRedirects(
                    response,
                    reverse("notifications") if return_to else self.detail_url,
                )
                self.assertContains(
                    self.client.get(reverse("notifications")), f'href="{message_url}"'
                )
                notification.refresh_from_db()
                self.assertIsNotNone(notification.read_at)
                contact_request.delete()

    def test_event_notification_does_not_show_contact_actions(self):
        Notification.objects.create(
            recipient=self.fan, event=self.event, subject="Event update", body="Details"
        )
        self.client.force_login(self.fan)
        response = self.client.get(reverse("notifications"))
        self.assertNotContains(response, 'class="notification-message"')
        self.assertNotContains(
            response, reverse("public_profile", args=[self.host.username])
        )

    def test_invalid_requests_do_not_notify(self):
        self.client.post(self.send_url, {"message": "x" * 501})
        self.client.post(
            reverse("send_contact_request", args=[self.event.pk, self.host.pk])
        )
        EventInterest.objects.filter(event=self.event, user=self.host).delete()
        self.client.post(self.send_url)
        self.assertFalse(ContactRequest.objects.exists())
        self.assertFalse(Notification.objects.exists())

    def test_nonsharing_recipient_and_closed_event_do_not_notify(self):
        self.fan.share_profile_with_attendees = False
        self.fan.save()
        self.assertEqual(self.client.post(self.send_url).status_code, 404)
        self.fan.share_profile_with_attendees = True
        self.fan.save()
        self.event.status = Event.Status.DONE
        self.event.save()
        self.client.post(self.send_url)
        self.assertFalse(Notification.objects.exists())

    def test_notification_failure_rolls_back_new_request(self):
        with patch(
            "events.community_views.notify_contact_request",
            side_effect=RuntimeError("notification failure"),
        ):
            with self.assertRaisesMessage(RuntimeError, "notification failure"):
                self.client.post(self.send_url)
        self.assertFalse(ContactRequest.objects.exists())
        self.assertFalse(Notification.objects.exists())

    def test_pending_stays_new_across_visits_and_in_badge(self):
        contact_request, notification = self.incoming(
            message="<script>alert(1)</script>"
        )
        event_notification = Notification.objects.create(
            recipient=self.fan, event=self.event, subject="Event update", body="Details"
        )
        self.client.force_login(self.fan)
        for _ in range(2):
            response = self.client.get(reverse("notifications"))
            self.assertContains(response, "Status: Pending")
            self.assertContains(response, self.response_url(contact_request))
            self.assertContains(response, self.response_url(contact_request, "decline"))
            self.assertContains(response, 'name="return_to" value="notifications"')
            self.assertContains(response, "&lt;script&gt;alert(1)&lt;/script&gt;")
        notification.refresh_from_db()
        event_notification.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertIsNone(notification.read_at)
        self.assertIsNotNone(event_notification.read_at)
        self.assertContains(response, "New</span>", count=1)
        self.assertContains(self.client.get(reverse("landing")), "1 new")
        contact_request.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(contact_request.status, "pending")

    def test_responses_from_both_pages_resolve_and_mark_read(self):
        for action in ("accept", "decline"):
            for return_to in ("notifications", "", "https://example.com"):
                with self.subTest(action=action, return_to=return_to):
                    contact_request, notification = self.incoming()
                    self.client.force_login(self.fan)
                    response = self.client.post(
                        self.response_url(contact_request, action),
                        {"return_to": return_to},
                    )
                    expected = (
                        reverse("notifications")
                        if return_to == "notifications"
                        else self.detail_url
                    )
                    self.assertRedirects(response, expected)
                    contact_request.refresh_from_db()
                    notification.refresh_from_db()
                    self.assertEqual(
                        contact_request.status,
                        "accepted" if action == "accept" else "declined",
                    )
                    self.assertIsNotNone(notification.read_at)
        page = self.client.get(reverse("notifications"))
        self.assertContains(page, "Status: Accepted")
        self.assertContains(page, "Status: Declined")
        self.assertNotContains(page, "New</span>")
        self.assertNotContains(page, "<button")
        self.assertFalse(self.host.notifications.exists())
        self.assertNotContains(self.client.get(reverse("landing")), "1 new")
        self.assertEqual(mail.outbox, [])

    def test_response_permissions_methods_csrf_and_replay(self):
        contact_request, notification = self.incoming()
        url = self.response_url(contact_request)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.post(url).status_code, 404)
        self.client.force_login(self.fan)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get(url).status_code, 405)
        csrf_client = Client(enforce_csrf_checks=True)
        csrf_client.force_login(self.fan)
        self.assertEqual(csrf_client.post(url).status_code, 403)
        response = self.client.post(
            self.response_url(contact_request, "unknown"),
            {"return_to": "notifications"},
        )
        self.assertRedirects(response, reverse("notifications"))
        contact_request.refresh_from_db()
        notification.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(contact_request.status, "pending")
        self.assertIsNone(notification.read_at)
        self.client.post(url)
        notification.refresh_from_db()
        resolved_at = notification.read_at
        self.assertEqual(self.client.post(url).status_code, 404)
        notification.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(notification.read_at, resolved_at)
        self.client.logout()
        self.assertEqual(self.client.post(url).status_code, 302)

    def test_resolution_failure_rolls_back_status_and_read_state(self):
        contact_request, notification = self.incoming()
        self.client.force_login(self.fan)
        with patch(
            "django.db.models.query.QuerySet.update",
            side_effect=RuntimeError("read state failure"),
        ):
            with self.assertRaisesMessage(RuntimeError, "read state failure"):
                self.client.post(self.response_url(contact_request))
        contact_request.refresh_from_db()
        notification.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(contact_request.status, "pending")
        self.assertIsNone(notification.read_at)

    def test_recipient_isolation(self):
        self.incoming(message="Private introduction")
        self.client.force_login(self.bystander)
        page = self.client.get(reverse("notifications"))
        self.assertNotContains(page, "Private introduction")
        self.assertContains(page, "No notifications yet.")

    def test_requests_first_with_stable_ties_across_pages(self):
        timestamp = timezone.now() - timedelta(days=2)
        request_notifications = [
            self.incoming(status="pending" if i % 2 else "accepted")[1]
            for i in range(21)
        ]
        Notification.objects.filter(
            pk__in=[n.pk for n in request_notifications]
        ).update(created_at=timestamp)
        event_notifications = [
            Notification.objects.create(
                recipient=self.fan, event=self.event, subject=f"Update {i}", body=""
            )
            for i in range(2)
        ]
        Notification.objects.filter(pk__in=[n.pk for n in event_notifications]).update(
            created_at=timezone.now()
        )
        # A newer request sorts ahead of equal-time requests in its own group.
        newest = request_notifications[0]
        Notification.objects.filter(pk=newest.pk).update(
            created_at=timestamp + timedelta(hours=1)
        )
        expected = (
            [newest.pk]
            + sorted([n.pk for n in request_notifications[1:]], reverse=True)
            + sorted([n.pk for n in event_notifications], reverse=True)
        )
        self.client.force_login(self.fan)
        pages = [
            self.client.get(reverse("notifications")),
            self.client.get(reverse("notifications"), {"page": 2}),
        ]
        actual = [n.pk for page in pages for n in page.context["notifications"]]
        self.assertEqual(actual, expected)
        self.assertEqual(len(pages[0].context["notifications"]), 20)
        self.assertEqual(len(pages[1].context["notifications"]), 3)

    def test_undisplayed_event_updates_stay_unread(self):
        for _ in range(20):
            self.incoming()
        event_notification = Notification.objects.create(
            recipient=self.fan, event=self.event, subject="Event update", body=""
        )
        self.client.force_login(self.fan)
        self.client.get(reverse("notifications"))
        event_notification.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertIsNone(event_notification.read_at)
        self.assertEqual(
            self.fan.notifications.filter(read_at__isnull=True).count(), 21
        )
        self.client.get(reverse("notifications"), {"page": 2})
        event_notification.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertIsNotNone(event_notification.read_at)
        self.assertEqual(
            self.fan.notifications.filter(read_at__isnull=True).count(), 20
        )

    def test_deleting_request_removes_only_its_notification(self):
        contact_request, notification = self.incoming()
        event_notification = Notification.objects.create(
            recipient=self.fan, event=self.event, subject="Event update", body=""
        )
        contact_request.delete()
        self.assertFalse(Notification.objects.filter(pk=notification.pk).exists())
        self.assertTrue(Notification.objects.filter(pk=event_notification.pk).exists())


class ConcurrentContactNotificationTests(TransactionTestCase):
    """Use independent database connections to exercise actual row locks."""

    def setUp(self):
        user_model = get_user_model()
        self.sender = user_model.objects.create_user(
            username="sender", email="sender@example.com"
        )
        self.recipient = user_model.objects.create_user(
            username="recipient",
            email="recipient@example.com",
            share_profile_with_attendees=True,
        )
        self.event = Event.objects.create(
            guid="concurrent", title="Concurrent requests", content_hash="v" * 64
        )
        for user in (self.sender, self.recipient):
            EventInterest.objects.create(event=self.event, user=user)

    def run_concurrently(self, view, submissions):
        barrier = Barrier(2)

        def submit(user_id, args):
            close_old_connections()
            try:
                request = RequestFactory().post("/")
                request.user = get_user_model().objects.get(pk=user_id)
                barrier.wait(timeout=10)
                try:
                    return view(request, *args).status_code
                except Http404:
                    return 404
            finally:
                connections["default"].close()

        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as executor:
            futures = [
                executor.submit(submit, user_id, args) for user_id, args in submissions
            ]
            return [future.result(timeout=30) for future in futures]

    def test_simultaneous_sends_and_responses(self):
        results = self.run_concurrently(
            send_contact_request,
            [(self.sender.pk, (self.event.pk, self.recipient.pk))] * 2,
        )
        self.assertEqual(results, [302, 302])
        self.assertEqual(ContactRequest.objects.count(), 1)
        self.assertEqual(Notification.objects.count(), 1)
        contact_request = ContactRequest.objects.get()
        results = self.run_concurrently(
            respond_to_contact_request,
            [
                (self.recipient.pk, (contact_request.pk, "accept")),
                (self.recipient.pk, (contact_request.pk, "decline")),
            ],
        )
        self.assertEqual(sorted(results), [302, 404])
        contact_request.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertIn(contact_request.status, ("accepted", "declined"))
        self.assertIsNotNone(Notification.objects.get().read_at)

    def test_reciprocal_requests_do_not_deadlock(self):
        self.sender.share_profile_with_attendees = True
        self.sender.save()
        delivery_barrier = Barrier(2)

        def deliver(contact_request):
            notification = notify_contact_request(contact_request)
            # Both sender locks remain held until the transactions commit.
            delivery_barrier.wait(timeout=10)
            return notification

        with patch(
            "events.community_views.notify_contact_request", side_effect=deliver
        ):
            results = self.run_concurrently(
                send_contact_request,
                [
                    (self.sender.pk, (self.event.pk, self.recipient.pk)),
                    (self.recipient.pk, (self.event.pk, self.sender.pk)),
                ],
            )
        self.assertEqual(results, [302, 302])
        self.assertEqual(ContactRequest.objects.count(), 2)
        self.assertEqual(Notification.objects.filter(read_at__isnull=True).count(), 2)



============================================================
FILE: events\tests\test_editing.py
============================================================
"""Tests for hosts editing the events they posted."""

from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.test import TestCase
from django.urls import reverse
from django.utils import timezone

from events.models import Event

NYC = ZoneInfo("America/New_York")


def as_input(moment):
    """Format a datetime the way the form's calendar input submits it."""
    return timezone.localtime(moment, NYC).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M")


def start_of_today():
    """Return midnight at the start of today in New York."""
    return timezone.localtime(timezone.now(), NYC).replace(
        hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0
    )


class EditTestCase(TestCase):
    """Shared users and a hosted event for the edit tests."""

    @classmethod
    def setUpTestData(cls):
        User = get_user_model()
        cls.host = User.objects.create_user(
            username="host", email="host@example.com", password="pass12345"
        )
        cls.other = User.objects.create_user(
            username="other", email="other@example.com", password="pass12345"
        )

    def setUp(self):
        self.event = self.hosted("yoga")
        self.url = reverse("event_edit", args=[self.event.pk])

    def hosted(self, guid, **fields):
        """Create an event posted by the host, upcoming by default."""
        defaults = {
            "title": "Sunset Yoga by the River",
            "description": "Bring a mat.",
            "start_time": datetime(2099, 1, 15, 18, 30, tzinfo=NYC),
            "end_time": datetime(2099, 1, 15, 19, 45, tzinfo=NYC),
            "location": "Pier 1, Brooklyn Bridge Park",
            "borough": Event.Borough.BROOKLYN,
            "categories": "Fitness | Yoga & Pilates",
            "registration_url": "https://example.org/tickets",
        }
        defaults.update(fields)
        return Event.objects.create(
            guid=Event.HOSTED_GUID_PREFIX + guid,
            host=self.host,
            content_hash="",
            **defaults,
        )

    def edit_data(self, event=None, **overrides):
        """Return the event's current details as a form submission."""
        event = event or self.event
        data = {
            "title": event.title,
            "description": event.description,
            "start_time": as_input(event.start_time),
            "end_time": as_input(event.end_time) if event.end_time else "",
            "location": event.location,
            "borough": event.borough,
            "categories": ["fitness", "yoga"],
            "image_url": event.image_url,
            "registration_url": event.registration_url,
        }
        data.update(overrides)
        return data


class EditButtonTests(EditTestCase):
    """Only the host sees an "Edit event" button, and only before it ends."""

    def detail(self, event):
        """Render an event's detail page."""
        return self.client.get(reverse("event_detail", args=[event.pk]))

    def test_host_sees_the_edit_button(self):
        """The host's own upcoming event links to its edit page."""
        self.client.force_login(self.host)

        self.assertContains(
            self.detail(self.event), f'href="{self.url}">Edit event</a>'
        )

    def test_other_visitors_do_not_see_the_edit_button(self):
        """Other users and logged-out visitors get no edit link."""
        self.assertNotContains(self.detail(self.event), "Edit event")

        self.client.force_login(self.other)
        self.assertNotContains(self.detail(self.event), "Edit event")

    def test_ended_and_feed_events_have_no_edit_button(self):
        """Hosts cannot edit events that ended, and feed events have no host."""
        ended = self.hosted(
            "ended",
            start_time=timezone.now() - timedelta(hours=3),
            end_time=timezone.now() - timedelta(hours=1),
        )
        feed = Event.objects.create(guid="123", title="Park Tour", content_hash="")
        self.client.force_login(self.host)

        self.assertNotContains(self.detail(ended), "Edit event")
        self.assertNotContains(self.detail(feed), "Edit event")


class EventUpdateViewTests(EditTestCase):
    """Hosts edit their events through the same validated form."""

    def test_logged_out_visitors_are_sent_to_login(self):
        """Editing requires an account."""
        response = self.client.get(self.url)

        self.assertRedirects(response, f"{reverse('login')}?next={self.url}")

    def test_only_the_host_can_open_or_submit_the_edit_form(self):
        """Other users get "not found" and cannot change the event."""
        self.client.force_login(self.other)

        self.assertEqual(self.client.get(self.url).status_code, 404)
        response = self.client.post(self.url, self.edit_data(title="Hijacked"))

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 404)
        self.event.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(self.event.title, "Sunset Yoga by the River")

    def test_feed_events_cannot_be_edited(self):
        """NYC Parks events have no host, so nobody can edit them."""
        feed = Event.objects.create(guid="123", title="Park Tour", content_hash="")
        self.client.force_login(self.host)

        response = self.client.get(reverse("event_edit", args=[feed.pk]))

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 404)

    def test_ended_events_cannot_be_edited(self):
        """Events past their end, done, or from an earlier day are closed."""
        now = timezone.now()
        closed = [
            self.hosted(
                "past-end",
                start_time=now - timedelta(hours=3),
                end_time=now - timedelta(minutes=1),
            ),
            self.hosted("done", status=Event.Status.DONE),
            self.hosted(
                "yesterday",
                start_time=start_of_today() - timedelta(hours=2),
                end_time=None,
            ),
        ]
        self.client.force_login(self.host)

        for event in closed:
            with self.subTest(event=event.guid):
                url = reverse("event_edit", args=[event.pk])
                self.assertEqual(self.client.get(url).status_code, 404)
                response = self.client.post(url, self.edit_data(event, title="Late"))
                self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 404)

    def test_form_opens_with_the_current_details(self):
        """Every saved detail is filled in, with times in New York time."""
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        response = self.client.get(self.url)

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "<h1>Edit event</h1>")
        self.assertContains(response, 'value="Sunset Yoga by the River"')
        self.assertContains(response, "Bring a mat.</textarea>")
        self.assertContains(response, 'name="start_time" value="2099-01-15T18:30"')
        self.assertContains(response, 'name="end_time" value="2099-01-15T19:45"')
        self.assertContains(response, '<option value="brooklyn" selected>')
        self.assertContains(response, 'value="fitness" id="id_categories_0" checked')
        self.assertContains(response, 'value="yoga" id="id_categories_1" checked')
        self.assertNotContains(response, 'value="art" id="id_categories_3" checked')
        self.assertContains(response, 'value="https://example.org/tickets"')
        self.assertContains(response, "Save changes")
        self.assertContains(
            response, f'href="{reverse("event_detail", args=[self.event.pk])}"'
        )

    def test_saving_updates_the_event_everywhere(self):
        """Changes appear on the detail page, in search, and in filters."""
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        response = self.client.post(
            self.url,
            self.edit_data(
                title="Moonlight Painting",
                start_time="2099-02-01T20:00",
                end_time="2099-02-01T22:00",
                borough="queens",
                categories=["art"],
            ),
        )

        detail_url = reverse("event_detail", args=[self.event.pk])
        self.assertRedirects(response, detail_url, fetch_redirect_response=False)
        self.event.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(self.event.title, "Moonlight Painting")
        self.assertEqual(self.event.start_time, datetime(2099, 2, 1, 20, tzinfo=NYC))
        self.assertEqual(self.event.borough, Event.Borough.QUEENS)
        self.assertEqual(self.event.categories, "Arts & Crafts")
        self.assertEqual(self.event.host, self.host)
        self.assertEqual(self.event.guid, Event.HOSTED_GUID_PREFIX + "yoga")

        detail = self.client.get(detail_url)
        self.assertContains(detail, "Event updated.")
        self.assertContains(detail, "Moonlight Painting")

        search = reverse("search")
        new_title = self.client.get(search, {"q": "moonlight"})
        old_title = self.client.get(search, {"q": "sunset"})
        self.assertEqual(list(new_title.context["events"]), [self.event])
        self.assertEqual(list(old_title.context["events"]), [])
        filters = {"borough": "queens", "category": "art", "date_from": "2099-02-01"}
        self.assertEqual(
            list(self.client.get(search, filters).context["events"]), [self.event]
        )
        brooklyn = self.client.get(search, {"borough": "brooklyn"})
        self.assertNotIn(self.event, brooklyn.context["events"])

    def test_invalid_changes_report_errors_and_keep_the_event(self):
        """The edit form uses the same validation as the create form."""
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        response = self.client.post(
            self.url,
            self.edit_data(title="", end_time="2099-01-15T17:00", categories=[]),
        )

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(
            set(response.context["form"].errors), {"title", "end_time", "categories"}
        )
        self.assertContains(response, "The end time must be after the start time.")
        self.event.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(self.event.title, "Sunset Yoga by the River")

    def test_events_under_way_can_still_be_corrected(self):
        """An unchanged past start time is fine; moving it into the past is not."""
        now = timezone.now()
        ongoing = self.hosted(
            "ongoing",
            start_time=(now - timedelta(hours=1)).replace(second=0, microsecond=0),
            end_time=now + timedelta(hours=2),
        )
        url = reverse("event_edit", args=[ongoing.pk])
        self.client.force_login(self.host)

        response = self.client.post(
            url, self.edit_data(ongoing, description="Meet by the flagpole.")
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 302)
        ongoing.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(ongoing.description, "Meet by the flagpole.")

        earlier = as_input(ongoing.start_time - timedelta(hours=1))
        response = self.client.post(url, self.edit_data(ongoing, start_time=earlier))
        self.assertContains(response, "Choose a start time in the future.")


class HasEndedTests(EditTestCase):
    """Events know when they are over, using the same rule as the sync."""

    def test_has_ended(self):
        """End times, the done status, and start days decide it."""
        now = timezone.now()
        cases = {
            "future": ({}, False),
            "past-end": (
                {
                    "start_time": now - timedelta(hours=2),
                    "end_time": now - timedelta(1),
                },
                True,
            ),
            "done": ({"status": Event.Status.DONE}, True),
            "today-no-end": ({"start_time": start_of_today(), "end_time": None}, False),
            "yesterday-no-end": (
                {"start_time": start_of_today() - timedelta(hours=1), "end_time": None},
                True,
            ),
            "undated": ({"start_time": None, "end_time": None}, False),
        }
        for guid, (fields, expected) in cases.items():
            with self.subTest(guid=guid):
                self.assertEqual(self.hosted(guid, **fields).has_ended, expected)



============================================================
FILE: events\tests\test_filters.py
============================================================
"""Tests for filtering events by date range, borough, and category."""

from datetime import date, datetime
from unittest.mock import patch
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

from django.test import SimpleTestCase, TestCase
from django.urls import reverse

from events.filters import (
    CATEGORY_GROUPS,
    EventFilterForm,
    filter_events,
    filter_params,
    nyc_today,
    preset_range,
)
from events.models import Event
from events.views import SEARCH_PAGE_SIZE

NYC = ZoneInfo("America/New_York")


def make_event(guid, **fields):
    """Create an upcoming event with optional filterable fields."""
    fields.setdefault("title", "Community gathering")
    fields.setdefault("content_hash", "f" * 64)
    return Event.objects.create(guid=guid, **fields)


def at(day, hour=9):
    """Return an NYC-local datetime on ``day`` of January 2099."""
    return datetime(2099, 1, day, hour, tzinfo=NYC)


class PresetRangeTests(SimpleTestCase):
    """Quick date buttons turn into inclusive date ranges."""

    def test_today_is_a_single_day(self):
        """ "Today" starts and ends on the current date."""
        today = date(2026, 10, 14)
        self.assertEqual(preset_range("today", today), (today, today))

    def test_weekend_before_and_during_the_weekend(self):
        """The weekend is the coming Saturday and Sunday, minus days gone by."""
        cases = {
            date(2026, 10, 12): (date(2026, 10, 17), date(2026, 10, 18)),
            date(2026, 10, 16): (date(2026, 10, 17), date(2026, 10, 18)),
            date(2026, 10, 17): (date(2026, 10, 17), date(2026, 10, 18)),
            date(2026, 10, 18): (date(2026, 10, 18), date(2026, 10, 18)),
        }
        for today, expected in cases.items():
            with self.subTest(today=today):
                self.assertEqual(preset_range("weekend", today), expected)

    def test_month_runs_to_the_last_day(self):
        """ "This month" ends on the month's last day, including in December."""
        cases = {
            date(2026, 10, 10): date(2026, 10, 31),
            date(2028, 2, 3): date(2028, 2, 29),
            date(2026, 12, 31): date(2026, 12, 31),
        }
        for today, end in cases.items():
            with self.subTest(today=today):
                self.assertEqual(preset_range("month", today), (today, end))

    def test_today_uses_new_york_time(self):
        """Late evening in New York is still "today" even after UTC midnight."""
        utc_after_midnight = datetime(2026, 10, 11, 2, 30, tzinfo=ZoneInfo("UTC"))
        with patch("events.filters.timezone.now", return_value=utc_after_midnight):
            self.assertEqual(nyc_today(), date(2026, 10, 10))


class EventFilterFormTests(SimpleTestCase):
    """The filter form validates input and normalises quick date buttons."""

    def test_quick_dates_replace_typed_dates(self):
        """A quick date button wins over stale dates already in the inputs."""
        form = EventFilterForm(
            {"when": "today", "date_from": "2026-01-01", "date_to": "2026-01-02"}
        )
        with patch("events.filters.nyc_today", return_value=date(2026, 10, 10)):
            self.assertTrue(form.is_valid())

        self.assertEqual(
            filter_params(form.cleaned_data),
            [("date_from", "2026-10-10"), ("date_to", "2026-10-10")],
        )

    def test_reversed_dates_are_rejected(self):
        """An end date before the start date reports a readable error."""
        form = EventFilterForm({"date_from": "2026-10-20", "date_to": "2026-10-10"})

        self.assertFalse(form.is_valid())
        self.assertIn("on or after the start", form.errors["date_to"][0])

    def test_unknown_choices_are_rejected(self):
        """Only listed boroughs, categories, and quick dates are accepted."""
        form = EventFilterForm(
            {"borough": ["atlantis"], "category": ["fitness"], "when": "decade"}
        )

        self.assertFalse(form.is_valid())
        self.assertEqual(set(form.errors), {"borough", "when"})
        self.assertEqual(form.cleaned_data["category"], ["fitness"])

    def test_params_keep_selection_order_and_skip_empty_filters(self):
        """Only active filters become query-string pairs."""
        form = EventFilterForm(
            {"borough": ["queens", "brooklyn"], "category": ["yoga"]}
        )
        self.assertTrue(form.is_valid())

        self.assertEqual(
            filter_params(form.cleaned_data),
            [("borough", "queens"), ("borough", "brooklyn"), ("category", "yoga")],
        )


class FilterEventsTests(TestCase):
    """Filters narrow querysets and combine with each other."""

    def filtered(self, **data):
        """Return the events matching the submitted filter data."""
        form = EventFilterForm(data)
        self.assertTrue(form.is_valid(), form.errors)
        return filter_events(Event.objects.all(), form.cleaned_data)

    def test_date_range_keeps_events_overlapping_the_chosen_days(self):
        """Events starting, ending, or running through the range all match."""
        before = make_event("before", start_time=at(9), end_time=at(9, 10))
        inside = make_event("inside", start_time=at(10), end_time=at(10, 10))
        ongoing = make_event("ongoing", start_time=at(8), end_time=at(12))
        open_ended = make_event("open-ended", start_time=at(11))
        late = make_event("late", start_time=at(11, 23), end_time=at(12, 1))
        after = make_event("after", start_time=at(12), end_time=at(12, 10))
        undated = make_event("undated")

        matches = self.filtered(date_from="2099-01-10", date_to="2099-01-11")

        self.assertCountEqual(matches, [inside, ongoing, open_ended, late])
        for event in (before, after, undated):
            self.assertNotIn(event, matches)

    def test_one_sided_date_ranges(self):
        """Only a start date or only an end date leaves the other side open."""
        early = make_event("early", start_time=at(5), end_time=at(5, 10))
        later = make_event("later", start_time=at(20), end_time=at(20, 10))

        self.assertEqual(list(self.filtered(date_from="2099-01-10")), [later])
        self.assertEqual(list(self.filtered(date_to="2099-01-10")), [early])

    def test_boroughs_come_from_park_id_prefixes(self):
        """Park IDs decide the borough, case-insensitively, and any may match."""
        brooklyn = make_event("bk", park_ids="B073")
        manhattan = make_event("mn", park_ids="m391")
        make_event("si", park_ids="R062")
        make_event("no-park")

        self.assertEqual(list(self.filtered(borough=["brooklyn"])), [brooklyn])
        self.assertCountEqual(
            self.filtered(borough=["brooklyn", "manhattan"]), [brooklyn, manhattan]
        )

    def test_categories_match_whole_feed_names_only(self):
        """Group names map to exact feed categories wherever they appear."""
        only = make_event("only", categories="Yoga & Pilates Classes")
        first = make_event("first", categories="Yoga & Pilates Classes | Fitness")
        middle = make_event("middle", categories="Art | Yoga & Pilates Classes | Games")
        last = make_event("last", categories="Games | Yoga & Pilates Classes")
        make_event("partial", categories="Chair Yoga & Pilates Classes Online")
        make_event("other", categories="Fitness | Games")

        self.assertCountEqual(
            self.filtered(category=["yoga"]), [only, first, middle, last]
        )

    def test_category_groups_include_related_feed_names(self):
        """Choosing "Arts & Crafts" finds every art-related feed category."""
        painting = make_event("painting", categories="Arts & Crafts | Games")
        theater = make_event("theater", categories="Theater")
        make_event("party", categories="Party Time")

        self.assertCountEqual(self.filtered(category=["art"]), [painting, theater])

    def test_every_category_group_has_feed_names(self):
        """No category button can silently match nothing by construction."""
        for slug, label, names in CATEGORY_GROUPS:
            with self.subTest(slug=slug):
                self.assertTrue(label)
                self.assertTrue(names)

    def test_different_filters_must_all_match(self):
        """Dates, boroughs, and categories combine with AND."""
        match = make_event(
            "match",
            park_ids="B001",
            categories="Fitness",
            start_time=at(10),
            end_time=at(10, 10),
        )
        make_event("wrong-borough", park_ids="Q001", categories="Fitness")
        make_event("wrong-category", park_ids="B002", categories="Games")
        make_event(
            "wrong-day",
            park_ids="B003",
            categories="Fitness",
            start_time=at(20),
            end_time=at(20, 10),
        )

        matches = self.filtered(
            date_from="2099-01-10",
            date_to="2099-01-10",
            borough=["brooklyn"],
            category=["fitness"],
        )

        self.assertEqual(list(matches), [match])


class FilteredSearchViewTests(TestCase):
    """Visitors apply filters from the search box on the home and search pages."""

    @classmethod
    def setUpTestData(cls):
        cls.brooklyn_yoga = make_event(
            "bk-yoga",
            title="Morning Yoga",
            park_ids="B073",
            categories="Yoga & Pilates Classes | Fitness",
            start_time=at(15),
            end_time=at(15, 10),
        )
        cls.queens_art = make_event(
            "qn-art",
            title="Watercolor Workshop",
            park_ids="Q001",
            categories="Arts & Crafts",
            start_time=at(16),
            end_time=at(16, 11),
        )
        cls.brooklyn_art = make_event(
            "bk-art",
            title="Mural Painting",
            park_ids="B010",
            categories="Art",
            start_time=at(14),
            end_time=at(14, 11),
        )
        cls.done = make_event(
            "done",
            title="Past Brooklyn Yoga",
            park_ids="B073",
            categories="Yoga & Pilates Classes",
            status=Event.Status.DONE,
        )

    def get(self, **params):
        """Request the search page with the given query parameters."""
        return self.client.get(reverse("search"), params)

    def test_home_page_shows_the_filter_bar_under_the_search_box(self):
        """Every visitor sees date, borough, and category filters on the home page."""
        response = self.client.get(reverse("landing"))
        content = response.content.decode()

        self.assertLess(content.index('name="q"'), content.index('class="filter-bar"'))
        self.assertContains(response, 'name="when" value="weekend"')
        self.assertContains(response, 'name="when" value="month"')
        self.assertContains(response, 'type="date" name="date_from"')
        self.assertContains(response, 'type="date" name="date_to"')
        self.assertContains(response, 'name="borough" value="brooklyn"')
        self.assertContains(response, 'name="category" value="yoga"')
        self.assertNotContains(response, "Clear filters")

    def test_filters_alone_list_upcoming_matches_soonest_first(self):
        """No search term is needed; done events never appear."""
        response = self.get(borough="brooklyn")

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "Filtered upcoming events")
        self.assertEqual(
            list(response.context["events"]), [self.brooklyn_art, self.brooklyn_yoga]
        )
        self.assertNotContains(response, self.done.title)

    def test_filters_combine_with_a_search_term(self):
        """Search results are narrowed by every selected filter."""
        response = self.get(q="painting OR yoga", borough="brooklyn", category="art")

        self.assertEqual(list(response.context["events"]), [self.brooklyn_art])

    def test_multiple_choices_and_dates_all_apply(self):
        """Several categories match any of them, within the chosen dates."""
        response = self.client.get(
            reverse("search"),
            {
                "category": ["yoga", "art"],
                "date_from": "2099-01-15",
                "date_to": "2099-01-16",
            },
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            list(response.context["events"]), [self.brooklyn_yoga, self.queens_art]
        )

    def test_selected_filters_stay_filled_in(self):
        """The form shows the active dates, boroughs, and categories again."""
        response = self.client.get(
            reverse("search"),
            {
                "q": "yoga",
                "date_from": "2099-01-01",
                "borough": ["brooklyn", "queens"],
                "category": "yoga",
            },
        )

        self.assertContains(response, 'name="date_from" value="2099-01-01"')
        self.assertContains(response, 'value="brooklyn" id="filter-borough_1" checked')
        self.assertContains(response, 'value="queens" id="filter-borough_2" checked')
        self.assertContains(response, 'value="yoga" id="filter-category_1" checked')
        self.assertNotContains(
            response, 'value="manhattan" id="filter-borough_0" checked'
        )

    def test_quick_date_buttons_fill_in_their_dates(self):
        """Choosing "This weekend" shows the matching dates in the calendar inputs."""
        with patch("events.filters.nyc_today", return_value=date(2099, 1, 12)):
            response = self.get(when="weekend")

        self.assertContains(response, 'name="date_from" value="2099-01-17"')
        self.assertContains(response, 'name="date_to" value="2099-01-18"')
        self.assertTrue(response.context["has_filters"])

    def test_pagination_keeps_the_search_term_and_filters(self):
        """Later pages use the same filters, so they stay filled in."""
        Event.objects.bulk_create(
            [
                Event(
                    guid=f"page-{index}",
                    title="Yoga Flow",
                    park_ids="B100",
                    categories="Yoga & Pilates Classes",
                    content_hash="p" * 64,
                )
                for index in range(SEARCH_PAGE_SIZE + 1)
            ]
        )
        params = {"q": "yoga flow", "borough": "brooklyn", "category": "yoga"}
        expected = "q=yoga%20flow&amp;borough=brooklyn&amp;category=yoga"

        first = self.client.get(reverse("search"), params)
        second = self.client.get(reverse("search"), {**params, "page": 2})

        self.assertContains(first, f'href="?{expected}&amp;page=2"')
        self.assertContains(second, f'href="?{expected}&amp;page=1"')
        self.assertContains(second, 'value="brooklyn" id="filter-borough_1" checked')
        self.assertEqual(len(second.context["events"]), 1)

    def test_clearing_filters_returns_to_the_unfiltered_list(self):
        """Clear keeps the search term, or goes back to the home page's list."""
        with_query = self.get(q="yoga flow", borough="brooklyn")
        without_query = self.get(borough="brooklyn")

        self.assertContains(with_query, 'href="?q=yoga%20flow">Clear filters</a>')
        self.assertContains(without_query, 'href="/">Clear filters</a>')

        cleared = self.get(q="yoga")
        self.assertEqual(list(cleared.context["events"]), [self.brooklyn_yoga])
        self.assertNotContains(cleared, "Clear filters")

    def test_no_matches_suggests_clearing_filters(self):
        """An empty filtered result explains how to widen the search."""
        response = self.get(borough="staten-island")

        self.assertContains(response, "No upcoming events match your search.")
        self.assertContains(response, "or clear some filters")

    def test_invalid_filters_show_errors_and_are_ignored(self):
        """Bad dates or choices never break the page or hide every event."""
        response = self.get(
            q="yoga", date_from="2099-01-20", date_to="2099-01-01", borough="atlantis"
        )

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "The end date must be on or after the start.")
        self.assertEqual(list(response.context["events"]), [self.brooklyn_yoga])

    def test_empty_submission_still_asks_for_input(self):
        """Submitting a blank search box and no filters lists nothing."""
        response = self.get(q="", date_from="", date_to="")

        self.assertContains(response, "Enter a search term or choose a filter")
        self.assertEqual(list(response.context["events"]), [])



============================================================
FILE: events\tests\test_hosting.py

"""Tests for event holders posting their own event listings."""

from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from unittest.mock import patch
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.test import TestCase
from django.urls import reverse

from events.forms import EventListingForm
from events.models import Event
from events.services import sync_events

NYC = ZoneInfo("America/New_York")
UTC = ZoneInfo("UTC")


def listing_data(**overrides):
    """Return a complete, valid submission of the event listing form."""
    data = {
        "title": "Sunset Yoga by the River",
        "description": "Bring a mat and unwind with an all-levels class.",
        "start_time": "2099-01-15T18:30",
        "end_time": "2099-01-15T19:45",
        "location": "Pier 1, Brooklyn Bridge Park",
        "borough": "brooklyn",
        "categories": ["yoga", "fitness"],
        "image_url": "https://example.org/yoga.png",
        "registration_url": "https://example.org/tickets",
    }
    data.update(overrides)
    return data


class EventCreateViewTests(TestCase):
    """Signed-in users post events through a validated form."""

    @classmethod
    def setUpTestData(cls):
        cls.host = get_user_model().objects.create_user(
            username="host",
            email="host@example.com",
            password="pass12345",
            display_name="River Yoga Collective",
        )

    def setUp(self):
        self.url = reverse("event_create")

    def post(self, **overrides):
        """Submit the form as the signed-in host."""
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        return self.client.post(self.url, listing_data(**overrides))

    def test_logged_out_visitors_are_sent_to_login(self):
        """The form requires an account, then returns to it after login."""
        response = self.client.get(self.url)

        self.assertEqual(self.url, "/events/new/")
        self.assertRedirects(response, f"{reverse('login')}?next={self.url}")
        self.client.post(self.url, listing_data())
        self.assertFalse(Event.objects.exists())

    def test_home_page_links_signed_in_users_to_the_form(self):
        """Only signed-in visitors see the "Post an event" link."""
        link = f'href="{self.url}">Post an event</a>'
        self.assertNotContains(self.client.get(reverse("landing")), link)

        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        self.assertContains(self.client.get(reverse("landing")), link)

    def test_form_asks_for_every_listing_detail(self):
        """The form offers all fields, with calendars, boroughs, and categories."""
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        response = self.client.get(self.url)

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertTemplateUsed(response, "events/event_form.html")
        for name in ("title", "description", "location", "image_url"):
            self.assertContains(response, f'name="{name}"')
        self.assertContains(response, 'type="datetime-local" name="start_time"')
        self.assertContains(response, 'type="datetime-local" name="end_time"')
        self.assertContains(response, '<option value="brooklyn">Brooklyn</option>')
        self.assertContains(response, 'name="categories" value="yoga"')
        self.assertContains(response, "Ticketing or registration link")

    def test_valid_submission_posts_the_event_for_its_host(self):
        """The event is saved in New York time and the host sees it posted."""
        response = self.post()

        event = Event.objects.get()
        self.assertRedirects(
            response,
            reverse("event_detail", args=[event.pk]),
            fetch_redirect_response=False,
        )
        self.assertEqual(event.host, self.host)
        self.assertTrue(event.guid.startswith(Event.HOSTED_GUID_PREFIX))
        self.assertEqual(event.start_time, datetime(2099, 1, 15, 18, 30, tzinfo=NYC))
        self.assertEqual(event.end_time, datetime(2099, 1, 15, 19, 45, tzinfo=NYC))
        self.assertEqual(event.borough, Event.Borough.BROOKLYN)
        self.assertEqual(event.categories, "Fitness | Yoga & Pilates")
        self.assertEqual(event.registration_url, "https://example.org/tickets")
        self.assertEqual(event.status, Event.Status.UPCOMING)

        detail = self.client.get(response.url)
        self.assertContains(detail, "Your event is posted.")
        self.assertContains(detail, "Hosted by <strong>River Yoga Collective</strong>")
        self.assertContains(detail, "(Brooklyn)")
        self.assertNotContains(self.client.get(response.url), "Your event is posted.")

    def test_each_posted_event_gets_its_own_id(self):
        """Hosts can post several events without clashing source IDs."""
        self.post()
        self.post(title="Second session")

        self.assertEqual(Event.objects.values("guid").distinct().count(), 2)

    def test_optional_fields_can_be_left_blank(self):
        """End time and both links are optional."""
        self.post(end_time="", image_url="", registration_url="")

        event = Event.objects.get()
        self.assertIsNone(event.end_time)
        self.assertEqual(event.image_url, "")
        self.assertEqual(event.registration_url, "")

    def test_required_fields_report_errors_and_keep_input(self):
        """Missing details show errors next to fields; typed values stay."""
        response = self.post(
            description="   ",
            location="",
            borough="",
            categories=[],
            start_time="",
        )

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertFalse(Event.objects.exists())
        form = response.context["form"]
        self.assertEqual(
            set(form.errors),
            {"description", "location", "borough", "categories", "start_time"},
        )
        self.assertContains(response, "Choose at least one category.")
        self.assertContains(response, 'value="Sunset Yoga by the River"')
        self.assertContains(response, 'value="https://example.org/tickets"')

    def test_start_time_must_be_in_the_future(self):
        """Events cannot be posted for a time that has already passed."""
        past = (datetime.now(NYC) - timedelta(hours=1)).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M")
        response = self.post(start_time=past, end_time="")

        self.assertContains(response, "Choose a start time in the future.")
        self.assertFalse(Event.objects.exists())

    def test_end_time_must_be_after_the_start(self):
        """An end time before or equal to the start time is rejected."""
        for end_time in ("2099-01-15T17:00", "2099-01-15T18:30"):
            with self.subTest(end_time=end_time):
                response = self.post(end_time=end_time)

                self.assertContains(
                    response, "The end time must be after the start time."
                )
        self.assertFalse(Event.objects.exists())

    def test_links_must_be_valid(self):
        """Image and ticketing links must be real web addresses."""
        response = self.post(image_url="not a link", registration_url="javascript:1")

        self.assertEqual(
            set(response.context["form"].errors), {"image_url", "registration_url"}
        )
        self.assertFalse(Event.objects.exists())

    def test_imported_events_have_no_host_line(self):
        """NYC Parks events keep their detail page without a host."""
        event = Event.objects.create(guid="123", title="Park Tour", content_hash="")

        response = self.client.get(reverse("event_detail", args=[event.pk]))

        self.assertNotContains(response, "Hosted by")


class EventListingFormTests(TestCase):
    """The form shows stored times as New York wall-clock time."""

    def test_existing_times_are_displayed_in_new_york_time(self):
        """A stored UTC time appears as the matching local time in the input."""
        form = EventListingForm(
            initial={"start_time": datetime(2099, 1, 15, 23, 30, tzinfo=UTC)}
        )

        self.assertIn('value="2099-01-15T18:30"', str(form["start_time"]))


class HostedEventDiscoveryTests(TestCase):
    """Posted events are discoverable like NYC Parks events."""

    @classmethod
    def setUpTestData(cls):
        cls.host = get_user_model().objects.create_user(
            username="host", email="host@example.com", password="pass12345"
        )

    def setUp(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        self.client.post(reverse("event_create"), listing_data())
        self.event = Event.objects.get()

    def test_posted_event_appears_on_the_home_page(self):
        """The home page's upcoming list includes hosted events."""
        response = self.client.get(reverse("landing"))

        self.assertIn(self.event, response.context["events"])

    def test_posted_event_appears_in_search(self):
        """Full-text search finds hosted events by their title."""
        response = self.client.get(reverse("search"), {"q": "sunset yoga"})

        self.assertEqual(list(response.context["events"]), [self.event])

    def test_posted_event_matches_borough_category_and_date_filters(self):
        """Every filter uses the borough and categories the host chose."""
        matching = {
            "borough": "brooklyn",
            "category": "yoga",
            "date_from": "2099-01-15",
            "date_to": "2099-01-15",
        }
        response = self.client.get(reverse("search"), matching)
        self.assertEqual(list(response.context["events"]), [self.event])

        for wrong in (
            {"borough": "queens"},
            {"category": "art"},
            {"date_from": "2099-01-16"},
        ):
            with self.subTest(wrong=wrong):
                response = self.client.get(reverse("search"), wrong)
                self.assertNotIn(self.event, response.context["events"])


class HostedEventSyncTests(TestCase):
    """The NYC Parks sync never edits hosted events, but retires past ones."""

    NOW = datetime(2099, 1, 15, 12, 0, tzinfo=NYC)

    @classmethod
    def setUpTestData(cls):
        cls.host = get_user_model().objects.create_user(
            username="host", email="host@example.com", password="pass12345"
        )

    def hosted(self, guid, **fields):
        """Create a hosted event directly in the database."""
        return Event.objects.create(
            guid=Event.HOSTED_GUID_PREFIX + guid,
            host=self.host,
            title=fields.pop("title", guid),
            content_hash="",
            **fields,
        )

    def sync(self, records=()):
        """Run the sync at a fixed time against the given feed records."""
        with (
            patch("events.services.fetch_feed", return_value=list(records)),
            patch("events.services.timezone.now", return_value=self.NOW),
        ):
            return sync_events()

    def test_sync_leaves_hosted_events_unchanged(self):
        """Importing the feed neither edits nor removes hosted events."""
        event = self.hosted(
            "kept",
            title="Community Cleanup",
            start_time=datetime(2099, 1, 20, 9, tzinfo=NYC),
            end_time=datetime(2099, 1, 20, 11, tzinfo=NYC),
            borough="queens",
        )
        updated_at = event.updated_at

        counts = self.sync([{"guid": "999", "title": "Park Tour"}])

        event.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(counts["created"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(event.title, "Community Cleanup")
        self.assertEqual(event.borough, "queens")
        self.assertEqual(event.status, Event.Status.UPCOMING)
        self.assertEqual(event.updated_at, updated_at)

    def test_finished_hosted_events_are_marked_done(self):
        """Hosted events end like feed events; without an end, after their day."""
        ended = self.hosted(
            "ended",
            start_time=self.NOW - timedelta(hours=3),
            end_time=self.NOW - timedelta(hours=1),
        )
        yesterday = self.hosted("yesterday", start_time=self.NOW - timedelta(days=1))
        this_morning = self.hosted(
            "this-morning", start_time=self.NOW - timedelta(hours=4)
        )
        feed_without_end = Event.objects.create(
            guid="555", title="Feed event", start_time=self.NOW - timedelta(days=1)
        )

        counts = self.sync()

        self.assertEqual(counts["marked_done"], 2)
        for event, status in (
            (ended, Event.Status.DONE),
            (yesterday, Event.Status.DONE),
            (this_morning, Event.Status.UPCOMING),
            (feed_without_end, Event.Status.UPCOMING),
        ):
            with self.subTest(event=event.guid):
                event.refresh_from_db()
                self.assertEqual(event.status, status)

FILE: events\tests\test_messaging.py
