"""Tests for pair-level attendee messaging."""

from datetime import datetime
from unittest.mock import patch
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.test import TestCase
from django.urls import reverse

from accounts.models import ContactPermission
from events.contact_services import create_contact_request
from events.forms import DirectMessageForm, EventPostForm, EventReplyForm
from events.models import (
    ContactRequest,
    Conversation,
    DirectMessage,
    Event,
    EventInterest,
    Notification,
)

User = get_user_model()
NYC = ZoneInfo("America/New_York")


def make_event(**kwargs):
    defaults = {
        "guid": "messages-event",
        "title": "Community Walk",
        "content_hash": "m" * 64,
        "start_time": datetime(2099, 3, 15, 9, 0, tzinfo=NYC),
        "status": Event.Status.UPCOMING,
    }
    defaults.update(kwargs)
    return Event.objects.create(**defaults)


class MessagingViewTests(TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.sender = User.objects.create_user(
            username="sender",
            email="sender@example.com",
            password="Secure-Passphrase-23!",
            share_profile_with_attendees=True,
        )
        self.recipient = User.objects.create_user(
            username="recipient",
            email="recipient@example.com",
            password="Secure-Passphrase-23!",
            share_profile_with_attendees=True,
        )
        self.outsider = User.objects.create_user(
            username="outsider",
            email="outsider@example.com",
            password="Secure-Passphrase-23!",
        )
        self.event = make_event()
        EventInterest.objects.create(event=self.event, user=self.sender)
        EventInterest.objects.create(event=self.event, user=self.recipient)
        self.client.force_login(self.sender)

    def accept_contact(self):
        return ContactRequest.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            sender=self.sender,
            recipient=self.recipient,
            status=ContactRequest.Status.ACCEPTED,
        )

    def test_profile_offers_send_message_for_shared_attendee(self):
        response = self.client.get(
            reverse("public_profile", args=[self.recipient.username])
        )

        self.assertContains(response, "Send Message")
        self.assertContains(
            response,
            reverse("message_user", args=[self.recipient.pk]),
        )

    def test_profile_self_and_missing_profile_are_handled(self):
        own_profile = self.client.get(
            reverse("public_profile", args=[self.sender.username])
        )
        missing_profile = self.client.get(
            reverse("public_profile", args=["no-such-user"])
        )

        self.assertFalse(own_profile.context["can_message"])
        self.assertEqual(missing_profile.status_code, 404)

    def test_cannot_start_conversation_with_self(self):
        response = self.client.get(reverse("message_user", args=[self.sender.pk]))

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 404)

    def test_contact_request_requires_an_eligible_recipient(self):
        contact_request, created = create_contact_request(
            self.event,
            self.sender,
            self.outsider,
            "Hello",
        )

        self.assertIsNone(contact_request)
        self.assertFalse(created)

    def test_hidden_profile_requires_accepted_contact_to_open_messages(self):
        self.recipient.share_profile_with_attendees = False
        self.recipient.save(update_fields=["share_profile_with_attendees"])

        response = self.client.get(reverse("message_user", args=[self.recipient.pk]))

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 404)
        self.accept_contact()
        response = self.client.get(reverse("message_user", args=[self.recipient.pk]))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 302)
        self.assertEqual(Conversation.objects.count(), 1)

    def test_start_flow_sends_contact_request_instead_of_message(self):
        response = self.client.get(reverse("message_user", args=[self.recipient.pk]))

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "Send Contact Request")
        self.assertContains(response, "Community Walk")

        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("message_user", args=[self.recipient.pk]),
            {"event": self.event.pk, "message": "Would you like to go together?"},
        )

        self.assertRedirects(
            response,
            reverse("message_user", args=[self.recipient.pk]),
        )
        contact_request = ContactRequest.objects.get()
        self.assertEqual(contact_request.status, ContactRequest.Status.PENDING)
        self.assertEqual(contact_request.sender, self.sender)
        self.assertEqual(contact_request.message, "Would you like to go together?")
        self.assertFalse(Conversation.objects.exists())
        notification = Notification.objects.get(contact_request=contact_request)
        self.assertEqual(notification.recipient, self.recipient)
        self.assertEqual(notification.event, self.event)

    def test_ineligible_contact_request_reports_that_shared_event_is_required(self):
        with patch(
            "events.messaging_views.create_contact_request",
            return_value=(None, False),
        ):
            response = self.client.post(
                reverse("message_user", args=[self.recipient.pk]),
                {"event": self.event.pk, "message": "Hello"},
                follow=True,
            )

        self.assertContains(
            response,
            "Choose an upcoming event that you both are interested in.",
        )

    def test_duplicate_pending_contact_request_is_not_created_again(self):
        existing = ContactRequest.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            sender=self.sender,
            recipient=self.recipient,
        )

        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("message_user", args=[self.recipient.pk]),
            {"event": self.event.pk, "message": "A duplicate"},
            follow=True,
        )

        self.assertContains(response, "already awaiting a response")
        self.assertEqual(ContactRequest.objects.count(), 1)
        self.assertEqual(existing.status, ContactRequest.Status.PENDING)

    def test_open_contact_permission_starts_an_accepted_conversation(self):
        self.recipient.contact_permission = ContactPermission.OPEN
        self.recipient.save(update_fields=["contact_permission"])

        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("message_user", args=[self.recipient.pk]),
            {"event": self.event.pk, "message": "Hello!"},
        )

        conversation = Conversation.objects.get()
        self.assertRedirects(
            response,
            reverse("conversation", args=[conversation.pk]),
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            ContactRequest.objects.get().status,
            ContactRequest.Status.ACCEPTED,
        )

    def test_accepting_request_creates_pair_thread_and_unlocks_messaging(self):
        contact_request = ContactRequest.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            sender=self.recipient,
            recipient=self.sender,
        )

        response = self.client.post(
            reverse(
                "respond_to_contact_request",
                args=[contact_request.pk, "accept"],
            )
        )

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 302)
        thread = Conversation.objects.get()
        self.assertEqual(thread.other_participant(self.sender), self.recipient)
        self.assertRedirects(
            self.client.get(reverse("message_user", args=[self.recipient.pk])),
            reverse("conversation", args=[thread.pk]),
        )

    def test_reply_creates_message_without_duplicate_notification(self):
        self.accept_contact()
        thread, _ = Conversation.get_or_create_for_users(self.sender, self.recipient)

        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("conversation", args=[thread.pk]),
            {"body": "See you there!"},
        )

        self.assertRedirects(response, reverse("conversation", args=[thread.pk]))
        message = DirectMessage.objects.get()
        self.assertEqual(message.sender, self.sender)
        self.assertIsNone(message.event)
        self.assertEqual(message.body, "See you there!")
        self.assertFalse(Notification.objects.exists())

    def test_messages_are_not_listed_as_notifications_and_mark_read_in_chat(self):
        self.accept_contact()
        thread, _ = Conversation.get_or_create_for_users(self.sender, self.recipient)
        message = DirectMessage.objects.create(
            conversation=thread,
            sender=self.sender,
            event=self.event,
            body="Meet by the main entrance.",
        )
        self.client.force_login(self.recipient)

        response = self.client.get(reverse("notifications"))

        self.assertNotContains(response, "Meet by the main entrance.")
        message.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertIsNone(message.read_at)

        response = self.client.get(reverse("conversation", args=[thread.pk]))

        self.assertNotContains(response, "Community Walk")
        self.assertContains(response, "Meet by the main entrance.")
        message.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertIsNotNone(message.read_at)

    def test_chat_composer_has_no_redundant_event_selector(self):
        self.accept_contact()
        thread, _ = Conversation.get_or_create_for_users(self.sender, self.recipient)

        response = self.client.get(reverse("conversation", args=[thread.pk]))

        self.assertContains(response, 'aria-label="Write a message"')
        self.assertContains(response, "chat-composer")
        self.assertNotContains(response, "Related event")
        self.assertNotContains(response, "Community Walk")

    def test_unaccepted_contacts_cannot_open_or_post_to_conversation(self):
        contact_request = ContactRequest.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            sender=self.sender,
            recipient=self.recipient,
        )
        thread, _ = Conversation.get_or_create_for_users(self.sender, self.recipient)

        response = self.client.get(reverse("conversation", args=[thread.pk]))

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 404)
        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("conversation", args=[thread.pk]),
            {"body": "Not yet"},
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 404)
        response = self.client.get(reverse("conversation_updates", args=[thread.pk]))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 404)
        self.assertEqual(contact_request.status, ContactRequest.Status.PENDING)
        self.assertFalse(DirectMessage.objects.exists())

    def test_nonparticipant_cannot_view_conversation(self):
        self.accept_contact()
        thread, _ = Conversation.get_or_create_for_users(self.sender, self.recipient)
        self.client.force_login(self.outsider)

        response = self.client.get(reverse("conversation", args=[thread.pk]))

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 404)

    def test_message_keeps_event_association_out_of_chat_display(self):
        self.accept_contact()
        thread, _ = Conversation.get_or_create_for_users(self.sender, self.recipient)
        DirectMessage.objects.create(
            conversation=thread,
            sender=self.sender,
            event=self.event,
            body="This message is still event-related.",
        )

        response = self.client.get(reverse("conversation", args=[thread.pk]))

        self.assertContains(response, "This message is still event-related.")
        self.assertNotContains(response, "Community Walk")
        self.assertNotContains(response, "Related event")

    def test_polling_returns_only_new_messages_and_marks_them_read(self):
        self.accept_contact()
        thread, _ = Conversation.get_or_create_for_users(self.sender, self.recipient)
        first = DirectMessage.objects.create(
            conversation=thread,
            sender=self.sender,
            body="Earlier message",
        )
        second = DirectMessage.objects.create(
            conversation=thread,
            sender=self.recipient,
            body="New reply",
        )
        self.client.force_login(self.sender)

        response = self.client.get(
            reverse("conversation_updates", args=[thread.pk]),
            {"after": first.pk},
        )

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(
            [item["id"] for item in response.json()["messages"]],
            [second.pk],
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.json()["messages"][0]["body"], "New reply")
        second.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertIsNotNone(second.read_at)

    def test_polling_rejects_invalid_cursor_and_nonparticipants(self):
        self.accept_contact()
        thread, _ = Conversation.get_or_create_for_users(self.sender, self.recipient)

        response = self.client.get(
            reverse("conversation_updates", args=[thread.pk]),
            {"after": "not-an-id"},
        )

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 400)
        negative_cursor = self.client.get(
            reverse("conversation_updates", args=[thread.pk]),
            {"after": "-1"},
        )
        self.assertEqual(negative_cursor.status_code, 400)
        self.client.force_login(self.outsider)
        response = self.client.get(
            reverse("conversation_updates", args=[thread.pk]),
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 404)

    def test_ajax_send_returns_created_message_without_page_reload(self):
        self.accept_contact()
        thread, _ = Conversation.get_or_create_for_users(self.sender, self.recipient)

        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("conversation", args=[thread.pk]),
            {"body": "Sent live"},
            HTTP_X_REQUESTED_WITH="XMLHttpRequest",
            HTTP_ACCEPT="application/json",
        )

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(response.json()["message"]["body"], "Sent live")
        self.assertEqual(DirectMessage.objects.get().sender, self.sender)

    def test_invalid_ajax_send_returns_form_errors(self):
        self.accept_contact()
        thread, _ = Conversation.get_or_create_for_users(self.sender, self.recipient)

        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("conversation", args=[thread.pk]),
            {"body": "   "},
            HTTP_X_REQUESTED_WITH="XMLHttpRequest",
        )

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 400)
        self.assertIn("errors", response.json())
        self.assertEqual(DirectMessage.objects.count(), 0)

    def test_whitespace_only_community_and_message_forms_are_invalid(self):
        cases = (
            (DirectMessageForm, "Your message cannot be empty."),
            (EventPostForm, "Your post cannot be empty."),
            (EventReplyForm, "Your reply cannot be empty."),
        )
        for form_class, expected_error in cases:
            with self.subTest(form=form_class.__name__):
                form = form_class(data={"body": "   "})
                self.assertFalse(form.is_valid())
                self.assertEqual(
                    form.errors.as_data()["body"][0].message,
                    expected_error,
                )

    def test_messages_navigation_shows_unread_count_not_notification_count(self):
        self.accept_contact()
        thread, _ = Conversation.get_or_create_for_users(self.sender, self.recipient)
        DirectMessage.objects.create(
            conversation=thread,
            sender=self.recipient,
            body="Unread for sender",
        )

        response = self.client.get(reverse("landing"))

        self.assertContains(response, "Messages")
        self.assertContains(response, "1 new")
        self.assertFalse(Notification.objects.exists())

    def test_unread_message_count_endpoint_reports_current_count(self):
        thread, _ = Conversation.get_or_create_for_users(self.sender, self.recipient)
        url = reverse("unread_message_count")

        self.assertEqual(self.client.get(url).json()["count"], 0)
        DirectMessage.objects.create(
            conversation=thread,
            sender=self.recipient,
            body="Arrived after landing loaded.",
        )

        response = self.client.get(url)

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(response["Cache-Control"], "no-store")
        self.assertEqual(response.json(), {"count": 1})

    def test_unread_message_count_endpoint_requires_login(self):
        self.client.logout()

        response = self.client.get(reverse("unread_message_count"))

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 302)
        self.assertIn("/login", response.url)

    def test_landing_page_has_live_message_count_badge_hook(self):
        response = self.client.get(reverse("landing"))

        self.assertContains(
            response,
            f'data-message-count-url="{reverse("unread_message_count")}"',
        )
        self.assertContains(response, "events/message-count.js")
        self.assertNotContains(response, "message-count-badge")
        self.assertNotContains(response, '<span class="count-pill"></span>', html=True)

    def test_landing_page_renders_badge_only_when_unread_messages_exist(self):
        thread, _ = Conversation.get_or_create_for_users(self.sender, self.recipient)
        DirectMessage.objects.create(
            conversation=thread,
            sender=self.recipient,
            body="Unread message",
        )

        response = self.client.get(reverse("landing"))

        self.assertContains(response, "Messages")
        self.assertContains(response, "message-count-badge")
        self.assertContains(response, "1 new")

    def test_messages_page_is_private_and_shows_last_sender(self):
        self.accept_contact()
        thread, _ = Conversation.get_or_create_for_users(self.sender, self.recipient)
        DirectMessage.objects.create(
            conversation=thread,
            sender=self.recipient,
            body="A private hello.",
        )

        response = self.client.get(reverse("inbox"))

        self.assertContains(response, "<h1>Messages</h1>", html=True)
        self.assertContains(response, "recipient")
        self.assertContains(response, "A private hello.")
        self.client.force_login(self.outsider)
        response = self.client.get(reverse("inbox"))
        self.assertFalse(response.context["conversations"])
        self.assertContains(response, "No messages yet")



============================================================
FILE: events\tests\test_models.py
============================================================
"""Tests for the Event model."""

from django.test import TestCase

from accounts.models import User
from events.models import (
    ContactRequest,
    Conversation,
    DirectMessage,
    Event,
    EventInterest,
    EventPost,
    EventReply,
)


class EventModelTests(TestCase):
    """Event rows default to upcoming and identify themselves by title."""

    def test_new_event_defaults_to_upcoming(self):
        """A freshly created event is upcoming until a sync marks it done."""
        event = Event.objects.create(guid="1", title="Yoga", content_hash="a" * 64)
        self.assertEqual(event.status, Event.Status.UPCOMING)
        self.assertEqual(event.get_status_display(), "Upcoming")
        self.assertEqual(Event.Status.DONE.value, "done")

    def test_str_returns_the_title(self):
        """Admin listings show the event title."""
        event = Event.objects.create(
            guid="2", title="Birdwatching", content_hash="b" * 64
        )
        self.assertEqual(str(event), "Birdwatching")

    def test_default_ordering_is_chronological(self):
        """Events are ordered by start time, then end time, then title."""
        self.assertEqual(Event._meta.ordering, ["start_time", "end_time", "title"])

    def test_primary_category_is_the_first_listed_category(self):
        """A card tag uses the first name in the pipe-separated category list."""
        event = Event.objects.create(
            guid="3",
            title="Zumba in the Park",
            categories="Fitness | Dance | Shape Up NYC",
            content_hash="c" * 64,
        )

        self.assertEqual(event.primary_category, "Fitness")

    def test_primary_category_handles_one_or_no_categories(self):
        """Events without a category list still return a clean, empty tag."""
        single = Event.objects.create(
            guid="4", title="Birdwatching", categories="Nature", content_hash="d" * 64
        )
        untagged = Event.objects.create(
            guid="5", title="Storytime Circle", content_hash="e" * 64
        )

        self.assertEqual(single.primary_category, "Nature")
        self.assertEqual(untagged.primary_category, "")

    def test_is_cancelled_follows_the_feed_title_prefix(self):
        """The feed's "CANCELLED" and "CANCELED" title prefixes both count."""
        cancelled = Event.objects.create(
            guid="6", title="CANCELLED: Yoga in the Park", content_hash="f" * 64
        )
        cancelled_us = Event.objects.create(
            guid="7", title="CANCELED: Tai Chi on the Lawn", content_hash="h" * 64
        )
        running = Event.objects.create(
            guid="8", title="Yoga in the Park", content_hash="g" * 64
        )

        self.assertTrue(cancelled.is_cancelled)
        self.assertTrue(cancelled_us.is_cancelled)
        self.assertFalse(running.is_cancelled)

    def test_community_and_message_models_have_useful_strings(self):
        """Admin-facing records identify their associated users and content."""
        sender = User.objects.create_user("model-sender", "sender@example.com")
        recipient = User.objects.create_user("model-recipient", "recipient@example.com")
        outsider = User.objects.create_user("model-outsider", "outsider@example.com")
        event = Event.objects.create(
            guid="community-models",
            title="Community Walk",
            content_hash="i" * 64,
        )
        interest = EventInterest.objects.create(event=event, user=sender)
        request = ContactRequest.objects.create(
            event=event,
            sender=sender,
            recipient=recipient,
        )
        conversation, _ = Conversation.get_or_create_for_users(sender, recipient)
        message = DirectMessage.objects.create(
            conversation=conversation,
            sender=sender,
            body="Hello",
        )
        post = EventPost.objects.create(event=event, author=sender, body="A post")
        reply = EventReply.objects.create(post=post, author=recipient, body="A reply")

        self.assertEqual(str(interest), f"{sender} â€” Interested â€” {event}")
        self.assertEqual(str(request), f"{sender} â†’ {recipient} (pending)")
        self.assertIsNone(conversation.other_participant(outsider))
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            Conversation.get_or_create_for_users(sender, sender)
        self.assertEqual(str(conversation), f"Conversation: {sender} â†” {recipient}")
        self.assertEqual(
            str(message),
            f"Message from {sender} in conversation {conversation.pk}",
        )
        self.assertEqual(str(post), f"Post by {sender} on {event}")
        self.assertEqual(str(reply), f"Reply by {recipient} on post {post.pk}")



============================================================
FILE: events\tests\test_notification_integration.py
============================================================
"""Keep upstream attendance and discussion features working with request alerts."""

from itertools import product
from unittest.mock import patch

from django.core import mail
from django.urls import reverse

from events.models import ContactRequest, Event, EventInterest, EventPost, EventReply
from events.models import Notification
from events.notifications import interested_recipients, notify_contact_request
from events.tests.test_notifications import NotifyTestCase


class NotificationIntegrationTests(NotifyTestCase):
    """Exercise the combined feature through its public views."""

    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        self.fan.share_profile_with_attendees = True
        self.fan.save()
        self.contact_url = reverse(
            "send_contact_request", args=[self.event.pk, self.fan.pk]
        )
        self.post = EventPost.objects.create(
            event=self.event, author=self.fan, body="What should I bring?"
        )
        self.reply_url = reverse("create_event_reply", args=[self.post.pk])

    def test_reply_alert_becomes_read_while_request_stays_new(self):
        contact_request = ContactRequest.objects.create(
            event=self.event, sender=self.host, recipient=self.fan
        )
        request_alert = notify_contact_request(contact_request)
        self.assertRedirects(
            self.client.post(self.reply_url, {"body": "Bring water."}), self.detail_url
        )
        reply_alert = Notification.objects.exclude(pk=request_alert.pk).get()
        self.assertEqual(reply_alert.recipient, self.fan)
        self.assertEqual(reply_alert.event, self.event)
        self.assertIsNone(reply_alert.contact_request_id)
        self.assertIn("New reply to your comment", reply_alert.subject)
        self.assertIn("host replied", reply_alert.body)
        self.client.force_login(self.fan)
        response = self.client.get(reverse("notifications"))
        self.assertEqual(
            [n.pk for n in response.context["notifications"]],
            [request_alert.pk, reply_alert.pk],
        )
        request_alert.refresh_from_db()
        reply_alert.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertIsNone(request_alert.read_at)
        self.assertIsNotNone(reply_alert.read_at)
        self.assertContains(self.client.get(reverse("landing")), "1 new")
        self.assertEqual(mail.outbox, [])

    def test_self_invalid_and_closed_event_replies_do_not_notify(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.fan)
        self.client.post(self.reply_url, {"body": "My own follow-up"})
        self.client.post(self.reply_url, {"body": "   "})
        self.event.status = Event.Status.DONE
        self.event.save()
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        self.client.post(self.reply_url, {"body": "Too late"})
        self.assertEqual(EventReply.objects.count(), 1)
        self.assertFalse(Notification.objects.exists())

    def test_reply_notification_failure_rolls_back_reply(self):
        with patch(
            "events.community_views.Notification.objects.create",
            side_effect=RuntimeError("reply alert failure"),
        ):
            with self.assertRaisesMessage(RuntimeError, "reply alert failure"):
                self.client.post(self.reply_url, {"body": "Bring water."})
        self.assertFalse(EventReply.objects.exists())
        self.assertFalse(Notification.objects.exists())

    def test_reply_for_long_event_title_fits_notification_subject(self):
        self.event.title = "A" * 300
        self.event.save()
        self.assertRedirects(
            self.client.post(self.reply_url, {"body": "Bring water."}), self.detail_url
        )
        alert = Notification.objects.get()
        self.assertEqual(len(alert.subject), 200)
        self.assertIn(self.event.title, alert.body)
        self.assertEqual(EventReply.objects.count(), 1)

    def test_both_intentions_support_requests_and_resolved_card_status(self):
        choices = EventInterest.Status.values
        for sender_status, recipient_status in product(choices, repeat=2):
            with self.subTest(sender=sender_status, recipient=recipient_status):
                ContactRequest.objects.all().delete()
                EventInterest.objects.filter(user=self.host).update(
                    status=sender_status
                )
                EventInterest.objects.filter(user=self.fan).update(
                    status=recipient_status
                )
                self.client.force_login(self.host)
                page = self.client.get(self.detail_url)
                self.assertContains(
                    page, reverse("public_profile", args=[self.fan.username])
                )
                group = (
                    "interested_users"
                    if recipient_status == EventInterest.Status.INTERESTED
                    else "want_to_attend_users"
                )
                self.assertEqual(page.context[group][0]["user"], self.fan)
                self.assertRedirects(
                    self.client.post(self.contact_url, {"message": "Hello"}),
                    self.detail_url,
                )
                self.assertContains(
                    self.client.get(self.detail_url), "awaiting response."
                )
                contact_request = ContactRequest.objects.get()
                alert = Notification.objects.get()
                self.assertIsNone(alert.read_at)
                self.client.force_login(self.fan)
                self.assertRedirects(
                    self.client.post(
                        reverse(
                            "respond_to_contact_request",
                            args=[contact_request.pk, "accept"],
                        ),
                        {"return_to": "notifications"},
                    ),
                    reverse("notifications"),
                )
                alert.refresh_from_db()
                self.assertIsNotNone(alert.read_at)
                self.client.force_login(self.host)
                page = self.client.get(self.detail_url)
                self.assertContains(page, "Contact request accepted.")
                self.assertNotContains(page, f'action="{self.contact_url}"')

    def test_want_to_attend_users_receive_host_announcements(self):
        EventInterest.objects.filter(user=self.fan).update(
            status=EventInterest.Status.WANT_TO_ATTEND
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            set(interested_recipients(self.event)), {self.fan, self.second_fan}
        )
        self.send()
        self.assertEqual(
            set(Notification.objects.values_list("recipient_id", flat=True)),
            {self.fan.pk, self.second_fan.pk},
        )
        self.assertEqual(len(mail.outbox), 2)

    def test_intention_switch_and_removal_do_not_resolve_request(self):
        self.client.post(self.contact_url)
        alert = Notification.objects.get()
        self.client.force_login(self.fan)
        url = reverse("toggle_event_interest", args=[self.event.pk])
        self.client.post(url, {"status": EventInterest.Status.WANT_TO_ATTEND})
        self.assertEqual(
            EventInterest.objects.get(user=self.fan).status,
            EventInterest.Status.WANT_TO_ATTEND,
        )
        self.client.post(url, {"status": EventInterest.Status.INTERESTED})
        self.assertEqual(
            EventInterest.objects.get(user=self.fan).status,
            EventInterest.Status.INTERESTED,
        )
        self.client.post(url, {"status": EventInterest.Status.INTERESTED})
        self.assertFalse(EventInterest.objects.filter(user=self.fan).exists())
        alert.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertIsNone(alert.read_at)
        self.assertEqual(alert.contact_request.status, ContactRequest.Status.PENDING)

    def test_invalid_intention_is_ignored(self):
        self.client.post(
            reverse("toggle_event_interest", args=[self.event.pk]),
            {"status": "invalid"},
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            EventInterest.objects.get(user=self.host).status,
            EventInterest.Status.INTERESTED,
        )

    def test_cards_hide_request_form_for_nonparticipants_and_closed_events(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.bystander)
        response = self.client.get(self.detail_url)
        self.assertNotContains(response, f'action="{self.contact_url}"')
        self.assertContains(response, "Select Interested or Want to Attend")
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        self.host.share_profile_with_attendees = True
        self.host.save()
        self.assertContains(self.client.get(self.detail_url), "This is your profile.")
        self.event.status = Event.Status.DONE
        self.event.save()
        response = self.client.get(self.detail_url)
        self.assertNotContains(response, f'action="{self.contact_url}"')
        self.assertContains(response, "New contact requests are closed")

    def test_declined_card_allows_new_request_and_anonymous_card_requires_login(self):
        self.client.post(self.contact_url)
        contact_request = ContactRequest.objects.get()
        self.client.force_login(self.fan)
        self.client.post(
            reverse("respond_to_contact_request", args=[contact_request.pk, "decline"])
        )
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        self.assertContains(
            self.client.get(self.detail_url), f'action="{self.contact_url}"'
        )
        self.client.logout()
        response = self.client.get(self.detail_url)
        self.assertNotContains(response, f'action="{self.contact_url}"')
        self.assertContains(response, "Log in to Contact")



============================================================
FILE: events\tests\test_notifications.py
============================================================
"""Tests for hosts notifying interested attendees and the notifications page."""

from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.core import mail
from django.test import RequestFactory, TestCase
from django.urls import reverse
from django.utils import timezone

from events.context_processors import notifications as notifications_context
from events.models import Event, EventInterest, Notification
from events.notifications import change_notice, describe_when

NYC = ZoneInfo("America/New_York")


def as_input(moment):
    """Format a datetime the way the form's calendar input submits it."""
    return timezone.localtime(moment, NYC).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M")


class NotifyTestCase(TestCase):
    """A host, two interested fans, a bystander, and one hosted event."""

    @classmethod
    def setUpTestData(cls):
        User = get_user_model()

        def user(name):
            return User.objects.create_user(
                username=name, email=f"{name}@example.com", password="pass12345"
            )

        cls.host = user("host")
        cls.fan = user("fan")
        cls.second_fan = user("second_fan")
        cls.bystander = user("bystander")

    def setUp(self):
        self.event = self.hosted("yoga")
        for person in (self.fan, self.second_fan, self.host):
            EventInterest.objects.create(event=self.event, user=person)
        self.notify_url = reverse("event_notify", args=[self.event.pk])
        self.detail_url = reverse("event_detail", args=[self.event.pk])

    def hosted(self, guid, **fields):
        """Create an upcoming event posted by the host."""
        defaults = {
            "title": "Sunset Yoga",
            "description": "Bring a mat.",
            "start_time": datetime(2099, 1, 15, 18, 30, tzinfo=NYC),
            "end_time": datetime(2099, 1, 15, 19, 45, tzinfo=NYC),
            "location": "Pier 1",
            "borough": Event.Borough.BROOKLYN,
            "categories": "Fitness",
        }
        defaults.update(fields)
        return Event.objects.create(
            guid=Event.HOSTED_GUID_PREFIX + guid,
            host=self.host,
            content_hash="",
            **defaults,
        )

    def send(self, subject="Bring water", message="It will be hot."):
        return self.client.post(
            self.notify_url, {"subject": subject, "message": message}, follow=True
        )


class HostMessageTests(NotifyTestCase):
    """The host writes a message to everyone marked Interested."""

    def test_host_sees_form_and_recipient_count(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        response = self.client.get(self.detail_url)
        self.assertContains(response, "Message interested attendees")
        self.assertEqual(response.context["recipient_count"], 2)
        self.assertContains(response, self.notify_url)

    def test_others_do_not_see_form(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.fan)
        response = self.client.get(self.detail_url)
        self.assertNotContains(response, "Message interested attendees")
        self.assertNotIn("recipient_count", response.context)

    def test_message_reaches_interested_users_except_host(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        response = self.send()

        self.assertRedirects(response, self.detail_url)
        self.assertContains(response, "Your message was sent to 2 interested people.")
        self.assertEqual(
            sorted(email.to[0] for email in mail.outbox),
            ["fan@example.com", "second_fan@example.com"],
        )
        email = mail.outbox[0]
        self.assertEqual(email.subject, "Bring water")
        self.assertIn("It will be hot.", email.body)
        self.assertIn("http://testserver" + self.detail_url, email.body)
        self.assertEqual(
            set(Notification.objects.values_list("recipient__username", flat=True)),
            {"fan", "second_fan"},
        )

    def test_singular_count(self):
        EventInterest.objects.filter(user=self.second_fan).delete()
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        self.assertContains(
            self.send(), "Your message was sent to 1 interested person."
        )

    def test_nobody_interested(self):
        EventInterest.objects.all().delete()
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        response = self.send()
        self.assertContains(response, "Nobody is interested in this event yet.")
        self.assertEqual(mail.outbox, [])
        self.assertFalse(Notification.objects.exists())

    def test_blank_message_is_rejected(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        response = self.send(subject="", message="  ")
        self.assertContains(response, "Write a subject and a message before sending.")
        self.assertEqual(mail.outbox, [])

    def test_only_host_can_send(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.bystander)
        self.assertEqual(self.send().status_code, 404)
        self.assertEqual(mail.outbox, [])

    def test_cannot_send_after_event_ended(self):
        past = timezone.now() - timedelta(days=2)
        self.event.start_time = past
        self.event.end_time = past + timedelta(hours=1)
        self.event.save()
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        self.assertEqual(self.send().status_code, 404)
        response = self.client.get(self.detail_url)
        self.assertNotContains(response, "Message interested attendees")

    def test_anonymous_redirected_to_login(self):
        response = self.client.post(self.notify_url, {"subject": "a", "message": "b"})
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 302)
        self.assertIn(reverse("login"), response["Location"])

    def test_get_not_allowed(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get(self.notify_url).status_code, 405)


class ChangeNoticeTests(NotifyTestCase):
    """Editing the date, time, or place tells interested users automatically."""

    def edit(self, **overrides):
        data = {
            "title": self.event.title,
            "description": self.event.description,
            "start_time": as_input(self.event.start_time),
            "end_time": as_input(self.event.end_time),
            "location": self.event.location,
            "borough": self.event.borough,
            "categories": ["fitness"],
            "image_url": "",
            "registration_url": "",
        }
        data.update(overrides)
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        return self.client.post(
            reverse("event_edit", args=[self.event.pk]), data, follow=True
        )

    def test_time_change_notifies(self):
        response = self.edit(start_time="2099-01-15T17:30")

        self.assertContains(
            response, "Event updated. We let 2 interested people know about the change."
        )
        self.assertEqual(len(mail.outbox), 2)
        email = mail.outbox[0]
        self.assertEqual(email.subject, "Update: Sunset Yoga has changed")
        self.assertIn(
            "When: Thursday, January 15, 2099 at 5:30 PM â€“ 7:45 PM "
            "(was Thursday, January 15, 2099 at 6:30 PM â€“ 7:45 PM)",
            email.body,
        )
        self.assertEqual(Notification.objects.count(), 2)

    def test_location_change_notifies(self):
        self.edit(location="Pier 6")
        self.assertIn("Where: Pier 6 (was Pier 1)", mail.outbox[0].body)

    def test_other_changes_do_not_notify(self):
        response = self.edit(title="Sunrise Yoga")
        self.assertContains(response, "Event updated.")
        self.assertNotContains(response, "know about the change")
        self.assertEqual(mail.outbox, [])
        self.assertFalse(Notification.objects.exists())

    def test_change_with_nobody_interested(self):
        EventInterest.objects.all().delete()
        response = self.edit(location="Pier 6")
        self.assertNotContains(response, "know about the change")
        self.assertEqual(mail.outbox, [])


class NotificationPageTests(NotifyTestCase):
    """Signed-in users read their notifications, newest first."""

    def notify(self, subject, minutes_ago=0):
        notification = Notification.objects.create(
            recipient=self.fan, event=self.event, subject=subject, body="Details"
        )
        Notification.objects.filter(pk=notification.pk).update(
            created_at=timezone.now() - timedelta(minutes=minutes_ago)
        )
        return notification

    def test_lists_newest_first_and_links_to_event(self):
        self.notify("Older", minutes_ago=30)
        self.notify("Newer")
        self.client.force_login(self.fan)
        response = self.client.get(reverse("notifications"))

        subjects = [n.subject for n in response.context["notifications"]]
        self.assertEqual(subjects, ["Newer", "Older"])
        self.assertContains(response, self.detail_url)
        self.assertContains(response, "New</span>", count=2)

    def test_opening_marks_read(self):
        notification = self.notify("Hello")
        self.client.force_login(self.fan)
        self.client.get(reverse("notifications"))
        notification.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertIsNotNone(notification.read_at)

        response = self.client.get(reverse("notifications"))
        self.assertNotContains(response, "New</span>")

    def test_only_own_notifications(self):
        self.notify("For the fan")
        self.client.force_login(self.bystander)
        response = self.client.get(reverse("notifications"))
        self.assertNotContains(response, "For the fan")
        self.assertContains(response, "No notifications yet.")

    def test_requires_login(self):
        response = self.client.get(reverse("notifications"))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 302)
        self.assertIn(reverse("login"), response["Location"])

    def test_home_shows_unread_count(self):
        self.notify("One")
        self.notify("Two")
        self.client.force_login(self.fan)
        response = self.client.get(reverse("landing"))
        self.assertContains(response, reverse("notifications"))
        self.assertContains(response, "2 new")

        self.client.get(reverse("notifications"))
        response = self.client.get(reverse("landing"))
        self.assertNotContains(response, "2 new")

    def test_context_processor_skips_anonymous_users(self):
        request = RequestFactory().get("/")
        request.user = type("Anon", (), {"is_authenticated": False})()
        self.assertEqual(notifications_context(request), {})


class MessageWordingTests(NotifyTestCase):
    """The text used in automatic change notices."""

    def test_describe_when(self):
        start = datetime(2099, 1, 15, 18, 30, tzinfo=NYC)
        self.assertEqual(describe_when(None, None), "To be announced")
        self.assertEqual(
            describe_when(start, None), "Thursday, January 15, 2099 at 6:30 PM"
        )

    def test_notification_str(self):
        notification = Notification(
            recipient=self.fan, event=self.event, subject="Hi", body=""
        )
        self.assertEqual(str(notification), "Hi â†’ fan")

    def test_no_notice_without_time_or_place_change(self):
        self.assertIsNone(change_notice(self.event, {}, ["title", "description"]))

    def test_notice_names_host(self):
        self.host.display_name = "Park Pals"
        self.host.save()
        previous = {"location": "Pier 1"}
        self.event.location = "Pier 6"
        subject, body = change_notice(self.event, previous, ["location"])
        self.assertTrue(body.startswith("Park Pals changed the details of Sunset Yoga"))



============================================================
FILE: events\tests\test_search.py
============================================================
"""PostgreSQL integration tests for public event search and its cards."""

from datetime import datetime
from unittest.mock import patch
from urllib.parse import quote
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.test import TestCase
from django.urls import reverse
from django.utils.html import escape

from events.models import Event
from events.search import search_events
from events.services import sync_events
from events.views import SEARCH_PAGE_SIZE

NYC = ZoneInfo("America/New_York")


def make_event(guid, **fields):
    """Create an event with optional discovery fields."""
    fields.setdefault("title", "Community gathering")
    fields.setdefault("content_hash", "s" * 64)
    return Event.objects.create(guid=guid, **fields)


class EventSearchTests(TestCase):
    """Search uses stemming, weighted ranking, and live database contents."""

    def search(self, query):
        """Run the full-text query against all events in this test."""
        return search_events(Event.objects.all(), query)

    def test_all_discovery_fields_support_case_insensitive_stemming(self):
        """Inflected words match in every field included in the document."""
        events = [
            make_event(field, **{field: "Walking"})
            for field in (
                "title",
                "categories",
                "park_names",
                "location",
                "description",
            )
        ]
        make_event("unrelated", title="Swimming")

        self.assertCountEqual(self.search("WALKS"), events)

    def test_title_matches_rank_above_categories_and_descriptions(self):
        """Identical terms favor the title, then discovery metadata."""
        description = make_event("description", description="Yoga")
        category = make_event("category", categories="Yoga")
        title = make_event("title", title="Yoga")

        self.assertEqual(list(self.search("yoga")), [title, category, description])

    def test_multiple_terms_can_match_across_fields(self):
        """All ordinary keywords must match, even across different fields."""
        match = make_event("prospect", title="Yoga", location="Prospect Park")
        make_event("central", title="Yoga", location="Central Park")

        self.assertEqual(list(self.search("yoga prospect")), [match])

    def test_websearch_supports_phrases_or_and_exclusions(self):
        """Visitors can quote phrases, combine alternatives, or exclude words."""
        morning = make_event("morning", title="Morning Yoga")
        kids = make_event("kids", title="Evening Yoga", description="Kids welcome")
        birds = make_event("birds", title="Bird Watching")

        self.assertEqual(list(self.search('"morning yoga"')), [morning])
        self.assertCountEqual(self.search("yoga OR bird"), [morning, kids, birds])
        self.assertEqual(list(self.search("yoga -kids")), [morning])

    def test_equal_ranks_sort_by_date_then_id_with_undated_events_last(self):
        """Pagination has deterministic ordering when relevance is equal."""
        undated = make_event("undated", title="Yoga")
        later = make_event(
            "later", title="Yoga", start_time=datetime(2099, 1, 16, 9, tzinfo=NYC)
        )
        first = make_event(
            "first", title="Yoga", start_time=datetime(2099, 1, 15, 9, tzinfo=NYC)
        )
        second = make_event("second", title="Yoga", start_time=first.start_time)

        self.assertEqual(list(self.search("yoga")), [first, second, later, undated])

    def test_imported_events_and_feed_updates_are_searchable_immediately(self):
        """The sync's bulk inserts and saves maintain the search document."""
        with patch(
            "events.services.fetch_feed",
            return_value=[{"guid": "imported", "title": "Outdoor Chess"}],
        ):
            sync_events()
        event = Event.objects.get(guid="imported")
        self.assertEqual(list(self.search("chess")), [event])

        with patch(
            "events.services.fetch_feed",
            return_value=[{"guid": "imported", "title": "Outdoor Painting"}],
        ):
            sync_events()
        event.refresh_from_db()

        self.assertFalse(self.search("chess").exists())
        self.assertEqual(list(self.search("painting")), [event])

    def test_queryset_updates_do_not_leave_stale_search_matches(self):
        """Database updates also work without model save hooks or signals."""
        event = make_event("updated", title="Pickleball")
        self.assertEqual(list(self.search("pickleball")), [event])

        Event.objects.filter(pk=event.pk).update(title="Tennis")
        event.refresh_from_db()

        self.assertFalse(self.search("pickleball").exists())
        self.assertEqual(list(self.search("tennis")), [event])


class SearchViewTests(TestCase):
    """Any visitor can submit a query and browse locally linked event cards."""

    @classmethod
    def setUpTestData(cls):
        cls.user = get_user_model().objects.create_user(username="searcher")
        cls.yoga = make_event(
            "yoga",
            title="Morning Yoga",
            description="Stretch and breathe in the park.",
            categories="Fitness | Yoga",
            location="Prospect Park",
            start_time=datetime(2099, 1, 15, 9, tzinfo=NYC),
            end_time=datetime(2099, 1, 15, 10, tzinfo=NYC),
            image_url="https://example.org/yoga.png",
            event_url="https://example.org/yoga",
            registration_url="https://example.org/register",
        )
        cls.done = make_event("done", title="Past Yoga", status=Event.Status.DONE)

    def test_search_is_public_and_only_lists_upcoming_matches(self):
        """Anonymous requests render results directly at the requested URL."""
        self.assertEqual(reverse("search"), "/search")
        response = self.client.get(reverse("search"), {"q": "yoga"})

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertTemplateUsed(response, "events/search.html")
        self.assertEqual(list(response.context["events"]), [self.yoga])
        self.assertNotContains(response, self.done.title)

    def test_signed_in_visitors_receive_the_same_results(self):
        """Signing in does not change which upcoming events match."""
        anonymous = self.client.get(reverse("search"), {"q": "yoga"})
        self.client.force_login(self.user)
        signed_in = self.client.get(reverse("search"), {"q": "yoga"})

        self.assertEqual(signed_in.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(
            list(signed_in.context["events"]), list(anonymous.context["events"])
        )

    def test_landing_search_form_is_available_to_all_visitors(self):
        """Search is available while the existing landing access stays intact."""
        for signed_in in (False, True):
            with self.subTest(signed_in=signed_in):
                if signed_in:
                    self.client.force_login(self.user)
                response = self.client.get(reverse("landing"))

                self.assertContains(response, 'role="search" method="get"')
                self.assertContains(response, 'action="/search"')
                self.assertContains(response, 'type="search" name="q"')
                self.assertContains(response, '<button type="submit">Search</button>')
                if not signed_in:
                    self.assertContains(response, "Please log in")
                    self.assertNotContains(response, self.yoga.title)

    def test_missing_or_whitespace_queries_show_a_prompt(self):
        """Opening search without keywords never lists every event."""
        for parameters in ({}, {"q": ""}, {"q": " \t "}):
            with self.subTest(parameters=parameters):
                response = self.client.get(reverse("search"), parameters)

                self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
                self.assertContains(response, "Enter a search term")
                self.assertEqual(list(response.context["events"]), [])
                self.assertNotContains(response, self.yoga.title)

    def test_nonmatching_punctuation_and_stopword_queries_are_safe(self):
        """Empty PostgreSQL queries and unmatched words render an empty state."""
        for query in ("astronomy", "!!!", "the and or"):
            with self.subTest(query=query):
                response = self.client.get(reverse("search"), {"q": query})

                self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
                self.assertContains(response, "No upcoming events match your search.")
                self.assertEqual(list(response.context["events"]), [])

    def test_query_is_trimmed_and_unclosed_quotes_are_tolerated(self):
        """User input does not need to be valid raw tsquery syntax."""
        response = self.client.get(reverse("search"), {"q": '  "yoga  '})

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(response.context["query"], '"yoga')
        self.assertContains(response, 'value="&quot;yoga"')
        self.assertEqual(list(response.context["events"]), [self.yoga])

    def test_queries_and_event_content_are_html_escaped(self):
        """Untrusted input remains text in headings, inputs, and cards."""
        query = '<script>alert("x")</script>'
        response = self.client.get(reverse("search"), {"q": query})
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, escape(query))
        self.assertNotContains(response, query)

        title = f"Yoga {query}"
        make_event("escaped", title=title, description="<b>Unsafe markup</b>")
        response = self.client.get(reverse("search"), {"q": "yoga"})
        self.assertContains(response, escape(title))
        self.assertContains(response, "&lt;b&gt;Unsafe markup&lt;/b&gt;")
        self.assertNotContains(response, query)

    def test_cards_link_to_local_detail_urls_and_keep_metadata(self):
        """The entire result card has one local link and NYC-local details."""
        response = self.client.get(reverse("search"), {"q": "yoga"})

        self.assertContains(
            response, f'<a class="event" href="/events/{self.yoga.pk}/">'
        )
        self.assertContains(response, "Jan 15")
        self.assertContains(response, "9:00 AM")
        self.assertContains(response, "10:00 AM")
        self.assertContains(response, "Prospect Park")
        self.assertContains(response, "Fitness | Yoga")
        self.assertContains(response, "Stretch and breathe in the park.")
        self.assertContains(response, 'src="https://example.org/yoga.png"')
        self.assertNotContains(response, f'href="{self.yoga.event_url}"')
        self.assertNotContains(response, f'href="{self.yoga.registration_url}"')
        self.assertRedirects(
            self.client.get(f"/events/{self.yoga.pk}"),
            f"/events/{self.yoga.pk}/",
            status_code=301,
        )

    def test_undated_cards_use_park_names_and_placeholder_images(self):
        """Optional event fields have the same fallbacks as landing cards."""
        make_event("undated", title="Yoga", park_names="Central Park")
        response = self.client.get(reverse("search"), {"q": "yoga"})

        self.assertContains(response, "Date to be announced")
        self.assertContains(response, "Central Park")
        self.assertContains(response, 'src="/static/events/no-image.svg"')
        self.assertContains(response, 'data-placeholder="/static/events/no-image.svg"')

    def test_pagination_preserves_encoded_queries_and_does_not_drop_matches(self):
        """Visitors can reach matches beyond the first page with reserved text."""
        Event.objects.bulk_create(
            [
                Event(
                    guid=f"page-{index}",
                    title="Yoga",
                    location="Central Park",
                    content_hash="p" * 64,
                )
                for index in range(SEARCH_PAGE_SIZE + 1)
            ]
        )
        query = 'yoga & "Central Park"'
        first = self.client.get(reverse("search"), {"q": query})
        second = self.client.get(reverse("search"), {"q": query, "page": 2})

        self.assertEqual(len(first.context["events"]), SEARCH_PAGE_SIZE)
        self.assertEqual(len(second.context["events"]), 1)
        self.assertEqual(first.context["paginator"].count, SEARCH_PAGE_SIZE + 1)
        self.assertContains(first, f'href="?q={quote(query)}&amp;page=2"')
        self.assertContains(second, f'href="?q={quote(query)}&amp;page=1"')
        first_ids = {event.pk for event in first.context["events"]}
        second_ids = {event.pk for event in second.context["events"]}
        self.assertFalse(first_ids & second_ids)
        self.assertEqual(len(first_ids | second_ids), SEARCH_PAGE_SIZE + 1)

    def test_invalid_result_pages_return_404(self):
        """Invalid or out-of-range page numbers do not produce server errors."""
        for page in ("invalid", "0", "999"):
            with self.subTest(page=page):
                response = self.client.get(
                    reverse("search"), {"q": "yoga", "page": page}
                )
                self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 404)



============================================================
FILE: events\tests\test_services.py

"""Tests for fetching and syncing the NYC Parks events feed."""

import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from io import BytesIO
from unittest.mock import patch
from urllib.error import URLError
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

from django.test import TestCase
from django.utils import timezone

from events.models import Event
from events.services import SyncError, parse_record, sync_events

NYC = ZoneInfo("America/New_York")


def feed_record(**overrides):
    """Return a typical feed record, with fields overridable per test."""
    record = {
        "guid": "1001",
        "title": "Morning Yoga",
        "description": "A calm&nbsp;class in the&nbsp;park.",
        "starttime": "2099-01-15T09:00:00.000",
        "endtime": "2099-01-15T10:00:00.000",
        "location": "Prospect Park",
        "parkids": "Q097",
        "parknames": "Prospect Park",
        "categories": "Fitness | Yoga",
        "coordinates": "40.6602, -73.9690",
        "link": {"url": "http://example.org/events/morning-yoga"},
        "image": {"url": "http://example.org/img/yoga.png"},
        "registration_url": {"url": "http://example.org/register"},
        "instructor": "Alex Kim",
        "contact_phone": "212-555-0100",
        ":version": "rv-1",
    }
    record.update(overrides)
    return record


def run_sync(records):
    """Run a sync against a mocked feed and return its counts."""
    body = BytesIO(json.dumps(records).encode("utf-8"))
    with patch("events.services.urlopen", return_value=body):
        return sync_events()


class FetchTests(TestCase):
    """The feed download either returns records or raises SyncError."""

    def test_network_failure_raises_sync_error(self):
        """An unreachable feed raises SyncError and leaves the DB empty."""
        with patch("events.services.urlopen", side_effect=URLError("boom")):
            with self.assertRaisesMessage(SyncError, "could not fetch"):
                sync_events()
        self.assertEqual(Event.objects.count(), 0)

    def test_payload_that_is_not_a_list_raises(self):
        """A JSON object instead of a list is rejected before any write."""
        body = BytesIO(b'{"error": "not a list"}')
        with patch("events.services.urlopen", return_value=body):
            with self.assertRaisesMessage(SyncError, "list of records"):
                sync_events()
        self.assertEqual(Event.objects.count(), 0)

    def test_payload_with_non_record_entries_raises(self):
        """A list containing non-objects is rejected before any write."""
        body = BytesIO(b"[1, 2, 3]")
        with patch("events.services.urlopen", return_value=body):
            with self.assertRaisesMessage(SyncError, "list of records"):
                sync_events()
        self.assertEqual(Event.objects.count(), 0)

    def test_invalid_json_raises_sync_error(self):
        """A body that is not JSON is reported as a fetch failure."""
        body = BytesIO(b"<html>gateway error</html>")
        with patch("events.services.urlopen", return_value=body):
            with self.assertRaisesMessage(SyncError, "could not fetch"):
                sync_events()


class ParseRecordTests(TestCase):
    """Individual feed records are normalized into model fields."""

    def test_html_is_decoded_and_stripped_to_plain_text(self):
        """Entities become real characters and tags are removed."""
        data = parse_record(
            feed_record(
                title="NYC Parks&#8217; Open Call",
                description="<b>Bold</b> &amp; beautiful &nbsp; days",
            )
        )
        self.assertEqual(data["title"], "NYC Parksâ€™ Open Call")
        self.assertEqual(data["description"], "Bold & beautiful days")

    def test_http_urls_are_upgraded_to_https(self):
        """Image, event and registration links load over https."""
        data = parse_record(feed_record())
        self.assertEqual(data["image_url"], "https://example.org/img/yoga.png")
        self.assertEqual(data["event_url"], "https://example.org/events/morning-yoga")
        self.assertEqual(data["registration_url"], "https://example.org/register")

    def test_missing_optional_fields_become_empty_values(self):
        """Records without extras still parse into blank, safe defaults."""
        data = parse_record({"guid": "9", "title": "Bare Event", ":version": "rv-9"})
        self.assertEqual(data["description"], "")
        self.assertEqual(data["image_url"], "")
        self.assertEqual(data["event_url"], "")
        self.assertEqual(data["registration_url"], "")
        self.assertIsNone(data["start_time"])
        self.assertIsNone(data["end_time"])
        self.assertIsNone(data["latitude"])
        self.assertIsNone(data["longitude"])

    def test_malformed_values_are_tolerated(self):
        """Unparseable times and coordinates become None instead of crashing."""
        data = parse_record(
            feed_record(starttime="not-a-date", coordinates="over there")
        )
        self.assertIsNone(data["start_time"])
        self.assertIsNone(data["latitude"])
        self.assertIsNone(data["longitude"])

    def test_nyc_times_become_aware_datetimes(self):
        """Naive feed timestamps are interpreted in New York City time."""
        data = parse_record(feed_record())
        self.assertEqual(
            data["start_time"],
            datetime(2099, 1, 15, 9, 0, tzinfo=NYC),
        )
        self.assertEqual(data["start_time"].utcoffset(), timedelta(hours=-5))

    def test_record_without_guid_or_title_raises(self):
        """A record that cannot identify an event aborts the whole sync."""
        for broken in (feed_record(guid=""), feed_record(title=" ")):
            with self.subTest(record=broken):
                with self.assertRaisesMessage(SyncError, "missing a guid or title"):
                    parse_record(broken)


class SyncTests(TestCase):
    """Syncs upsert by guid, skip unchanged rows, and mark finished events."""

    def test_new_records_are_created(self):
        """Every feed record becomes an upcoming event with audit fields."""
        counts = run_sync([feed_record(), feed_record(guid="1002", title="Birdwalk")])

        self.assertEqual(
            counts,
            {
                "fetched": 2,
                "created": 2,
                "updated": 0,
                "unchanged": 0,
                "marked_done": 0,
            },
        )
        event = Event.objects.get(guid="1001")
        self.assertEqual(event.title, "Morning Yoga")
        self.assertEqual(event.status, Event.Status.UPCOMING)
        self.assertEqual(event.latitude, 40.6602)
        self.assertEqual(event.source_version, "rv-1")
        self.assertEqual(len(event.content_hash), 64)
        self.assertIsNotNone(event.first_seen_at)
        self.assertIsNotNone(event.last_synced_at)

    def test_unchanged_records_are_not_rewritten(self):
        """A second identical sync writes nothing for existing rows."""
        run_sync([feed_record()])
        event = Event.objects.get(guid="1001")
        event.last_synced_at = timezone.now() - timedelta(hours=2)
        event.save()
        stale_synced_at = event.last_synced_at

        counts = run_sync([feed_record()])

        self.assertEqual(counts["unchanged"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(counts["updated"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(counts["created"], 0)
        event.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(event.last_synced_at, stale_synced_at)

    def test_source_version_changes_alone_do_not_count_as_changes(self):
        """The feed rewrites every row's version stamp, so it is ignored."""
        run_sync([feed_record()])
        counts = run_sync([feed_record(**{":version": "rv-2"})])

        self.assertEqual(counts["unchanged"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(counts["updated"], 0)

    def test_changed_records_are_updated_in_place(self):
        """Edited feed content replaces the stored copy for the same guid."""
        run_sync([feed_record()])
        counts = run_sync(
            [
                feed_record(
                    title="Morning Yoga (Moved)",
                    endtime="2099-01-15T11:00:00.000",
                    **{":version": "rv-2"},
                )
            ]
        )

        self.assertEqual(counts["updated"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(counts["created"], 0)
        event = Event.objects.get(guid="1001")
        self.assertEqual(event.title, "Morning Yoga (Moved)")
        self.assertEqual(event.end_time, datetime(2099, 1, 15, 11, 0, tzinfo=NYC))

    def test_records_that_left_the_feed_are_kept(self):
        """Events stay in the DB after the rolling feed drops them."""
        Event.objects.create(
            guid="old-1",
            title="Last Week's Fair",
            content_hash="c" * 64,
            status=Event.Status.DONE,
        )

        counts = run_sync([feed_record()])

        self.assertEqual(counts["fetched"], 1)
        self.assertTrue(Event.objects.filter(guid="old-1").exists())

    def test_finished_events_are_marked_done(self):
        """Upcoming events whose end time has passed become done."""
        Event.objects.create(
            guid="past-1",
            title="Yesterday's Concert",
            content_hash="d" * 64,
            status=Event.Status.UPCOMING,
            start_time=timezone.now() - timedelta(days=1),
            end_time=timezone.now() - timedelta(hours=1),
        )

        counts = run_sync([feed_record()])

        self.assertEqual(counts["marked_done"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(Event.objects.get(guid="past-1").status, Event.Status.DONE)

    def test_events_without_end_time_are_not_marked_done(self):
        """An undated event never looks finished to the sync."""
        Event.objects.create(
            guid="open-1",
            title="Ongoing Exhibit",
            content_hash="e" * 64,
            status=Event.Status.UPCOMING,
            end_time=None,
        )

        counts = run_sync([feed_record()])

        self.assertEqual(counts["marked_done"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(Event.objects.get(guid="open-1").status, Event.Status.UPCOMING)

    def test_parse_failure_leaves_existing_rows_untouched(self):
        """One broken record aborts the sync before the DB is written."""
        Event.objects.create(guid="1001", title="Old Title", content_hash="f" * 64)

        body = BytesIO(json.dumps([feed_record(), {"title": "No guid"}]).encode())
        with patch("events.services.urlopen", return_value=body):
            with self.assertRaisesMessage(SyncError, "missing a guid or title"):
                sync_events()

        self.assertEqual(Event.objects.get(guid="1001").title, "Old Title")
        self.assertEqual(Event.objects.count(), 1)

FILE: events\tests\test_views.py
