"""Tests for the landing page and its event list."""

from datetime import datetime
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.test import TestCase
from django.urls import reverse

from events.models import Event
from events.views import (
    ARCHIVED_EVENT_PAGE_SIZE,
    UPCOMING_EVENT_LIMIT,
)

NYC = ZoneInfo("America/New_York")


def make_event(guid, title, **kwargs):
    """Create an event row with sensible defaults for view tests."""
    kwargs.setdefault("content_hash", "v" * 64)
    return Event.objects.create(guid=guid, title=title, **kwargs)


class LandingViewTests(TestCase):
    """The landing page shows events to signed-in visitors only."""

    @classmethod
    def setUpTestData(cls):
        cls.user = get_user_model().objects.create_user(
            username="alice", email="alice@example.com", password="pass12345"
        )

    def setUp(self):
        self.alpha_event = make_event(
            "a1",
            "Alpha Yoga",
            start_time=datetime(2099, 1, 15, 9, 0, tzinfo=NYC),
            end_time=datetime(2099, 1, 15, 10, 0, tzinfo=NYC),
            location="Prospect Park",
            categories="Fitness | Yoga",
            description="Stretch and breathe in the park.",
            event_url="https://example.org/alpha",
            registration_url="https://example.org/alpha/register",
            image_url="https://example.org/img/yoga.png",
        )
        make_event(
            "b2",
            "Beta Birdwatching",
            start_time=datetime(2099, 1, 16, 10, 0, tzinfo=NYC),
            end_time=datetime(2099, 1, 16, 12, 0, tzinfo=NYC),
            park_names="Central Park",
        )
        self.past_event = make_event(
            "c3",
            "Hidden Past Event",
            start_time=datetime(2099, 1, 14, 9, 0, tzinfo=NYC),
            status=Event.Status.DONE,
        )

    def test_anonymous_visitors_see_the_login_prompt(self):
        """Logged-out visitors get the welcome page without an event list."""
        response = self.client.get(reverse("landing"))

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "Please log in")
        self.assertNotContains(response, "Alpha Yoga")
        self.assertEqual(list(response.context["events"]), [])

    def test_signed_in_visitors_see_upcoming_events_in_order(self):
        """Upcoming events are listed soonest first;
        done events appear in the archive."""
        make_event(
            "d4",
            "Undated Event",
            start_time=None,
            end_time=datetime(2099, 1, 20, 9, 0, tzinfo=NYC),
        )
        self.client.login(username="alice", password="pass12345")

        response = self.client.get(reverse("landing"))

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(
            [event.title for event in response.context["events"]],
            ["Alpha Yoga", "Beta Birdwatching", "Undated Event"],
        )
        self.assertNotIn(self.past_event, response.context["events"])
        self.assertIn(self.past_event, response.context["archived_events"])
        self.assertContains(response, "Hidden Past Event")

    def test_event_card_shows_details(self):
        """Cards render the time in NYC local time, place, and useful links."""
        self.client.login(username="alice", password="pass12345")

        response = self.client.get(reverse("landing"))

        self.assertContains(response, "Jan 15")
        self.assertContains(response, "9:00 AM")
        self.assertContains(response, "10:00 AM")
        self.assertContains(response, "Prospect Park")
        self.assertContains(response, "Fitness | Yoga")
        self.assertContains(
            response,
            f'href="{reverse("event_detail", args=[self.alpha_event.pk])}"',
        )
        self.assertContains(response, ">Register</a>")
        self.assertContains(response, "Stretch and breathe in the park.")

    def test_images_use_the_stored_url_or_a_placeholder(self):
        """Events with an image link to it; the rest fall back to a placeholder."""
        self.client.login(username="alice", password="pass12345")

        response = self.client.get(reverse("landing"))
        placeholder = "/static/events/no-image.svg"

        self.assertContains(
            response,
            'src="https://example.org/img/yoga.png"',
        )
        self.assertContains(response, f'data-placeholder="{placeholder}"')
        self.assertContains(response, f'src="{placeholder}"')

    def test_empty_state_message_is_shown_without_events(self):
        """A brand-new install explains that no events are synced yet."""
        Event.objects.all().delete()
        self.client.login(username="alice", password="pass12345")

        response = self.client.get(reverse("landing"))

        self.assertContains(response, "No upcoming events yet.")

    def test_cancelled_events_show_a_label_instead_of_register(self):
        """A "CANCELLED" title swaps the registration link for a plain label."""
        cancelled = make_event(
            "e5",
            "CANCELLED: Twilight Kayak Tour",
            start_time=datetime(2099, 1, 17, 19, 0, tzinfo=NYC),
            registration_url="https://example.org/e5/register",
        )
        self.client.login(username="alice", password="pass12345")

        response = self.client.get(reverse("landing"))

        self.assertContains(response, "Cancelled")
        self.assertNotContains(response, f'href="{cancelled.registration_url}"')

    def test_event_list_is_capped(self):
        """Only the first 20 upcoming events are loaded per page view."""
        for index in range(UPCOMING_EVENT_LIMIT + 5):
            make_event(
                f"bulk-{index}",
                f"Bulk Event {index}",
                start_time=datetime(2099, 2, 1 + index, 9, 0, tzinfo=NYC),
            )
        self.client.login(username="alice", password="pass12345")

        response = self.client.get(reverse("landing"))

        self.assertEqual(len(response.context["events"]), UPCOMING_EVENT_LIMIT)

    def test_archived_events_are_shown_on_landing_page(self):
        """The landing page displays completed events in its archive section."""
        self.client.login(username="alice", password="pass12345")

        response = self.client.get(reverse("landing"))

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "Archived events")
        self.assertContains(response, "Hidden Past Event")
        self.assertIn(self.past_event, response.context["archived_events"])


class ArchivedEventsViewTests(TestCase):
    """Tests for the archived events page."""

    def setUp(self):
        self.user = get_user_model().objects.create_user(
            username="archive_user",
            email="archive@example.com",
            password="pass12345",
        )

        self.old_event = make_event(
            "archive-1",
            "Older Completed Event",
            start_time=datetime(2099, 1, 10, 9, 0, tzinfo=NYC),
            status=Event.Status.DONE,
        )

        self.new_event = make_event(
            "archive-2",
            "Newer Completed Event",
            start_time=datetime(2099, 1, 20, 9, 0, tzinfo=NYC),
            status=Event.Status.DONE,
        )

        self.upcoming_event = make_event(
            "archive-3",
            "Upcoming Event",
            start_time=datetime(2099, 2, 10, 9, 0, tzinfo=NYC),
            status=Event.Status.UPCOMING,
        )

        self.cancelled_event = make_event(
            "archive-4",
            "Cancelled Event",
            start_time=datetime(2099, 1, 15, 9, 0, tzinfo=NYC),
            status=Event.Status.CANCELLED,
        )

    def test_anonymous_users_are_redirected_to_login(self):
        """Anonymous visitors must log in to access the archive."""
        response = self.client.get(reverse("archived_events"))

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 302)
        self.assertIn("/accounts/login/", response.url)

    def test_archived_page_shows_only_completed_events(self):
        """The archive contains completed events, not upcoming or cancelled ones."""
        self.client.login(username="archive_user", password="pass12345")

        response = self.client.get(reverse("archived_events"))

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "Older Completed Event")
        self.assertContains(response, "Newer Completed Event")
        self.assertNotContains(response, "Upcoming Event")
        self.assertNotContains(response, "Cancelled Event")

    def test_archived_events_are_sorted_newest_first(self):
        """More recently completed events appear before older events."""
        self.client.login(username="archive_user", password="pass12345")

        response = self.client.get(reverse("archived_events"))

        self.assertEqual(
            [event.title for event in response.context["events"]],
            ["Newer Completed Event", "Older Completed Event"],
        )

    def test_archived_events_can_be_searched_by_keyword(self):
        """Keyword search returns matching completed events only."""
        matching_event = make_event(
            "archive-yoga",
            "Community Yoga",
            status=Event.Status.DONE,
        )
        make_event(
            "archive-upcoming-yoga",
            "Upcoming Yoga",
            status=Event.Status.UPCOMING,
        )
        self.client.login(username="archive_user", password="pass12345")
        response = self.client.get(
            reverse("archived_events"),
            {"q": "Yoga"},
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(
            list(response.context["events"]),
            [matching_event],
        )
        self.assertContains(response, "Community Yoga")
        self.assertNotContains(response, "Upcoming Yoga")
        self.assertEqual(response.context["query"], "Yoga")

    def test_archived_events_can_be_filtered_by_date(self):
        """Date filters narrow the completed events."""
        self.client.login(username="archive_user", password="pass12345")

        response = self.client.get(
            reverse("archived_events"),
            {
                "date_from": "2099-01-19",
                "date_to": "2099-01-21",
            },
        )

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(
            list(response.context["events"]),
            [self.new_event],
        )
        self.assertTrue(response.context["has_filters"])

    def test_archived_events_can_be_filtered_by_category(self):
        """Category filters return matching completed events."""
        yoga_event = make_event(
            "archive-category-yoga",
            "Yoga Session",
            categories="Yoga & Pilates",
            status=Event.Status.DONE,
        )

        make_event(
            "archive-category-art",
            "Art Workshop",
            categories="Arts & Crafts",
            status=Event.Status.DONE,
        )

        self.client.login(username="archive_user", password="pass12345")

        response = self.client.get(
            reverse("archived_events"),
            {"category": "yoga"},
        )

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(
            list(response.context["events"]),
            [yoga_event],
        )

    def test_archived_events_can_be_filtered_by_borough(self):
        """Borough filters return matching completed events."""
        brooklyn_event = make_event(
            "archive-brooklyn",
            "Brooklyn Park Event",
            borough="brooklyn",
            status=Event.Status.DONE,
        )

        make_event(
            "archive-queens",
            "Queens Park Event",
            borough="queens",
            status=Event.Status.DONE,
        )

        self.client.login(username="archive_user", password="pass12345")

        response = self.client.get(
            reverse("archived_events"),
            {"borough": "brooklyn"},
        )

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(
            list(response.context["events"]),
            [brooklyn_event],
        )

    def test_archived_events_can_combine_search_and_filters(self):
        """Keyword search and category filters work together."""
        matching_event = make_event(
            "archive-combined-yoga",
            "Community Yoga",
            categories="Yoga & Pilates",
            status=Event.Status.DONE,
        )

        make_event(
            "archive-combined-art",
            "Community Art",
            categories="Arts & Crafts",
            status=Event.Status.DONE,
        )

        self.client.login(username="archive_user", password="pass12345")

        response = self.client.get(
            reverse("archived_events"),
            {
                "q": "Community",
                "category": "yoga",
            },
        )

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(
            list(response.context["events"]),
            [matching_event],
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.context["query"], "Community")

    def test_archived_events_pagination_preserves_search_parameters(self):
        """Pagination links retain the active search and filters."""
        for index in range(ARCHIVED_EVENT_PAGE_SIZE + 1):
            make_event(
                f"archive-page-{index}",
                f"Community Event {index}",
                categories="Yoga & Pilates",
                status=Event.Status.DONE,
            )

        self.client.login(username="archive_user", password="pass12345")

        response = self.client.get(
            reverse("archived_events"),
            {
                "q": "Community",
                "category": "yoga",
            },
        )

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertTrue(response.context["is_paginated"])
        self.assertEqual(
            response.context["paginator"].count,
            ARCHIVED_EVENT_PAGE_SIZE + 1,
        )

        self.assertContains(
            response,
            "q=Community&amp;category=yoga&amp;page=2",
        )

    def test_archived_events_show_empty_state_for_no_matches(self):
        """The archive explains when a search has no matching events."""
        self.client.login(username="archive_user", password="pass12345")

        response = self.client.get(
            reverse("archived_events"),
            {"q": "NonexistentActivity"},
        )

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(len(response.context["events"]), 0)
        self.assertContains(response, "No archived events match your search.")

    def test_archived_events_handle_invalid_date_filter(self):
        """Invalid filter input keeps the form errors visible."""
        self.client.login(username="archive_user", password="pass12345")

        response = self.client.get(
            reverse("archived_events"),
            {"date_from": "not-a-date"},
        )

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertTrue(response.context["filter_form"].errors)


class EventDetailViewTests(TestCase):
    """Tests for the event details page."""

    def setUp(self):
        self.event = make_event(
            "detail-1",
            "Community Yoga",
            start_time=datetime(2099, 3, 15, 9, 0, tzinfo=NYC),
            end_time=datetime(2099, 3, 15, 10, 0, tzinfo=NYC),
            location="Prospect Park",
            categories="Fitness | Yoga",
            description="Join us for a community yoga session.",
            event_url="https://example.org/yoga",
            registration_url="https://example.org/yoga/register",
            image_url="https://example.org/yoga.png",
        )
        self.url = reverse("event_detail", args=[self.event.pk])

    def test_event_details_page_displays_event_information(self):
        """The details page displays the selected event's information."""
        response = self.client.get(self.url)

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(response.context["event"], self.event)
        self.assertContains(response, "Community Yoga")
        self.assertContains(response, "Prospect Park")
        self.assertContains(response, "Fitness | Yoga")
        self.assertContains(response, "Join us for a community yoga session.")

    def test_event_details_page_shows_available_links(self):
        """Official and registration links open in the current tab."""
        response = self.client.get(self.url)

        self.assertContains(response, 'href="https://example.org/yoga"')
        self.assertContains(response, 'href="https://example.org/yoga/register"')
        self.assertNotContains(response, 'target="_blank"')

    def test_missing_event_information_is_handled(self):
        """Missing optional information does not break the details page."""
        event = make_event("detail-2", "Event With Missing Information")
        response = self.client.get(reverse("event_detail", args=[event.pk]))

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "Event With Missing Information")
        self.assertContains(response, "To be announced")
        self.assertContains(response, "No description is available for this event.")
        self.assertContains(response, 'alt="No image available"')
        self.assertNotContains(response, ">Register</a>")
        self.assertNotContains(response, ">Official Event Page</a>")

    def test_missing_event_returns_404(self):
        """A nonexistent event ID returns a 404 response."""
        response = self.client.get(reverse("event_detail", args=[999999999]))

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 404)

    def test_community_section_is_displayed(self):
        """The event detail page displays the community features."""
        response = self.client.get(reverse("event_detail", args=[self.event.pk]))

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "Connect with Other Attendees")
        self.assertContains(response, "Attendees who share their profiles")
        self.assertContains(response, "Discuss This Event")
        self.assertContains(response, "No discussion posts yet.")

"""URL patterns for hosted events and event community actions."""

from django.urls import path

from reports.views import report_event, report_post, report_reply

from . import community_views
from .views import (
    EventCreateView,
    EventUpdateView,
    MyEventsView,
    cancel_event,
    notify_attendees,
    ArchivedEventsView,
)

urlpatterns = [
    path("new/", EventCreateView.as_view(), name="event_create"),
    path("mine/", MyEventsView.as_view(), name="my_events"),
    path("<int:pk>/edit/", EventUpdateView.as_view(), name="event_edit"),
    path("<int:pk>/notify/", notify_attendees, name="event_notify"),
    path("<int:pk>/cancel/", cancel_event, name="event_cancel"),
    path("archived/", ArchivedEventsView.as_view(), name="archived_events"),
    path("<int:pk>/report/", report_event, name="event_report"),
    path("posts/<int:post_id>/report/", report_post, name="event_post_report"),
    path("replies/<int:reply_id>/report/", report_reply, name="event_reply_report"),
    path(
        "<int:event_id>/interest/",
        community_views.toggle_event_interest,
        name="toggle_event_interest",
    ),
    path(
        "<int:event_id>/posts/",
        community_views.create_event_post,
        name="create_event_post",
    ),
    path(
        "posts/<int:post_id>/replies/",
        community_views.create_event_reply,
        name="create_event_reply",
    ),
    path(
        "<int:event_id>/contact/<int:user_id>/",
        community_views.send_contact_request,
        name="send_contact_request",
    ),
    path(
        "contact-requests/<int:request_id>/<str:action>/",
        community_views.respond_to_contact_request,
        name="respond_to_contact_request",
    ),
]

"""Views for the landing page, public event search, and hosted events."""

from urllib.parse import quote, urlencode
from uuid import uuid4

from django.contrib import messages
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.contrib.auth.mixins import LoginRequiredMixin
from django.db import transaction
from django.db.models import Case, F, Value, When
from django.http import Http404, QueryDict
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404, redirect, render
from django.urls import reverse
from django.utils import timezone
from django.views.decorators.http import require_http_methods, require_POST
from django.views.generic import (
    CreateView,
    DetailView,
    ListView,
    TemplateView,
    UpdateView,
)

from .filters import EventFilterForm, filter_events, filter_params
from .forms import CancelEventForm, EventListingForm, NotifyAttendeesForm
from .models import ContactRequest, Event, EventInterest, EventPost, Notification
from .notifications import (
    cancel_notice,
    change_notice,
    interested_recipients,
    notify_interested,
)
from .search import search_events

UPCOMING_EVENT_LIMIT = 20
SEARCH_PAGE_SIZE = 20
NOTIFICATION_PAGE_SIZE = 20
ARCHIVED_EVENT_PREVIEW_LIMIT = 3
ARCHIVED_EVENT_PAGE_SIZE = 20


class EventCreateView(LoginRequiredMixin, CreateView):
    """Let a signed-in user post an event that everyone can discover."""

    model = Event
    form_class = EventListingForm
    template_name = "events/event_form.html"

    def form_valid(self, form):
        """Make the current user the host and give the event its own ID."""
        form.instance.host = self.request.user
        form.instance.guid = Event.HOSTED_GUID_PREFIX + uuid4().hex
        response = super().form_valid(form)
        messages.success(self.request, "Your event is posted.")
        return response

    def get_success_url(self):
        """Show the new event's detail page."""
        return reverse("event_detail", args=[self.object.pk])


def get_hosted_upcoming_event(user, pk):
    """Return an event the user hosts that has not ended or been cancelled.

    Other users' events, NYC Parks feed events, and events that have ended
    or been cancelled are reported as not found.
    """
    event = get_object_or_404(Event, pk=pk, host=user)
    if not event.is_open:
        raise Http404("This event has ended or was cancelled.")
    return event


def people(count):
    """Return e.g. "1 interested person" or "3 interested people"."""
    return f"{count} interested {'person' if count == 1 else 'people'}"


class EventUpdateView(LoginRequiredMixin, UpdateView):
    """Let a host edit their own event until it ends.

    Changing the date, time, or location notifies everyone interested.
    """

    form_class = EventListingForm
    template_name = "events/event_form.html"

    def get_object(self, queryset=None):
        """Only the host can edit, and only before the event ends."""
        return get_hosted_upcoming_event(self.request.user, self.kwargs["pk"])

    def form_valid(self, form):
        """Save the changes, notify interested users, and confirm on the page."""
        notice = change_notice(form.instance, form.initial, form.changed_data)
        response = super().form_valid(form)
        confirmation = "Event updated."
        if notice:
            event_url = self.request.build_absolute_uri(self.get_success_url())
            count = notify_interested(self.object, *notice, event_url=event_url)
            if count:
                confirmation += f" We let {people(count)} know about the change."
        messages.success(self.request, confirmation)
        return response

    def get_success_url(self):
        """Show the updated event's detail page."""
        return reverse("event_detail", args=[self.object.pk])


@login_required
@require_POST
def notify_attendees(request, pk):
    """Send the host's message to everyone interested in their event."""
    event = get_hosted_upcoming_event(request.user, pk)
    detail_url = reverse("event_detail", args=[event.pk])
    form = NotifyAttendeesForm(request.POST)
    if not form.is_valid():
        messages.error(request, "Write a subject and a message before sending.")
        return redirect(detail_url)
    count = notify_interested(
        event,
        form.cleaned_data["subject"],
        form.cleaned_data["message"],
        event_url=request.build_absolute_uri(detail_url),
    )
    if count:
        messages.success(request, f"Your message was sent to {people(count)}.")
    else:
        messages.info(request, "Nobody is interested in this event yet.")
    return redirect(detail_url)


@login_required
@require_http_methods(["GET", "POST"])
def cancel_event(request, pk):
    """Confirm, then cancel a host's event and tell everyone interested."""
    event = get_hosted_upcoming_event(request.user, pk)
    form = CancelEventForm(request.POST if request.method == "POST" else None)
    if form.is_bound and form.is_valid():
        detail_url = reverse("event_detail", args=[event.pk])
        with transaction.atomic():
            # Only one of two simultaneous submissions can make the change,
            # so attendees are never told twice.
            cancelled = Event.objects.filter(
                pk=event.pk, status=Event.Status.UPCOMING
            ).update(status=Event.Status.CANCELLED, updated_at=timezone.now())
            if not cancelled:
                raise Http404("This event was already cancelled.")
            event.status = Event.Status.CANCELLED
            count = notify_interested(
                event,
                *cancel_notice(event, form.cleaned_data["message"]),
                event_url=request.build_absolute_uri(detail_url),
            )
        confirmation = "Event cancelled."
        if count:
            confirmation += f" We let {people(count)} know."
        messages.success(request, confirmation)
        return redirect(detail_url)
    return render(
        request,
        "events/event_cancel.html",
        {
            "event": event,
            "form": form,
            "recipient_count": interested_recipients(event).count(),
        },
    )


class MyEventsView(LoginRequiredMixin, TemplateView):
    """List the events a user posted: upcoming ones, then archived ones."""

    template_name = "events/my_events.html"

    def get_context_data(self, **kwargs):
        """Split hosted events into upcoming (soonest first) and archived.

        Archived events are cancelled or over, most recent first.
        """
        context = super().get_context_data(**kwargs)
        events = list(
            self.request.user.hosted_events.order_by(
                F("start_time").asc(nulls_last=True), "end_time", "title", "pk"
            )
        )
        context["upcoming_events"] = [event for event in events if event.is_open]
        context["archived_events"] = [
            event for event in reversed(events) if not event.is_open
        ]
        return context


class NotificationListView(LoginRequiredMixin, ListView):
    """List contact requests first, newest first within each group.

    Displayed event updates become read. Pending contact requests stay new
    until the recipient resolves them.
    """

    template_name = "events/notifications.html"
    context_object_name = "notifications"
    paginate_by = NOTIFICATION_PAGE_SIZE

    def get_queryset(self):
        """Order both groups before pagination and load request context."""
        return (
            self.request.user.notifications.select_related(
                "event",
                "contact_request__sender",
            )
            .annotate(
                _request_priority=Case(
                    When(contact_request__isnull=False, then=Value(0)),
                    default=Value(1),
                )
            )
            .order_by("_request_priority", "-created_at", "-pk")
        )

    def get_context_data(self, **kwargs):
        """Mark only displayed event updates read."""
        context = super().get_context_data(**kwargs)
        shown = context["notifications"]
        unread = [
            n.pk for n in shown if n.read_at is None and n.contact_request_id is None
        ]
        Notification.objects.filter(pk__in=unread).update(read_at=timezone.now())
        return context


class EventDetailView(DetailView):
    """Display event details and its community content."""

    model = Event
    template_name = "events/detail.html"
    context_object_name = "event"

    def get_context_data(self, **kwargs):
        """Add attendance intentions, attendee profiles, and discussions."""
        context = super().get_context_data(**kwargs)

        event = self.object
        user = self.request.user
        is_authenticated = user.is_authenticated

        # Get all attendance intention records for this event.
        interests = EventInterest.objects.filter(event=event)

        # Count intentions directly from the database.
        context["interest_count"] = interests.count()
        context["interested_count"] = interests.filter(
            status=EventInterest.Status.INTERESTED
        ).count()
        context["want_to_attend_count"] = interests.filter(
            status=EventInterest.Status.WANT_TO_ATTEND
        ).count()

        # Get the current user's selected intention.
        current_interest = (
            interests.filter(user=user).first() if is_authenticated else None
        )

        context["user_has_interest"] = current_interest is not None
        context["current_interest_status"] = (
            current_interest.status if current_interest else ""
        )

        # Control whether users can interact with the event.
        context["can_interact"] = (
            is_authenticated and event.status == Event.Status.UPCOMING
        )
        context["can_manage"] = (
            is_authenticated and event.host_id == user.pk and event.is_open
        )
        context["host_cancelled"] = event.status == Event.Status.CANCELLED

        if context["can_manage"]:
            context["recipient_count"] = interested_recipients(event).count()

        # Find contact requests sent by the current user.
        request_by_recipient = {}

        if is_authenticated:
            requests = (
                ContactRequest.objects.filter(
                    event=event,
                    sender=user,
                )
                .exclude(status=ContactRequest.Status.DECLINED)
                .order_by("-created_at")
            )

            for contact_request in requests:
                request_by_recipient.setdefault(
                    contact_request.recipient_id,
                    contact_request,
                )

        # Include all users who have chosen to share their profiles.
        # Do not exclude the current user: their avatar should also move
        # between the two groups when they change their intention.
        visible_interests = (
            interests.filter(user__share_profile_with_attendees=True)
            .select_related("user")
            .order_by("user__display_name", "user__username")
        )

        attendees = [
            {
                "user": interest.user,
                "status": interest.status,
                "contact_request": request_by_recipient.get(interest.user_id),
            }
            for interest in visible_interests
        ]

        # Split the same attendee list into the two intention groups.
        context["interested_users"] = [
            attendee
            for attendee in attendees
            if attendee["status"] == EventInterest.Status.INTERESTED
        ]

        context["want_to_attend_users"] = [
            attendee
            for attendee in attendees
            if attendee["status"] == EventInterest.Status.WANT_TO_ATTEND
        ]

        context["attendees"] = attendees

        # Event discussion posts and replies.
        context["posts"] = (
            EventPost.objects.filter(event=event)
            .select_related("author")
            .prefetch_related("replies__author")
        )

        # Contact requests received by the current user.
        context["received_contact_requests"] = (
            ContactRequest.objects.filter(
                event=event,
                recipient=user,
                status=ContactRequest.Status.PENDING,
            ).select_related("sender")
            if is_authenticated
            else ContactRequest.objects.none()
        )

        return context


class LandingView(TemplateView):
    """Render the landing page with upcoming and archived events for signed-in visitors.

    Anonymous visitors see the same page with the login prompt, matching the
    behaviour the accounts tests expect, but no event lists.
    """

    template_name = "events/home.html"

    def get_context_data(self, **kwargs):
        """Add upcoming events, archived events, and an empty filter bar."""
        context = super().get_context_data(**kwargs)
        context["filter_form"] = EventFilterForm()
        context["events"] = []
        context["archived_events"] = []
        if self.request.user.is_authenticated:
            # Upcoming events, soonest first.
            context["events"] = Event.objects.filter(
                status=Event.Status.UPCOMING
            ).order_by(
                F("start_time").asc(nulls_last=True),
                "end_time",
                "title",
            )[
                :UPCOMING_EVENT_LIMIT
            ]

            # Recently completed events, newest first.
            context["archived_events"] = Event.objects.filter(
                status=Event.Status.DONE
            ).order_by(
                F("start_time").desc(nulls_last=True),
                "-end_time",
                "-title",
            )[
                :ARCHIVED_EVENT_PREVIEW_LIMIT
            ]

        return context


class EventSearchMixin:
    """Share search, filtering, and pagination behavior across event lists."""

    event_status = None
    default_ordering = ()
    empty_without_filters = False
    clear_filters_url_name = None
    search_action_url_name = None

    def get_queryset(self):
        """Filter events by status, keyword, and validated form fields."""
        self.query = self.request.GET.get("q", "").strip()

        form = EventFilterForm(self.request.GET)
        is_valid = form.is_valid()
        filters = form.cleaned_data

        self.filter_params = filter_params(filters)

        if is_valid:
            self.filter_form = EventFilterForm(QueryDict(urlencode(self.filter_params)))
        else:
            self.filter_form = form

        events = filter_events(
            Event.objects.filter(status=self.event_status),
            filters,
        )

        if self.query:
            return search_events(events, self.query)

        if self.empty_without_filters and not self.filter_params:
            return Event.objects.none()

        return events.order_by(*self.default_ordering)

    def get_context_data(self, **kwargs):
        """Preserve search and filter parameters in pagination and forms."""
        context = super().get_context_data(**kwargs)

        search_params = [("q", self.query)] if self.query else []

        context["query"] = self.query
        context["filter_form"] = self.filter_form
        context["has_filters"] = bool(self.filter_params)
        context["page_query"] = urlencode(
            search_params + self.filter_params,
            quote_via=quote,
        )

        if self.search_action_url_name:
            context["search_action"] = reverse(self.search_action_url_name)

        if self.query:
            context["clear_filters_url"] = "?" + urlencode(
                search_params,
                quote_via=quote,
            )
        else:
            context["clear_filters_url"] = reverse(self.clear_filters_url_name)

        return context


class ArchivedEventsView(
    EventSearchMixin,
    LoginRequiredMixin,
    ListView,
):
    """Display and search completed events in the public archive."""

    model = Event
    template_name = "events/archived_events.html"
    context_object_name = "events"
    paginate_by = ARCHIVED_EVENT_PAGE_SIZE

    event_status = Event.Status.DONE

    default_ordering = (
        F("start_time").desc(nulls_last=True),
        "-end_time",
        "-title",
        "-pk",
    )

    empty_without_filters = False
    clear_filters_url_name = "archived_events"
    search_action_url_name = "archived_events"


class SearchView(EventSearchMixin, ListView):
    """Let any visitor search and filter upcoming events."""

    template_name = "events/search.html"
    context_object_name = "events"
    paginate_by = SEARCH_PAGE_SIZE

    event_status = Event.Status.UPCOMING

    default_ordering = (
        F("start_time").asc(nulls_last=True),
        "end_time",
        "title",
        "pk",
    )
    empty_without_filters = True
    clear_filters_url_name = "landing"

FILE: reports\tests\test_admin.py

"""Moderation queue grouping, authorization, links, and resolution lifecycle."""

from datetime import timedelta

from django.contrib.auth.models import Permission
from django.db import connection
from django.test import Client, TestCase
from django.test.utils import CaptureQueriesContext
from django.urls import reverse
from django.utils import timezone

from accounts.models import User
from events.models import (
    ContactRequest,
    Conversation,
    DirectMessage,
    Event,
    EventPost,
    EventReply,
)
from reports.models import Report

NOTICE = "Conversations may be reviewed by administrators for moderation purposes."


class ReportAdminTests(TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpTestData(cls):
        cls.admin = User.objects.create_superuser(
            "moderator", "mod@example.com", "pass"
        )
        cls.viewer = User.objects.create_user(
            "viewer", "viewer@example.com", is_staff=True
        )
        cls.viewer.user_permissions.add(
            Permission.objects.get(
                codename="view_report", content_type__app_label="reports"
            )
        )
        cls.reporter = User.objects.create_user("reporter", "reporter@example.com")
        cls.subject = User.objects.create_user("subject", "subject@example.com")
        cls.event = Event.objects.create(guid="queue-event", title="Park walk")
        cls.post = EventPost.objects.create(
            event=cls.event, author=cls.subject, body="Reported comment"
        )
        cls.reply = EventReply.objects.create(
            post=cls.post, author=cls.subject, body="Reported reply"
        )
        cls.thread, _ = Conversation.get_or_create_for_users(cls.reporter, cls.subject)
        ContactRequest.objects.create(
            event=cls.event,
            sender=cls.reporter,
            recipient=cls.subject,
            status=ContactRequest.Status.ACCEPTED,
        )
        cls.message = DirectMessage.objects.create(
            conversation=cls.thread, sender=cls.subject, body="PRIVATE CHAT TEXT"
        )
        cls.url = reverse("admin:reports_report_changelist")

    def setUp(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.admin)

    def report(self, kind=Report.Kind.EVENT, **kwargs):
        fields = {
            "event": self.event,
            "event_title": self.event.title,
            "reporter": self.reporter,
            "reason": Report.Reason.OTHER,
            "details": "Report explanation",
            "kind": kind,
        }
        if kind == Report.Kind.POST:
            fields["reported_post"] = self.post
        elif kind == Report.Kind.REPLY:
            fields["reported_reply"] = self.reply
        elif kind in (Report.Kind.USER, Report.Kind.PROFILE):
            fields.update(event=None, reported_user=self.subject)
        fields.update(kwargs)
        return Report.objects.create(**fields)

    def groups(self, **params):
        response = self.client.get(self.url, params)
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        return response.context["groups"]

    def action(self, report, bulk=False, **params):
        name = "resolve_all" if bulk else "resolve"
        return self.client.post(
            reverse(f"admin:reports_report_{name}", args=[report.pk]), params
        )

    def test_all_kinds_group_by_primary_target_and_link_to_management(self):
        first = self.report()
        self.report(reporter=self.subject)
        for kind in ("post", "reply", "user", "profile"):
            self.report(kind)
        groups = self.groups()
        self.assertEqual(len(groups), 5)
        event_group = next(g for g in groups if g["kind"] == "event")
        self.assertEqual(event_group["report_count"], 2)
        self.assertEqual(len(event_group["reports"]), 2)
        self.assertIn(first, event_group["reports"])
        destinations = {
            "event": (
                "events_event",
                self.event.pk,
                reverse("event_detail", args=[self.event.pk]),
            ),
            "post": ("events_eventpost", self.post.pk, f"#comment-{self.post.pk}"),
            "reply": ("events_eventreply", self.reply.pk, f"#reply-{self.reply.pk}"),
            "user": (
                "accounts_user",
                self.subject.pk,
                reverse("public_profile", args=[self.subject.username]),
            ),
            "profile": (
                "accounts_user",
                self.subject.pk,
                reverse("public_profile", args=[self.subject.username]),
            ),
        }
        for group in groups:
            model, pk, public = destinations[group["kind"]]
            self.assertEqual(
                group["admin_url"], reverse(f"admin:{model}_change", args=[pk])
            )
            self.assertIn(public, group["public_url"])
            self.assertEqual(self.client.get(group["admin_url"]).status_code, 200)
        other = EventPost.objects.create(
            event=self.event, author=self.subject, body="Other"
        )
        self.report("post", reported_post=other)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.groups()), 6)

    def test_status_and_sorting_use_matching_reports(self):
        old = self.report()
        self.report(reporter=self.subject)
        other = Event.objects.create(guid="other", title="Other")
        recent = self.report(event=other)
        Report.objects.filter(pk=old.pk).update(
            created_at=timezone.now() - timedelta(days=3)
        )
        self.assertEqual(self.groups(sort="newest")[0]["target_id"], other.pk)
        self.assertEqual(self.groups(sort="oldest")[0]["target_id"], self.event.pk)
        self.assertEqual(self.groups(sort="most")[0]["report_count"], 2)
        self.assertEqual(self.groups(sort="fewest")[0]["report_count"], 1)
        self.action(recent)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.groups()), 1)
        self.assertEqual(self.groups(status="resolved")[0]["target_id"], other.pk)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.groups(status="all")), 2)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.groups(status="invalid", sort="invalid")), 1)

    def test_pagination_is_by_group_with_stable_ties(self):
        timestamp = timezone.now()
        for index in range(27):
            event = Event.objects.create(guid=f"page-{index}", title=f"Event {index}")
            self.report(event=event)
        Report.objects.update(created_at=timestamp)
        first = self.groups()
        second = self.groups(page=2)
        self.assertEqual(len(first), 25)
        self.assertEqual(len(second), 2)
        keys = [g["target_id"] for g in first + second]
        self.assertEqual(keys, sorted(set(keys)))

    def test_deleted_targets_are_singletons_and_deleted_users_are_safe(self):
        first = self.report()
        second = self.report(reporter=self.subject)
        self.event.delete()
        self.reporter.delete()
        groups = self.groups()
        self.assertEqual(len(groups), 2)
        self.assertTrue(
            all(g["summary"] == "Deleted target â€” Park walk" for g in groups)
        )
        self.assertTrue(
            all(g["admin_url"] is None and g["public_url"] is None for g in groups)
        )
        self.action(first, bulk=True)
        second.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertIsNone(second.resolved_at)
        self.assertContains(
            self.client.get(self.url, {"status": "all"}), "Deleted user"
        )

    def test_resolution_records_first_administrator_and_preserves_timestamp(self):
        report = self.report()
        response = self.action(report)
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 302)
        report.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(report.resolved_by, self.admin)
        original_time = report.resolved_at
        self.viewer.user_permissions.add(
            Permission.objects.get(codename="change_report")
        )
        self.client.force_login(self.viewer)
        self.action(report)
        report.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(report.resolved_by, self.admin)
        self.assertEqual(report.resolved_at, original_time)
        self.admin.delete()
        report.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertIsNone(report.resolved_by)
        self.assertEqual(report.resolved_at, original_time)
        self.assertContains(
            self.client.get(self.url, {"status": "resolved"}), "Unknown"
        )

    def test_bulk_only_resolves_exact_target_kind_and_includes_new_reports(self):
        report = self.report("user")
        second = self.report("user", reporter=self.subject)
        profile = self.report("profile")
        event = self.report()
        self.action(report)
        report.refresh_from_db()
        original_time = report.resolved_at
        newer = self.report("user")
        self.action(report, bulk=True)
        for item in (second, newer):
            item.refresh_from_db()
            self.assertEqual(item.resolved_by, self.admin)
            self.assertIsNotNone(item.resolved_at)
        for item in (profile, event):
            item.refresh_from_db()
            self.assertIsNone(item.resolved_at)
        report.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(report.resolved_at, original_time)

    def test_permissions_methods_csrf_and_no_generic_editing(self):
        report = self.report()
        action = reverse("admin:reports_report_resolve", args=[report.pk])
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get(action).status_code, 405)
        csrf_client = Client(enforce_csrf_checks=True)
        csrf_client.force_login(self.admin)
        self.assertEqual(csrf_client.post(action).status_code, 403)
        self.client.force_login(self.viewer)
        response = self.client.get(self.url)
        self.assertContains(response, "Report explanation")
        self.assertNotContains(response, "Manage event")
        self.assertNotContains(response, "Resolve report")
        self.assertEqual(self.action(report).status_code, 403)
        self.assertEqual(self.action(report, bulk=True).status_code, 403)
        self.client.force_login(self.reporter)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get(self.url).status_code, 302)
        self.subject.is_staff = True
        self.subject.save()
        self.client.force_login(self.subject)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get(self.url).status_code, 403)
        self.client.force_login(self.admin)
        self.assertEqual(
            self.client.get(reverse("admin:reports_report_add")).status_code, 403
        )
        self.client.post(
            reverse("admin:reports_report_change", args=[report.pk]),
            {"details": "Changed"},
        )
        report.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(report.details, "Report explanation")

    def test_escape_chat_privacy_and_notices(self):
        report = self.report(
            "user",
            conversation=self.thread,
            context_message=self.message,
            details="<script>alert('unsafe')</script>",
        )
        response = self.client.get(self.url)
        self.assertContains(response, "&lt;script&gt;")
        self.assertNotContains(response, "<script>alert")
        self.assertNotContains(response, self.message.body)
        self.assertNotContains(response, reverse("conversation", args=[self.thread.pk]))
        self.client.force_login(self.reporter)
        self.assertContains(
            self.client.get(reverse("conversation", args=[self.thread.pk])), NOTICE
        )
        report.delete()
        self.assertContains(
            self.client.get(reverse("conversation_report", args=[self.thread.pk])),
            NOTICE,
        )
        self.assertNotContains(
            self.client.get(reverse("event_report", args=[self.event.pk])), NOTICE
        )
        detail = self.client.get(reverse("event_detail", args=[self.event.pk]))
        self.assertContains(detail, f'id="comment-{self.post.pk}"')
        self.assertContains(detail, f'id="reply-{self.reply.pk}"')
        # The profile route still enforces the user's sharing preference.
        self.assertContains(
            self.client.get(reverse("public_profile", args=[self.subject.username])),
            "Profile Unavailable",
        )

    def test_queries_do_not_grow_with_group_count(self):
        self.report()
        with CaptureQueriesContext(connection) as initial:
            self.client.get(self.url)
        for index in range(5):
            event = Event.objects.create(guid=f"query-{index}", title="Extra")
            self.report(event=event)
        with CaptureQueriesContext(connection) as expanded:
            self.client.get(self.url)
        self.assertEqual(len(initial), len(expanded))

    def test_empty_feed_and_action_redirect_filters(self):
        self.assertContains(self.client.get(self.url), "No reports match this filter.")
        report = self.report()
        url = reverse("admin:reports_report_resolve", args=[report.pk])
        response = self.client.post(url + "?status=all&sort=most&page=2")
        self.assertEqual(response.url, self.url + "?status=all&sort=most&page=2")
        self.assertEqual(
            self.client.post(
                reverse("admin:reports_report_resolve", args=[999999])
            ).status_code,
            404,
        )
