"""Reporting public comments and users with reference-only chat context."""

from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from datetime import timedelta
from threading import Barrier
from unittest.mock import patch

from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.db import IntegrityError, connections, transaction
from django.test import Client, TestCase, TransactionTestCase
from django.urls import reverse
from django.utils import timezone

from events.models import (
    ContactRequest,
    Conversation,
    DirectMessage,
    Event,
    EventPost,
    EventReply,
)
from reports.forms import ReportForm
from reports.models import Report
from reports.services import record_report, unresolved_report_lookup

from .test_reporting import ReportTestData


class ContentReportTestData(ReportTestData):
    """An event discussion and an approved conversation with its author."""

    @classmethod
    def create_content_data(cls):
        cls.create_data()
        cls.outsider = get_user_model().objects.create_user(
            username="outsider", email="outsider@example.com"
        )
        cls.post = EventPost.objects.create(
            event=cls.event, author=cls.host, body="Public comment to report."
        )
        cls.reply = EventReply.objects.create(
            post=cls.post, author=cls.host, body="Public reply to report."
        )
        cls.own_post = EventPost.objects.create(
            event=cls.event, author=cls.reporter, body="My own comment."
        )
        cls.own_reply = EventReply.objects.create(
            post=cls.post, author=cls.reporter, body="My own reply."
        )
        cls.thread, _ = Conversation.get_or_create_for_users(cls.reporter, cls.host)
        cls.contact = ContactRequest.objects.create(
            event=cls.event,
            sender=cls.reporter,
            recipient=cls.host,
            status=ContactRequest.Status.ACCEPTED,
        )
        cls.targets = {
            Report.Kind.POST: cls.post,
            Report.Kind.REPLY: cls.reply,
            Report.Kind.USER: cls.host,
        }
        cls.urls = {
            Report.Kind.POST: reverse("event_post_report", args=[cls.post.pk]),
            Report.Kind.REPLY: reverse("event_reply_report", args=[cls.reply.pk]),
            Report.Kind.USER: reverse("conversation_report", args=[cls.thread.pk]),
        }
        cls.chat_url = reverse("conversation", args=[cls.thread.pk])

    def submit(self, report_kind, **fields):
        data = {"reason": Report.Reason.HARASSMENT_HATE, "details": "Please review."}
        data.update(fields)
        return self.client.post(self.urls[report_kind], data)

    def message(self, body="Private message", sender=None):
        return DirectMessage.objects.create(
            conversation=self.thread, sender=sender or self.host, body=body
        )


class PublicCommentReportingTests(ContentReportTestData, TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpTestData(cls):
        cls.create_content_data()

    def setUp(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.reporter)

    def test_menus_report_others_comments_and_replies_but_not_own_content(self):
        response = self.client.get(self.detail_url)
        self.assertContains(response, 'aria-label="Comment actions"')
        self.assertContains(response, 'aria-label="Reply actions"')
        for kind in (Report.Kind.POST, Report.Kind.REPLY):
            self.assertContains(response, f'href="{self.urls[kind]}"')
        self.assertNotContains(
            response, reverse("event_post_report", args=[self.own_post.pk])
        )
        self.assertNotContains(
            response, reverse("event_reply_report", args=[self.own_reply.pk])
        )
        self.assertContains(response, "/static/reports/menus.js")
        self.assertContains(response, "/static/reports/menus.css")

        self.client.logout()
        response = self.client.get(self.detail_url)
        for url in self.urls.values():
            if url != self.urls[Report.Kind.USER]:
                self.assertContains(response, f'href="{url}"')

    def test_shared_form_identifies_source_author_and_optional_details(self):
        for kind, comment in (
            (Report.Kind.POST, self.post),
            (Report.Kind.REPLY, self.reply),
        ):
            with self.subTest(kind=kind):
                response = self.client.get(self.urls[kind])
                self.assertTemplateUsed(response, "reports/report_form.html")
                self.assertContains(response, comment.body)
                self.assertContains(response, self.host.get_display_name())
                self.assertContains(response, f'<a href="{self.detail_url}">Cancel</a>')
                self.assertFalse(response.context["form"].fields["details"].required)
        self.assertFalse(Report.objects.exists())

    def test_post_and_reply_reports_derive_exact_target_author_and_event(self):
        for kind, target_field, comment in (
            (Report.Kind.POST, "reported_post", self.post),
            (Report.Kind.REPLY, "reported_reply", self.reply),
        ):
            with self.subTest(kind=kind):
                self.assertRedirects(self.submit(kind), self.detail_url)
                report = Report.objects.get(kind=kind)
                self.assertEqual(getattr(report, target_field), comment)
                self.assertEqual(report.reported_user, comment.author)
                self.assertEqual(report.reporter, self.reporter)
                self.assertEqual(report.event, self.event)
                self.assertEqual(report.event_title, self.event.title)
                self.assertIsNone(report.conversation_id)
                self.assertIsNone(report.context_message_id)

    def test_event_other_comments_replies_and_user_reports_are_independent(self):
        self.submit(Report.Kind.POST)
        self.assertRedirects(self.submit(Report.Kind.REPLY), self.detail_url)
        self.assertRedirects(
            self.client.post(self.report_url, {"reason": Report.Reason.OTHER}),
            self.detail_url,
        )
        another = EventPost.objects.create(
            event=self.event, author=self.host, body="Another comment."
        )
        self.assertRedirects(
            self.client.post(
                reverse("event_post_report", args=[another.pk]),
                {"reason": Report.Reason.OTHER},
            ),
            self.detail_url,
        )
        self.assertRedirects(self.submit(Report.Kind.USER), self.chat_url)
        self.assertEqual(Report.objects.count(), 5)

    def test_any_signed_in_user_can_report_comments_without_event_interest(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.outsider)
        for kind in (Report.Kind.POST, Report.Kind.REPLY):
            self.assertRedirects(self.submit(kind), self.detail_url)
        self.assertEqual(Report.objects.filter(reporter=self.outsider).count(), 2)

    def test_closed_and_ended_events_still_allow_comment_reports(self):
        for status in (Event.Status.DONE, Event.Status.CANCELLED):
            with self.subTest(status=status):
                self.event.status = status
                self.event.save(update_fields=["status"])
                response = self.client.get(self.detail_url)
                for kind in (Report.Kind.POST, Report.Kind.REPLY):
                    self.assertContains(response, f'href="{self.urls[kind]}"')
                    self.assertEqual(self.client.get(self.urls[kind]).status_code, 200)
                    self.assertRedirects(self.submit(kind), self.detail_url)
                Report.objects.update(resolved_at=timezone.now())

    def test_self_reports_are_rejected_for_comments_and_replies(self):
        urls = (
            reverse("event_post_report", args=[self.own_post.pk]),
            reverse("event_reply_report", args=[self.own_reply.pk]),
        )
        for url in urls:
            self.assertEqual(self.client.get(url).status_code, 404)
            self.assertEqual(
                self.client.post(url, {"reason": Report.Reason.OTHER}).status_code, 404
            )
        self.assertFalse(Report.objects.exists())

    def test_target_identity_and_context_cannot_be_forged(self):
        for kind in (Report.Kind.POST, Report.Kind.REPLY):
            with self.subTest(kind=kind):
                self.assertRedirects(
                    self.submit(
                        kind,
                        kind=Report.Kind.USER,
                        reported_post=self.own_post.pk,
                        reported_reply=self.own_reply.pk,
                        reported_user=self.outsider.pk,
                        reporter=self.host.pk,
                        event=self.event.pk + 100,
                        event_title="Forged title",
                        conversation=self.thread.pk,
                        context_message=9999,
                        resolved_at="2000-01-01T00:00:00Z",
                    ),
                    self.detail_url,
                )
                report = Report.objects.get(kind=kind)
                self.assertEqual(report.reported_user, self.host)
                self.assertEqual(report.reporter, self.reporter)
                self.assertEqual(report.event, self.event)
                self.assertIsNone(report.resolved_at)
                self.assertIsNone(report.conversation_id)

    def test_comment_excerpt_is_escaped(self):
        self.post.body = '<script>alert("comment")</script>'
        self.post.save(update_fields=["body"])
        response = self.client.get(self.urls[Report.Kind.POST])
        self.assertContains(response, "&lt;script&gt;")
        self.assertNotContains(response, self.post.body)


class ContentReportValidationTests(ContentReportTestData, TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpTestData(cls):
        cls.create_content_data()

    def setUp(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.reporter)

    def test_new_endpoints_require_login_and_preserve_return_destination(self):
        self.client.logout()
        for url in self.urls.values():
            for response in (
                self.client.get(url),
                self.client.post(url, {"reason": Report.Reason.OTHER}),
            ):
                self.assertRedirects(response, reverse("login") + "?next=" + url)
        self.assertFalse(Report.objects.exists())

    def test_all_new_forms_validate_reason_and_details_and_preserve_input(self):
        for kind, url in self.urls.items():
            for data, error_field in (
                ({"details": "Keep this explanation."}, "reason"),
                ({"reason": "invalid", "details": "Keep this explanation."}, "reason"),
                ({"reason": Report.Reason.OTHER, "details": "x" * 5001}, "details"),
            ):
                with self.subTest(kind=kind, error_field=error_field):
                    response = self.client.post(url, data)
                    self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
                    self.assertIn(error_field, response.context["form"].errors)
                    self.assertContains(response, data["details"])
                    self.assertFalse(Report.objects.exists())
            response = self.client.post(url, {"reason": Report.Reason.OTHER})
            expected_url = (
                self.chat_url if kind == Report.Kind.USER else self.detail_url
            )
            self.assertRedirects(response, expected_url)
            self.assertEqual(Report.objects.get(kind=kind).details, "")
            # Keep the no-write assertions above scoped to each form.
            Report.objects.all().delete()

    def test_missing_targets_return_404(self):
        for name in ("event_post_report", "event_reply_report", "conversation_report"):
            url = reverse(name, args=[999999])
            self.assertEqual(self.client.get(url).status_code, 404)
            self.assertEqual(
                self.client.post(url, {"reason": Report.Reason.OTHER}).status_code, 404
            )

    def test_new_endpoints_reject_other_http_methods(self):
        for url in self.urls.values():
            for method in ("put", "patch", "delete", "head", "options"):
                self.assertEqual(getattr(self.client, method)(url).status_code, 405)
        self.assertFalse(Report.objects.exists())

    def test_all_new_submissions_require_csrf_and_accept_a_valid_token(self):
        client = Client(enforce_csrf_checks=True)
        client.force_login(self.reporter)
        for kind, url in self.urls.items():
            with self.subTest(kind=kind):
                data = {"reason": Report.Reason.OTHER}
                self.assertEqual(client.post(url, data).status_code, 403)
                self.assertFalse(Report.objects.filter(kind=kind).exists())
                client.get(url)
                data["csrfmiddlewaretoken"] = client.cookies["csrftoken"].value
                self.assertEqual(client.post(url, data).status_code, 302)
                self.assertTrue(Report.objects.filter(kind=kind).exists())

    def test_duplicate_reports_preserve_original_and_resolution_allows_another(self):
        for kind, url in self.urls.items():
            with self.subTest(kind=kind):
                self.submit(kind)
                original = Report.objects.values().get(kind=kind)
                expected_url = (
                    self.chat_url if kind == Report.Kind.USER else self.detail_url
                )
                for response in (
                    self.client.get(url),
                    self.submit(kind, details="Replacement"),
                ):
                    self.assertRedirects(response, expected_url)
                    self.assertEqual(Report.objects.values().get(kind=kind), original)
                Report.objects.filter(kind=kind).update(resolved_at=timezone.now())
                self.assertEqual(self.client.get(url).status_code, 200)
                self.assertRedirects(
                    self.submit(kind, details="New incident"), expected_url
                )
                self.assertEqual(Report.objects.filter(kind=kind).count(), 2)
                self.assertEqual(
                    Report.objects.get(kind=kind, resolved_at=None).details,
                    "New incident",
                )


class ChatUserReportingTests(ContentReportTestData, TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpTestData(cls):
        cls.create_content_data()

    def setUp(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.reporter)

    def test_chat_menu_and_form_identify_other_user_without_showing_chat_contents(self):
        response = self.client.get(self.chat_url)
        self.assertContains(response, 'aria-label="Conversation actions"')
        self.assertContains(response, f'href="{self.urls[Report.Kind.USER]}"')
        self.assertContains(response, "Report user")
        message = self.message("Sensitive message not copied into report page.")
        response = self.client.get(self.urls[Report.Kind.USER])
        self.assertContains(response, self.host.get_display_name())
        self.assertContains(response, f'<a href="{self.chat_url}">Cancel</a>')
        self.assertNotContains(response, message.body)
        message.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertIsNone(message.read_at)
        self.assertFalse(Report.objects.exists())

    def test_submission_anchors_latest_message_at_submit_even_if_it_is_reporters(self):
        self.message("Message present when form opened.")
        self.client.get(self.urls[Report.Kind.USER])
        latest = self.message(
            "Reporter replies while report form is open.", sender=self.reporter
        )
        backdated = self.message("Inserted later but earlier in conversation order.")
        DirectMessage.objects.filter(pk=backdated.pk).update(
            created_at=timezone.now() - timedelta(days=1)
        )
        self.assertRedirects(self.submit(Report.Kind.USER), self.chat_url)
        report = Report.objects.get()
        self.assertEqual(report.kind, Report.Kind.USER)
        self.assertEqual(report.reported_user, self.host)
        self.assertEqual(report.conversation, self.thread)
        self.assertEqual(report.context_message, latest)
        self.assertEqual(report.event_title, "")
        self.assertIsNone(report.event_id)
        self.assertIsNone(report.reported_post_id)
        self.assertIsNone(report.reported_reply_id)
        self.assertNotIn(latest.body, Report.objects.values().get().values())

        self.message("Later arrival does not move the saved anchor.")
        self.submit(Report.Kind.USER, details="Replacement")
        report.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(report.context_message, latest)
        self.assertEqual(report.details, "Please review.")

    def test_message_id_breaks_timestamp_ties(self):
        first = self.message()
        latest = self.message()
        DirectMessage.objects.filter(pk__in=[first.pk, latest.pk]).update(
            created_at=first.created_at
        )
        self.submit(Report.Kind.USER)
        self.assertEqual(Report.objects.get().context_message, latest)

    def test_empty_conversation_has_user_and_conversation_but_no_anchor(self):
        self.assertRedirects(self.submit(Report.Kind.USER), self.chat_url)
        report = Report.objects.get()
        self.assertEqual(report.reported_user, self.host)
        self.assertEqual(report.conversation, self.thread)
        self.assertIsNone(report.context_message_id)

    def test_reporting_does_not_change_messages_or_conversation_activity(self):
        message = self.message()
        before_message = DirectMessage.objects.values().get(pk=message.pk)
        before_thread = Conversation.objects.values().get(pk=self.thread.pk)
        self.client.get(self.urls[Report.Kind.USER])
        self.submit(Report.Kind.USER)
        self.assertEqual(
            DirectMessage.objects.values().get(pk=message.pk), before_message
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            Conversation.objects.values().get(pk=self.thread.pk), before_thread
        )
        self.assertEqual(DirectMessage.objects.count(), 1)

    def test_nonparticipants_including_staff_cannot_report_chat_users(self):
        self.outsider.is_staff = True
        self.outsider.is_superuser = True
        self.outsider.save(update_fields=["is_staff", "is_superuser"])
        self.client.force_login(self.outsider)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get(self.urls[Report.Kind.USER]).status_code, 404)
        self.assertEqual(self.submit(Report.Kind.USER).status_code, 404)
        self.assertFalse(Report.objects.exists())

    def test_participants_need_an_accepted_contact_request(self):
        self.contact.status = ContactRequest.Status.PENDING
        self.contact.save(update_fields=["status"])
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get(self.urls[Report.Kind.USER]).status_code, 404)
        self.assertEqual(self.submit(Report.Kind.USER).status_code, 404)
        self.assertFalse(Report.objects.exists())

    def test_forged_user_conversation_and_message_ids_are_ignored(self):
        other_thread, _ = Conversation.get_or_create_for_users(self.host, self.outsider)
        other_message = DirectMessage.objects.create(
            conversation=other_thread,
            sender=self.outsider,
            body="Unrelated private message.",
        )
        anchor = self.message()
        self.assertRedirects(
            self.submit(
                Report.Kind.USER,
                reported_user=self.reporter.pk,
                conversation=other_thread.pk,
                context_message=other_message.pk,
                event=self.event.pk,
                reported_post=self.post.pk,
                reported_reply=self.reply.pk,
            ),
            self.chat_url,
        )
        report = Report.objects.get()
        self.assertEqual(report.reported_user, self.host)
        self.assertEqual(report.conversation, self.thread)
        self.assertEqual(report.context_message, anchor)
        self.assertIsNone(report.event_id)

    def test_both_participants_can_report_each_other(self):
        self.submit(Report.Kind.USER)
        self.client.force_login(self.host)
        self.assertRedirects(self.submit(Report.Kind.USER), self.chat_url)
        self.assertEqual(
            Report.objects.get(reporter=self.host).reported_user, self.reporter
        )
        self.assertEqual(Report.objects.count(), 2)

    def test_resolution_captures_fresh_context_without_changing_original(self):
        first = self.message("Original context")
        self.submit(Report.Kind.USER)
        original = Report.objects.get()
        original.resolved_at = timezone.now()
        original.save(update_fields=["resolved_at"])
        latest = self.message("New context")
        self.submit(Report.Kind.USER)
        self.assertEqual(Report.objects.get(resolved_at=None).context_message, latest)
        original.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(original.context_message, first)

    def test_confirmations_and_duplicate_feedback_are_rendered_in_chat(self):
        for expected in (
            "Your report has been submitted.",
            "You have already reported this user.",
        ):
            response = self.client.post(
                self.urls[Report.Kind.USER],
                {"reason": Report.Reason.OTHER},
                follow=True,
            )
            self.assertRedirects(response, self.chat_url)
            self.assertContains(response, expected)
            self.assertContains(response, 'class="message-alerts" role="status"')

    def test_recreated_conversation_does_not_bypass_unresolved_user_report(self):
        self.submit(Report.Kind.USER)
        original = Report.objects.get()
        self.thread.delete()
        thread, _ = Conversation.get_or_create_for_users(self.reporter, self.host)
        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("conversation_report", args=[thread.pk]),
            {"reason": Report.Reason.OTHER},
        )
        self.assertRedirects(response, reverse("conversation", args=[thread.pk]))
        original.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertIsNone(original.conversation_id)
        self.assertEqual(Report.objects.count(), 1)


class ContentReportHistoryTests(ContentReportTestData, TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpTestData(cls):
        cls.create_content_data()

    def setUp(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.reporter)

    def test_database_enforces_each_target_constraint_independently(self):
        targets = {Report.Kind.EVENT: self.event, **self.targets}
        data = {"reason": Report.Reason.OTHER, "details": "Original"}
        for kind, target in targets.items():
            with self.subTest(kind=kind):
                original, created = record_report(
                    self.reporter, kind, target, data, conversation=self.thread
                )
                self.assertTrue(created)
                with self.assertRaises(IntegrityError), transaction.atomic():
                    Report.objects.create(
                        **unresolved_report_lookup(self.reporter, kind, target),
                        reason=Report.Reason.SPAM_SCAM,
                    )
                self.assertEqual(Report.objects.get(kind=kind), original)
                if kind != Report.Kind.EVENT:
                    self.assertIn(
                        f"{original.get_kind_display()} report #{original.pk}",
                        str(original),
                    )
        self.assertEqual(Report.objects.count(), 4)

    def test_deleting_parent_comment_clears_post_and_reply_references(self):
        self.submit(Report.Kind.POST)
        self.submit(Report.Kind.REPLY)
        self.post.delete()
        for report in Report.objects.all():
            self.assertIsNone(report.reported_post_id)
            self.assertIsNone(report.reported_reply_id)
            self.assertEqual(report.reported_user, self.host)
            self.assertEqual(report.event, self.event)
            self.assertEqual(report.details, "Please review.")
        self.assertEqual(Report.objects.count(), 2)

    def test_deleting_event_keeps_comment_reports_and_clears_deleted_sources(self):
        self.submit(Report.Kind.POST)
        self.submit(Report.Kind.REPLY)
        self.event.delete()
        for report in Report.objects.all():
            self.assertIsNone(report.event_id)
            self.assertIsNone(report.reported_post_id)
            self.assertIsNone(report.reported_reply_id)
            self.assertEqual(report.reported_user, self.host)
        self.assertEqual(Report.objects.count(), 2)

    def test_deleting_anchor_keeps_report_and_conversation(self):
        message = self.message()
        self.submit(Report.Kind.USER)
        message.delete()
        report = Report.objects.get()
        self.assertIsNone(report.context_message_id)
        self.assertEqual(report.conversation, self.thread)
        self.assertEqual(report.details, "Please review.")

    def test_deleting_conversation_clears_chat_context_but_keeps_report(self):
        self.message()
        self.submit(Report.Kind.USER)
        self.thread.delete()
        report = Report.objects.get()
        self.assertIsNone(report.conversation_id)
        self.assertIsNone(report.context_message_id)
        self.assertEqual(report.reported_user, self.host)
        self.assertIsNotNone(report.created_at)

    def test_deleting_reported_user_retains_all_reports_with_cleared_links(self):
        self.message()
        for kind in self.urls:
            self.submit(kind)
        self.host.delete()
        for report in Report.objects.all():
            self.assertIsNone(report.reported_user_id)
            self.assertIsNone(report.reported_post_id)
            self.assertIsNone(report.reported_reply_id)
            self.assertIsNone(report.conversation_id)
            self.assertIsNone(report.context_message_id)
            self.assertEqual(report.reporter, self.reporter)
        self.assertEqual(Report.objects.count(), 3)

    def test_deleting_reporter_retains_all_report_types(self):
        for kind in self.urls:
            self.submit(kind)
        self.reporter.delete()
        for report in Report.objects.all():
            self.assertIsNone(report.reporter_id)
            self.assertEqual(report.reported_user, self.host)
            self.assertEqual(report.details, "Please review.")
        self.assertEqual(Report.objects.count(), 3)


class ConcurrentContentReportTests(ContentReportTestData, TransactionTestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.create_content_data()

    def test_concurrent_submissions_preserve_one_report_per_new_target_kind(self):
        for kind, url in self.urls.items():
            with self.subTest(kind=kind):
                clients = [Client(), Client()]
                for client in clients:
                    client.force_login(self.reporter)
                ready = Barrier(2, timeout=15)
                original_is_valid = ReportForm.is_valid

                def validate_together(form):
                    valid = original_is_valid(form)
                    ready.wait()
                    return valid

                def submit(item):
                    client, details = item
                    try:
                        return client.post(
                            url, {"reason": Report.Reason.OTHER, "details": details}
                        )
                    finally:
                        connections.close_all()

                with patch.object(ReportForm, "is_valid", validate_together):
                    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
                        responses = list(
                            pool.map(submit, zip(clients, ("First", "Second")))
                        )
                expected_url = (
                    self.chat_url if kind == Report.Kind.USER else self.detail_url
                )
                for response in responses:
                    self.assertRedirects(
                        response, expected_url, fetch_redirect_response=False
                    )
                self.assertIn(
                    Report.objects.get(kind=kind).details, ("First", "Second")
                )
        self.assertEqual(Report.objects.count(), 3)



============================================================
FILE: reports\tests\test_conversation_admin.py
============================================================
"""Private conversation review is read-only and gated by open chat reports."""

from urllib.parse import parse_qs, urlsplit

from django.contrib import admin
from django.contrib.auth.models import Permission
from django.db import connection
from django.test import TestCase
from django.test.utils import CaptureQueriesContext
from django.urls import reverse
from django.utils import timezone

from accounts.models import User
from events.models import Conversation, DirectMessage
from reports.models import Report


class ConversationReviewTests(TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpTestData(cls):
        cls.moderator = User.objects.create_superuser("moderator", "mod@example.com")
        cls.viewer = User.objects.create_user(
            "viewer", "viewer@example.com", is_staff=True
        )
        cls.editor = User.objects.create_user(
            "editor", "editor@example.com", is_staff=True
        )
        cls.staff = User.objects.create_user(
            "staff", "staff@example.com", is_staff=True
        )
        cls.viewer.user_permissions.add(Permission.objects.get(codename="view_report"))
        cls.editor.user_permissions.add(
            Permission.objects.get(codename="change_report")
        )
        cls.first = User.objects.create_user("first", "first@example.com")
        cls.second = User.objects.create_user("second", "second@example.com")
        cls.third = User.objects.create_user("third", "third@example.com")
        cls.thread, _ = Conversation.get_or_create_for_users(cls.first, cls.second)
        cls.other_thread, _ = Conversation.get_or_create_for_users(
            cls.third, cls.second
        )
        cls.message = DirectMessage.objects.create(
            conversation=cls.thread, sender=cls.second, body="Original context"
        )
        cls.other_message = DirectMessage.objects.create(
            conversation=cls.other_thread, sender=cls.second, body="UNREPORTED SECRET"
        )
        cls.report = Report.objects.create(
            kind=Report.Kind.USER,
            reporter=cls.first,
            reported_user=cls.second,
            conversation=cls.thread,
            context_message=cls.message,
            reason=Report.Reason.HARASSMENT_HATE,
            details="Please review this conversation.",
        )
        cls.url = reverse("admin:reports_report_conversation", args=[cls.report.pk])
        cls.feed_url = reverse("admin:reports_report_changelist")

    def setUp(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.moderator)

    def extra_report(self, **fields):
        defaults = {
            "kind": Report.Kind.USER,
            "reporter": self.second,
            "reported_user": self.first,
            "conversation": self.thread,
            "reason": Report.Reason.OTHER,
        }
        defaults.update(fields)
        return Report.objects.create(**defaults)

    def test_permission_matrix_and_read_only_methods(self):
        for user in (self.moderator, self.viewer, self.editor):
            self.client.force_login(user)
            self.assertContains(
                self.client.get(self.url, follow=True), "Original context"
            )
            self.assertEqual(self.client.post(self.url).status_code, 405)
        self.client.force_login(self.staff)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get(self.url).status_code, 403)
        for user in (self.first, self.second):
            self.client.force_login(user)
            self.assertEqual(self.client.get(self.url).status_code, 302)
        self.moderator.is_active = False
        self.moderator.save()
        self.client.force_login(self.moderator)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get(self.url).status_code, 302)
        self.client.logout()
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get(self.url).status_code, 302)

    def test_access_requires_open_chat_report_for_exact_conversation(self):
        other = self.extra_report(
            reporter=self.third,
            reported_user=self.second,
            conversation=self.other_thread,
            resolved_at=timezone.now(),
        )
        other_url = reverse("admin:reports_report_conversation", args=[other.pk])
        # Reporting the same account elsewhere does not unlock this thread.
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get(other_url).status_code, 404)
        self.assertEqual(
            self.client.get(other_url, {"message_page": 1}).status_code, 404
        )
        # Even a populated conversation field on a non-chat report is insufficient.
        profile = self.extra_report(
            kind=Report.Kind.PROFILE, conversation=self.other_thread
        )
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get(other_url).status_code, 404)
        profile_url = reverse("admin:reports_report_conversation", args=[profile.pk])
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get(profile_url).status_code, 404)
        self.assertNotIn(Conversation, admin.site._registry)
        self.assertNotIn(DirectMessage, admin.site._registry)

    def test_access_ends_after_last_resolution_and_links_disappear(self):
        other = self.extra_report()
        self.client.post(reverse("admin:reports_report_resolve", args=[self.report.pk]))
        # The selected entry can be resolved while another report keeps access open.
        response = self.client.get(self.url, follow=True)
        self.assertContains(response, "Original context")
        self.assertContains(response, "Status: Resolved")
        self.assertContains(self.client.get(self.feed_url, {"status": "all"}), self.url)
        self.client.post(reverse("admin:reports_report_resolve", args=[other.pk]))
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get(self.url).status_code, 404)
        self.assertEqual(
            self.client.get(self.url, {"message_page": 1}).status_code, 404
        )
        self.assertNotContains(
            self.client.get(self.feed_url, {"status": "all"}), self.url
        )

    def test_missing_reports_and_conversations(self):
        self.assertEqual(
            self.client.get(
                reverse("admin:reports_report_conversation", args=[99999])
            ).status_code,
            404,
        )
        self.thread.delete()
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get(self.url).status_code, 404)
        self.assertNotContains(self.client.get(self.feed_url), "Review conversation")

    def test_report_removal_revokes_access(self):
        self.report.delete()
        self.assertEqual(
            self.client.get(self.url, {"message_page": 1}).status_code, 404
        )

    def test_initial_jump_and_pagination_with_timestamp_ties(self):
        messages = [self.message]
        messages += DirectMessage.objects.bulk_create(
            [
                DirectMessage(
                    conversation=self.thread, sender=self.first, body=f"Message {n}"
                )
                for n in range(2, 106)
            ]
        )
        self.thread.messages.update(created_at=timezone.now())
        self.report.context_message = messages[50]
        self.report.save(update_fields=["context_message"])
        response = self.client.get(self.url + "?status=all&sort=most&page=3")
        parsed = urlsplit(response.url)
        self.assertEqual(parsed.fragment, f"message-{messages[50].pk}")
        self.assertEqual(
            parse_qs(parsed.query),
            {
                "status": ["all"],
                "sort": ["most"],
                "page": ["3"],
                "message_page": ["2"],
            },
        )
        response = self.client.get(response.url)
        page = response.context["message_page"]
        self.assertEqual([m.pk for m in page], [m.pk for m in messages[50:100]])
        self.assertContains(response, "Report context message")
        self.assertContains(
            response, 'class="module conversation-message report-context-message"'
        )
        self.assertContains(response, "Earlier messages")
        self.assertContains(response, "Later messages")
        self.assertEqual(
            response.context["feed_url"], self.feed_url + "?status=all&sort=most&page=3"
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            len(self.client.get(self.url, {"message_page": 3}).context["message_page"]),
            5,
        )
        for invalid in ("bad", "9999"):
            self.assertEqual(
                self.client.get(self.url, {"message_page": invalid}).status_code, 200
            )
        # The 50th message remains on page one.
        self.report.context_message = messages[49]
        self.report.save(update_fields=["context_message"])
        self.assertIn("message_page=1#", self.client.get(self.url).url)

    def test_missing_or_foreign_context_defaults_to_latest_page(self):
        DirectMessage.objects.bulk_create(
            [
                DirectMessage(conversation=self.thread, sender=self.first, body="Later")
                for _ in range(51)
            ]
        )
        for context in (None, self.other_message):
            self.report.context_message = context
            self.report.save(update_fields=["context_message"])
            response = self.client.get(self.url)
            self.assertTrue(response.url.endswith("message_page=2"))
            response = self.client.get(response.url)
            self.assertContains(response, "context message is unavailable")
            self.assertNotContains(response, self.other_message.body)
            self.assertNotContains(response, "Report context</a>")
        self.thread.messages.all().delete()
        self.assertContains(
            self.client.get(self.url, follow=True), "This conversation has no messages."
        )

    def test_live_messages_escaping_and_no_state_changes(self):
        later = DirectMessage.objects.create(
            conversation=self.thread,
            sender=self.first,
            body="<script>unsafe()</script>\nLater message",
        )
        before = {
            model: list(model.objects.order_by("pk").values())
            for model in (Conversation, DirectMessage, Report)
        }
        response = self.client.get(self.url, follow=True)
        self.assertContains(response, "Original context")
        self.assertContains(response, "Later message")
        self.assertContains(response, "&lt;script&gt;unsafe()&lt;/script&gt;")
        self.assertNotContains(response, "<script>unsafe()")
        self.assertNotContains(response, self.other_message.body)
        self.assertContains(response, f'id="message-{later.pk}"')
        for model, values in before.items():
            self.assertEqual(list(model.objects.order_by("pk").values()), values)
        for hop, _ in response.redirect_chain:
            self.assertTrue(hop.startswith(self.url))
        for header in ("no-cache", "no-store", "private"):
            self.assertIn(header, response.headers["Cache-Control"])
        # Moderation did not bypass participant access on the main site.
        self.assertEqual(
            self.client.get(reverse("conversation", args=[self.thread.pk])).status_code,
            404,
        )

    def test_grouped_reports_link_to_their_own_conversations_without_extra_queries(
        self,
    ):
        response = self.client.get(self.feed_url)
        self.assertContains(response, self.url)
        self.assertNotContains(response, self.message.body)
        with CaptureQueriesContext(connection) as initial:
            self.client.get(self.feed_url)
        other = self.extra_report(
            reporter=self.third,
            reported_user=self.second,
            conversation=self.other_thread,
            context_message=self.other_message,
        )
        with CaptureQueriesContext(connection) as expanded:
            response = self.client.get(self.feed_url)
        self.assertEqual(len(initial), len(expanded))
        self.assertEqual(len(response.context["groups"]), 1)
        self.assertContains(response, self.url)
        self.assertContains(
            response, reverse("admin:reports_report_conversation", args=[other.pk])
        )
        self.assertNotContains(response, self.other_message.body)



============================================================
FILE: reports\tests\test_migration.py
============================================================
"""Existing event reports retain their identity and fields across the upgrade."""

from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.db import connection
from django.db.migrations.executor import MigrationExecutor
from django.test import TransactionTestCase
from django.utils import timezone

from events.models import Conversation, DirectMessage, Event, EventPost, EventReply
from reports.models import Report


class ReportKindMigrationTests(TransactionTestCase):
    def restore_latest(self):
        executor = MigrationExecutor(connection)
        executor.migrate(executor.loader.graph.leaf_nodes())

    def test_existing_reports_become_event_reports_without_losing_history(self):
        self.addCleanup(self.restore_latest)
        before = [("reports", "0001_initial")]
        after = [("reports", "0002_comment_and_chat_reports")]
        executor = MigrationExecutor(connection)
        executor.migrate(before)
        old_report = executor.loader.project_state(before).apps.get_model(
            "reports", "Report"
        )
        user = get_user_model().objects.create_user(
            "migration-reporter", "migration@example.com"
        )
        event = Event.objects.create(
            guid="report-migration", title="Original event", content_hash=""
        )
        existing = old_report.objects.create(
            reporter_id=user.pk,
            event_id=event.pk,
            event_title=event.title,
            reason="spam_scam",
            details="Original details",
        )
        deleted_source = old_report.objects.create(
            event_title="Deleted event",
            reason="other",
            details="Resolved history",
            resolved_at=timezone.now(),
        )
        executor = MigrationExecutor(connection)
        executor.migrate(after)
        migrated_report = executor.loader.project_state(after).apps.get_model(
            "reports", "Report"
        )
        upgraded = migrated_report.objects.get(pk=existing.pk)
        self.assertEqual(upgraded.kind, Report.Kind.EVENT)
        self.assertEqual(upgraded.event_id, event.pk)
        self.assertEqual(upgraded.reporter_id, user.pk)
        self.assertEqual(upgraded.event_title, existing.event_title)
        self.assertEqual(upgraded.reason, existing.reason)
        self.assertEqual(upgraded.details, existing.details)
        self.assertEqual(upgraded.created_at, existing.created_at)
        self.assertIsNone(upgraded.reported_user_id)
        self.assertIsNone(upgraded.context_message_id)
        historical = migrated_report.objects.get(pk=deleted_source.pk)
        self.assertEqual(historical.kind, Report.Kind.EVENT)
        self.assertEqual(historical.event_title, "Deleted event")
        self.assertEqual(historical.resolved_at, deleted_source.resolved_at)
        self.assertIsNone(historical.event_id)

    def test_profile_migration_preserves_existing_report_kinds_and_context(self):
        self.addCleanup(self.restore_latest)
        before = [("reports", "0002_comment_and_chat_reports")]
        after = [("reports", "0003_profile_reports")]
        executor = MigrationExecutor(connection)
        executor.migrate(before)
        old_report = executor.loader.project_state(before).apps.get_model(
            "reports", "Report"
        )
        User = get_user_model()
        reporter = User.objects.create_user(
            "migration-viewer", "migration-viewer@example.com"
        )
        subject = User.objects.create_user(
            "migration-subject", "migration-subject@example.com"
        )
        event = Event.objects.create(
            guid="profile-migration", title="Shared event", content_hash=""
        )
        post = EventPost.objects.create(event=event, author=subject, body="Comment")
        reply = EventReply.objects.create(post=post, author=subject, body="Reply")
        thread, _ = Conversation.get_or_create_for_users(reporter, subject)
        message = DirectMessage.objects.create(
            conversation=thread, sender=subject, body="Chat message"
        )
        for kind, fields in (
            ("event", {"event_id": event.pk, "event_title": event.title}),
            (
                "post",
                {
                    "event_id": event.pk,
                    "reported_post_id": post.pk,
                    "reported_user_id": subject.pk,
                },
            ),
            (
                "reply",
                {
                    "event_id": event.pk,
                    "reported_reply_id": reply.pk,
                    "reported_user_id": subject.pk,
                },
            ),
            (
                "user",
                {
                    "reported_user_id": subject.pk,
                    "conversation_id": thread.pk,
                    "context_message_id": message.pk,
                },
            ),
        ):
            old_report.objects.create(
                kind=kind,
                reporter_id=reporter.pk,
                reason="other",
                details="Existing concern",
                **fields
            )
        original = list(old_report.objects.order_by("pk").values())
        executor = MigrationExecutor(connection)
        executor.migrate(after)
        migrated_report = executor.loader.project_state(after).apps.get_model(
            "reports", "Report"
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            list(migrated_report.objects.order_by("pk").values()), original
        )
        profile_report = migrated_report.objects.create(
            kind=Report.Kind.PROFILE,
            reporter_id=reporter.pk,
            reported_user_id=subject.pk,
            reason=Report.Reason.OTHER,
        )
        self.assertEqual(profile_report.reported_user_id, subject.pk)
        self.assertIsNone(profile_report.conversation_id)
        self.assertEqual(migrated_report.objects.count(), 5)

    def test_resolution_attribution_migration_preserves_history(self):
        self.addCleanup(self.restore_latest)
        before = [("reports", "0003_profile_reports")]
        after = [("reports", "0004_report_resolved_by")]
        executor = MigrationExecutor(connection)
        executor.migrate(before)
        old_report = executor.loader.project_state(before).apps.get_model(
            "reports", "Report"
        )
        resolved_at = timezone.now()
        report = old_report.objects.create(
            event_title="Historical event",
            reason="other",
            details="Original history",
            resolved_at=resolved_at,
        )
        original = old_report.objects.values().get(pk=report.pk)
        MigrationExecutor(connection).migrate(after)
        migrated = Report.objects.get(pk=report.pk)
        self.assertIsNone(migrated.resolved_by_id)
        self.assertEqual(Report.objects.values(*original).get(pk=report.pk), original)



============================================================
FILE: reports\tests\test_profile_reporting.py
============================================================
"""Profile-content reporting is public-profile scoped and independent of chats."""

from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from threading import Barrier
from unittest.mock import patch

from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.core import mail
from django.db import IntegrityError, connections, transaction
from django.test import Client, TestCase, TransactionTestCase
from django.urls import reverse
from django.utils import timezone

from events.models import (
    ContactRequest,
    Conversation,
    DirectMessage,
    Event,
    Notification,
)
from reports.forms import ReportForm
from reports.models import Report


class ProfileReportTestData:
    @classmethod
    def create_data(cls):
        User = get_user_model()
        cls.viewer = User.objects.create_user(
            username="profile-viewer", email="viewer@example.com"
        )
        cls.target = User.objects.create_user(
            username="profile-subject",
            email="subject@example.com",
            display_name="Jordan Park",
            share_profile_with_attendees=True,
            bio="Public profile biography.",
        )
        cls.hidden = User.objects.create_user(
            username="hidden-profile", email="hidden@example.com"
        )
        cls.report_url = reverse("profile_report", args=[cls.target.username])
        cls.profile_url = reverse("public_profile", args=[cls.target.username])

    def submit(self, **fields):
        data = {
            "reason": Report.Reason.INAPPROPRIATE_CONTENT,
            "details": "Please review the bio.",
        }
        data.update(fields)
        return self.client.post(self.report_url, data)

    def approved_chat(self):
        event = Event.objects.create(
            guid="profile-report-chat", title="Shared event", content_hash=""
        )
        ContactRequest.objects.create(
            event=event,
            sender=self.viewer,
            recipient=self.target,
            status=ContactRequest.Status.ACCEPTED,
        )
        thread, _ = Conversation.get_or_create_for_users(self.viewer, self.target)
        message = DirectMessage.objects.create(
            conversation=thread, sender=self.target, body="Private chat context."
        )
        return thread, message


class ProfileReportingTests(ProfileReportTestData, TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpTestData(cls):
        cls.create_data()

    def setUp(self):
        self.client.force_login(self.viewer)

    def test_profile_menu_does_not_require_messaging_access(self):
        response = self.client.get(self.profile_url)
        self.assertFalse(response.context["can_message"])
        self.assertContains(response, 'aria-label="Profile actions"')
        self.assertContains(response, f'href="{self.report_url}"')
        self.assertContains(response, "Report profile")
        self.assertContains(response, "/static/reports/menus.css")
        self.assertContains(response, "/static/reports/menus.js")
        self.assertNotContains(response, "Send Message")
        self.assertFalse(ContactRequest.objects.exists())
        self.assertFalse(Conversation.objects.exists())

        self.assertEqual(self.client.get(self.report_url).status_code, 200)
        self.assertRedirects(self.submit(), self.profile_url)

    def test_messaging_and_reporting_actions_can_both_be_shown(self):
        self.approved_chat()
        response = self.client.get(self.profile_url)
        self.assertContains(response, "Send Message")
        self.assertContains(response, f'href="{self.report_url}"')

    def test_own_profile_keeps_edit_action_and_hides_report_menu(self):
        response = self.client.get(
            reverse("public_profile", args=[self.viewer.username])
        )
        self.assertContains(response, "Edit profile")
        self.assertNotContains(response, "Report profile")
        self.assertNotContains(response, 'aria-label="Profile actions"')

    def test_hidden_profile_does_not_offer_reporting_or_reveal_private_fields(self):
        response = self.client.get(
            reverse("public_profile", args=[self.hidden.username])
        )
        self.assertContains(response, "Profile Unavailable")
        self.assertNotContains(response, "Report profile")
        self.assertNotContains(
            response, reverse("profile_report", args=[self.hidden.username])
        )

    def test_shared_form_identifies_profile_and_has_cancel_link(self):
        response = self.client.get(self.report_url)
        self.assertTemplateUsed(response, "reports/report_form.html")
        self.assertContains(response, "Report profile")
        self.assertContains(response, self.target.display_name)
        self.assertContains(response, f"@{self.target.username}")
        self.assertContains(response, f'<a href="{self.profile_url}">Cancel</a>')
        self.assertFalse(response.context["form"].fields["details"].required)
        for value, label in Report.Reason.choices:
            self.assertContains(response, f'<option value="{value}">{label}</option>')
        self.assertFalse(Report.objects.exists())

    def test_submission_records_profile_reference_without_changing_user_or_notifying(
        self,
    ):
        before = get_user_model().objects.values().get(pk=self.target.pk)
        response = self.client.post(
            self.report_url,
            {
                "reason": Report.Reason.INAPPROPRIATE_CONTENT,
                "details": "  Please review.\n  ",
            },
            follow=True,
        )
        self.assertRedirects(response, self.profile_url)
        self.assertContains(response, "Your report has been submitted.")
        self.assertContains(response, 'class="profile-alerts" role="status"')
        report = Report.objects.get()
        self.assertEqual(report.kind, Report.Kind.PROFILE)
        self.assertEqual(report.reporter, self.viewer)
        self.assertEqual(report.reported_user, self.target)
        self.assertEqual(report.details, "Please review.")
        self.assertEqual(report.event_title, "")
        for field in (
            "event_id",
            "reported_post_id",
            "reported_reply_id",
            "conversation_id",
            "context_message_id",
            "resolved_at",
        ):
            self.assertIsNone(getattr(report, field))
        self.assertIsNotNone(report.created_at)
        self.assertEqual(
            get_user_model().objects.values().get(pk=self.target.pk), before
        )
        self.assertFalse(Notification.objects.exists())
        self.assertEqual(mail.outbox, [])

    def test_profile_report_follows_live_content_without_snapshotting_text_or_photo(
        self,
    ):
        self.target.profile_photo = "profile_photos/original.jpg"
        self.target.save(update_fields=["profile_photo"])
        self.submit()
        report = Report.objects.get()
        saved = Report.objects.values().get()
        self.assertNotIn(self.target.bio, saved.values())
        self.assertNotIn(self.target.profile_photo.name, saved.values())
        self.assertNotIn(self.target.display_name, saved.values())

        self.target.bio = "Updated biography."
        self.target.profile_photo = "profile_photos/replacement.jpg"
        self.target.save(update_fields=["bio", "profile_photo"])
        report.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(report.reported_user.bio, "Updated biography.")
        self.assertEqual(
            report.reported_user.profile_photo.name, "profile_photos/replacement.jpg"
        )

    def test_login_is_required_with_return_to_report_form(self):
        self.client.logout()
        for response in (
            self.client.get(self.report_url),
            self.submit(),
        ):
            self.assertRedirects(
                response, reverse("login") + "?next=" + self.report_url
            )
        self.assertFalse(Report.objects.exists())

    def test_self_hidden_and_missing_targets_are_rejected_on_get_and_post(self):
        for username in (self.viewer.username, self.hidden.username, "missing-member"):
            with self.subTest(username=username):
                url = reverse("profile_report", args=[username])
                self.assertEqual(self.client.get(url).status_code, 404)
                self.assertEqual(
                    self.client.post(url, {"reason": Report.Reason.OTHER}).status_code,
                    404,
                )
        self.assertFalse(Report.objects.exists())

    def test_sharing_disabled_while_form_is_open_prevents_submission(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get(self.report_url).status_code, 200)
        self.target.share_profile_with_attendees = False
        self.target.save(update_fields=["share_profile_with_attendees"])
        self.assertEqual(self.submit().status_code, 404)
        self.assertFalse(Report.objects.exists())

    def test_invalid_reason_or_overlong_details_preserve_input_without_saving(self):
        for data, error_field in (
            ({"details": "Keep my explanation."}, "reason"),
            ({"reason": "invalid", "details": "Keep my explanation."}, "reason"),
            ({"reason": Report.Reason.OTHER, "details": "x" * 5001}, "details"),
        ):
            with self.subTest(error_field=error_field):
                response = self.client.post(self.report_url, data)
                self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
                self.assertIn(error_field, response.context["form"].errors)
                self.assertContains(response, data["details"])
                self.assertFalse(Report.objects.exists())

    def test_other_reason_allows_omitted_or_empty_details(self):
        self.assertRedirects(
            self.client.post(self.report_url, {"reason": Report.Reason.OTHER}),
            self.profile_url,
        )
        self.assertEqual(Report.objects.get().details, "")

    def test_profile_identity_and_invalid_details_are_escaped(self):
        self.target.display_name = '<script>alert("name")</script>'
        self.target.save(update_fields=["display_name"])
        details = '<script>alert("details")</script>'
        response = self.client.post(self.report_url, {"details": details})
        self.assertContains(response, "&lt;script&gt;")
        self.assertNotContains(response, self.target.display_name)
        self.assertNotContains(response, details)

    def test_submitted_identity_and_chat_context_are_ignored(self):
        thread, message = self.approved_chat()
        response = self.submit(
            kind=Report.Kind.USER,
            reporter=self.target.pk,
            reported_user=self.hidden.pk,
            conversation=thread.pk,
            context_message=message.pk,
            event=99999,
            reported_post=99999,
            reported_reply=99999,
            event_title="Forged context",
            resolved_at="2000-01-01T00:00:00Z",
        )
        self.assertRedirects(response, self.profile_url)
        report = Report.objects.get()
        self.assertEqual(report.kind, Report.Kind.PROFILE)
        self.assertEqual(report.reporter, self.viewer)
        self.assertEqual(report.reported_user, self.target)
        self.assertEqual(report.event_title, "")
        for field in (
            "event_id",
            "reported_post_id",
            "reported_reply_id",
            "conversation_id",
            "context_message_id",
            "resolved_at",
        ):
            self.assertIsNone(getattr(report, field))
        message.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertIsNone(message.read_at)

    def test_profile_and_chat_reports_are_independent_in_both_submission_orders(self):
        thread, anchor = self.approved_chat()
        chat_url = reverse("conversation_report", args=[thread.pk])
        for urls in ((self.report_url, chat_url), (chat_url, self.report_url)):
            with self.subTest(urls=urls):
                for url in urls:
                    self.assertEqual(
                        self.client.post(
                            url, {"reason": Report.Reason.OTHER}
                        ).status_code,
                        302,
                    )
                self.assertEqual(Report.objects.count(), 2)
                profile_report = Report.objects.get(kind=Report.Kind.PROFILE)
                chat_report = Report.objects.get(kind=Report.Kind.USER)
                self.assertEqual(profile_report.reported_user, self.target)
                self.assertIsNone(profile_report.conversation_id)
                self.assertIsNone(profile_report.context_message_id)
                self.assertEqual(chat_report.context_message, anchor)
                self.assertEqual(chat_report.conversation, thread)
                Report.objects.all().delete()

    def test_duplicate_reports_preserve_original_and_show_feedback(self):
        self.submit()
        original = Report.objects.values().get()
        for response in (
            self.client.get(self.report_url, follow=True),
            self.client.post(
                self.report_url,
                {"reason": Report.Reason.OTHER, "details": "Replacement"},
                follow=True,
            ),
        ):
            self.assertRedirects(response, self.profile_url)
            self.assertContains(response, "You have already reported this profile.")
            self.assertEqual(Report.objects.values().get(), original)

    def test_resolving_profile_report_allows_another_without_resolving_chat_report(
        self,
    ):
        thread, _ = self.approved_chat()
        self.client.post(
            reverse("conversation_report", args=[thread.pk]),
            {"reason": Report.Reason.OTHER},
        )
        self.submit()
        original = Report.objects.get(kind=Report.Kind.PROFILE)
        original.resolved_at = timezone.now()
        original.save(update_fields=["resolved_at"])
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get(self.report_url).status_code, 200)
        self.assertRedirects(
            self.submit(details="New profile concern"), self.profile_url
        )
        self.assertEqual(Report.objects.filter(kind=Report.Kind.PROFILE).count(), 2)
        self.assertEqual(
            Report.objects.get(kind=Report.Kind.PROFILE, resolved_at=None).details,
            "New profile concern",
        )
        self.assertIsNone(Report.objects.get(kind=Report.Kind.USER).resolved_at)
        original.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(original.details, "Please review the bio.")

    def test_database_enforces_profile_report_limit(self):
        self.submit()
        with self.assertRaises(IntegrityError), transaction.atomic():
            Report.objects.create(
                kind=Report.Kind.PROFILE,
                reporter=self.viewer,
                reported_user=self.target,
                reason=Report.Reason.OTHER,
            )
        self.assertEqual(Report.objects.count(), 1)

    def test_other_reporters_and_other_shared_profiles_are_independent(self):
        self.submit()
        self.client.force_login(self.hidden)
        self.assertRedirects(self.submit(), self.profile_url)
        self.assertEqual(Report.objects.count(), 2)

        self.client.force_login(self.viewer)
        self.hidden.share_profile_with_attendees = True
        self.hidden.save(update_fields=["share_profile_with_attendees"])
        other_url = reverse("profile_report", args=[self.hidden.username])
        self.assertEqual(
            self.client.post(other_url, {"reason": Report.Reason.OTHER}).status_code,
            302,
        )
        self.assertEqual(Report.objects.filter(reporter=self.viewer).count(), 2)

    def test_deleting_reported_account_clears_link_and_keeps_report(self):
        self.submit()
        self.target.delete()
        report = Report.objects.get()
        self.assertIsNone(report.reported_user_id)
        self.assertEqual(report.reporter, self.viewer)
        self.assertEqual(report.kind, Report.Kind.PROFILE)
        self.assertEqual(report.details, "Please review the bio.")
        self.assertIsNotNone(report.created_at)

    def test_deleting_reporter_keeps_report_and_target(self):
        self.submit()
        self.viewer.delete()
        report = Report.objects.get()
        self.assertIsNone(report.reporter_id)
        self.assertEqual(report.reported_user, self.target)

    def test_csrf_required_and_valid_token_allows_submission(self):
        client = Client(enforce_csrf_checks=True)
        client.force_login(self.viewer)
        data = {"reason": Report.Reason.OTHER}
        self.assertEqual(client.post(self.report_url, data).status_code, 403)
        self.assertFalse(Report.objects.exists())
        response = client.get(self.report_url)
        self.assertContains(response, 'name="csrfmiddlewaretoken"')
        data["csrfmiddlewaretoken"] = client.cookies["csrftoken"].value
        self.assertRedirects(client.post(self.report_url, data), self.profile_url)

    def test_unsupported_methods_are_rejected(self):
        for method in ("put", "patch", "delete", "head", "options"):
            self.assertEqual(
                getattr(self.client, method)(self.report_url).status_code, 405
            )
        self.assertFalse(Report.objects.exists())


class ConcurrentProfileReportTests(ProfileReportTestData, TransactionTestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.create_data()

    def test_concurrent_profile_reports_keep_one_original_record(self):
        clients = [Client(), Client()]
        for client in clients:
            client.force_login(self.viewer)
        ready = Barrier(2, timeout=15)
        original_is_valid = ReportForm.is_valid

        def validate_together(form):
            valid = original_is_valid(form)
            ready.wait()
            return valid

        def submit(item):
            client, details = item
            try:
                return client.post(
                    self.report_url, {"reason": Report.Reason.OTHER, "details": details}
                )
            finally:
                connections.close_all()

        with patch.object(ReportForm, "is_valid", validate_together):
            with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
                responses = list(pool.map(submit, zip(clients, ("First", "Second"))))
        for response in responses:
            self.assertRedirects(
                response, self.profile_url, fetch_redirect_response=False
            )
        self.assertIn(Report.objects.get().details, ("First", "Second"))
        self.assertCountEqual(
            [
                str(message)
                for response in responses
                for message in response.wsgi_request._messages
            ],
            [
                "Your report has been submitted.",
                "You have already reported this profile.",
            ],
        )



============================================================
FILE: reports\tests\test_reporting.py
